Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:00.220Z
1	"""Run/conversation persistence — a thin client over the single-writer SQLite `Store`.
2	
3	`RunStore` keeps the small verb set `api.py`, `events.py` and `loop.py` already use
4	(`get / create / append_events / update / list_runs`) so the migration off the Modal Dict
5	`faultline-runs` was a backend swap, not a rewrite (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4.3). It also
6	passes the identity/conversation verbs straight through.
7	
8	Two backends, same surface:
9	
10	  * `SqliteStore`         — in-process. Used by every test (temp dir or `:memory:`) and by a local
11	                            `uvicorn harness.api:app` run.
12	  * `ModalStoreClient`    — `Store().<method>.remote(...)` on the `@app.cls(max_containers=1)`
13	                            defined in `modal_app.py`. Used in the deployed harness, where the
14	                            database must live in exactly one process.
15	
16	Nothing else may open the database file: single writer is what makes SQLite safe here.
17	"""
18	
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import threading
22	from typing import Any, Callable
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
25	
26	from . import config
27	from .sqlite_store import (  # re-exported: callers import these from harness.store
28	    ANON_USER_ID,
29	    TERMINAL_STATUSES,
30	    SqliteStore,
31	    new_id,
32	    now_iso,
33	    valid_user_id,
34	)
35	
36	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
37	
38	__all__ = [
39	    "ANON_USER_ID",
40	    "TERMINAL_STATUSES",
41	    "ModalStoreClient",
42	    "RunStore",
43	    "SqliteStore",
44	    "memory_store",
45	    "new_id",
46	    "now_iso",
47	    "valid_user_id",
48	]
49	
50	# Every verb the Store exposes; `ModalStoreClient` forwards each one to the Modal class.
51	STORE_METHODS = (
52	    "upsert_user",
53	    "list_conversations",
54	    "create_conversation",
55	    "get_conversation",
56	    "update_conversation",
57	    "delete_conversation",
58	    "create_run",
59	    "append_events",
60	    "finish_run",
61	    "get_run",
62	    "list_runs",
63	    "events_after",
64	    "checkpoint",
65	    "health",
66	)
67	
68	
69	def memory_store() -> SqliteStore:
70	    """A fresh private database (tests, and any caller that wants a throwaway store)."""
71	    return SqliteStore(":memory:").open()
72	
73	
74	class ModalStoreClient:
75	    """Forwards every Store verb to the single-container Modal class.
76	
77	    `cls_handle` is the decorated class from `modal_app` when we are running inside the app (no
78	    lookup, no dependency on what is currently deployed); otherwise the class is resolved by name.
79	    """
80	
81	    def __init__(self, cls_handle: Any = None, *, app_name: str = config.APP_NAME,
82	                 class_name: str = "Store"):
83	        self._cls_handle = cls_handle
84	        self._app_name = app_name
85	        self._class_name = class_name
86	        self._instance: Any = None
87	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
88	
89	    def _obj(self) -> Any:
90	        with self._lock:
91	            if self._instance is None:
92	                handle = self._cls_handle
93	                if handle is None:
94	                    import modal
95	
96	                    handle = modal.Cls.from_name(
97	                        self._app_name, self._class_name,
98	                        environment_name=config.modal_environment(),
99	                    )
100	                self._instance = handle()
101	            return self._instance
102	
103	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Callable[..., Any]:
104	        if name not in STORE_METHODS:
105	            raise AttributeError(name)
106	
107	        def call(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
108	            return getattr(self._obj(), name).remote(*args, **kwargs)
109	
110	        return call
111	
112	
113	class RunStore:
114	    """The verbs the rest of the harness calls. Backend-agnostic; never opens a file itself."""
115	
116	    def __init__(self, backend: Any = None):
117	        self._backend = backend
118	
119	    @property
120	    def backend(self) -> Any:
121	        if self._backend is None:
122	            self._backend = ModalStoreClient()
123	        return self._backend
124	
125	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
126	    def get(self, run_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
127	        record = self.backend.get_run(run_id)
128	        return record if isinstance(record, dict) else None
129	
130	    def create(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
131	        return self.backend.create_run(dict(record))
132	
133	    def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
134	        """Idempotent on (run_id, seq); returns {run_id, appended, count} — NOT the whole record."""
135	        return self.backend.append_events(run_id, list(events or []))
136	
137	    def update(self, run_id: str, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
138	        """Merge scalar fields (status, finished_at, evaluation, usage, …) into the run row."""
139	        return self.backend.finish_run(run_id, dict(fields))
140	
141	    def finish_run(self, run_id: str, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
142	        return self.update(run_id, **fields)
143	
144	    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 50, user_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
145	        return list(self.backend.list_runs(limit, user_id) or [])
146	
147	    def events_after(self, run_id: str, after: int = -1, limit: int = 2000) -> dict[str, Any]:
148	        return self.backend.events_after(run_id, after, limit)
149	
150	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ identity + conversations
151	    def upsert_user(self, user_id: str, user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
152	        return self.backend.upsert_user(user_id, user_agent)
153	
154	    def list_conversations(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
155	        return list(self.backend.list_conversations(user_id, limit) or [])
156	
157	    def create_conversation(self, user_id: str, scenario_id: str, title: str | None = None,
158	                            user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
159	        return self.backend.create_conversation(user_id, scenario_id, title, user_agent)
160	
161	    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
162	        return self.backend.get_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
163	
164	    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None, *,
165	                            title: str | None = None, archived: bool | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
166	        return self.backend.update_conversation(conversation_id, user_id, title=title, archived=archived)
167	
168	    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
169	        return self.backend.delete_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
170	
171	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ ops
172	    def checkpoint(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
173	        return self.backend.checkpoint(force)
174	
175	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
176	        return self.backend.health()
177
Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:00.221Z
1	-- Faultline harness store, migration 1 (ARCHITECTURE.md §4.4).
2	-- All timestamps ISO-8601 UTC ("…Z"). JSON columns hold pydantic dumps from faultline_common.schemas.
3	-- Applied by harness/sqlite_store.py inside one transaction; `PRAGMA user_version` gates it.
4	--
5	-- Deviation from the doc, deliberate: `runs.status` CHECK also allows 'unevaluated' and
6	-- 'interrupted', which schemas.RunStatus gained with PLAN.md §2.11. The doc's five-value CHECK
7	-- would reject a run that ends interrupted and lose the whole transcript.
8	
9	CREATE TABLE users (
10	  id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                     -- u_<uuid4>, minted by the browser (§6)
11	  created_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
12	  last_seen_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
13	  user_agent    TEXT
14	);
15	
16	CREATE TABLE conversations (
17	  id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                     -- c_…
18	  user_id       TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
19	  scenario_id   TEXT NOT NULL,
20	  title         TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- defaults to the scenario title; PATCHable
21	  created_at    TEXT NOT NULL,
22	  updated_at    TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- bumped on every run event (drives list order)
23	  archived_at   TEXT                                  -- soft delete
24	);
25	CREATE INDEX ix_conversations_user ON conversations(user_id, updated_at DESC);
26	
27	CREATE TABLE runs (
28	  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                   -- r_…
29	  conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES conversations(id),
30	  user_id         TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), -- denormalised for ownership checks
31	  scenario_id     TEXT NOT NULL,
32	  model           TEXT NOT NULL,
33	  seed            INTEGER,
34	  max_steps       INTEGER NOT NULL,
35	  task_prompt     TEXT NOT NULL,                      -- what the user turn actually contained
36	  episode_id      TEXT,                               -- sandbox-env episode (set after reset)
37	  status          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN
38	                    ('queued','running','ok','error','truncated','unevaluated','interrupted')),
39	  score           REAL,                               -- 0..100 from evaluate
40	  evaluation_json TEXT,                               -- EvaluateResponse (checks, tests, ledger)
41	  input_tokens    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
42	  output_tokens   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
43	  error           TEXT,
44	  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
45	  started_at      TEXT,
46	  finished_at     TEXT,
47	  steps           INTEGER,                            -- final step count (RunRecord.steps)
48	  summary         TEXT,                               -- the agent's submit summary
49	  extra_json      TEXT                                -- additive RunRecord fields (usage detail, …)
50	);
51	CREATE INDEX ix_runs_conversation ON runs(conversation_id, created_at);
52	CREATE INDEX ix_runs_user ON runs(user_id, created_at DESC);
53	CREATE INDEX ix_runs_live ON runs(status) WHERE status IN ('queued','running');
54	
55	-- Append-only event log: the source of truth for replay, SSE resume and evidence export.
56	CREATE TABLE events (
57	  run_id  TEXT    NOT NULL REFERENCES runs(id),
58	  seq     INTEGER NOT NULL,                           -- 0-based; == Event.id == SSE id / Last-Event-ID
59	  ts      TEXT    NOT NULL,
60	  type    TEXT    NOT NULL,                           -- EventType (run.started … llm.call, turn.thinking)
61	  step    INTEGER,
62	  data    TEXT    NOT NULL,                           -- JSON: Event.data
63	  PRIMARY KEY (run_id, seq)
64	) WITHOUT ROWID;
65	
66	-- Transcript projection in Anthropic message shape: what the UI renders and what the model saw.
67	CREATE TABLE messages (
68	  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                   -- m_…
69	  conversation_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES conversations(id),
70	  run_id          TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES runs(id),
71	  seq             INTEGER NOT NULL,                   -- order within the conversation
72	  role            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('user','assistant')),
73	  step            INTEGER,                            -- harness step (NULL for the task prompt)
74	  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
75	  UNIQUE (conversation_id, seq)
76	);
77	CREATE INDEX ix_messages_run ON messages(run_id, seq);
78	
79	CREATE TABLE blocks (
80	  id           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                      -- b_…
81	  message_id   TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES messages(id),
82	  seq          INTEGER NOT NULL,                      -- order within the message
83	  type         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('text','thinking','tool_use','tool_result')),
84	  text         TEXT,                                  -- text / thinking / tool_result output
85	  tool_name    TEXT,                                  -- tool_use
86	  tool_use_id  TEXT,                                  -- tool_use and tool_result (join key)
87	  input_json   TEXT,                                  -- tool_use input
88	  is_error     INTEGER,                               -- tool_result
89	  exit_code    INTEGER,                               -- tool_result (run_command)
90	  duration_ms  INTEGER,                               -- tool_result
91	  fault_json   TEXT,                                  -- FaultFired echoed on the result, if any
92	  truncated    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
93	  UNIQUE (message_id, seq)
94	);
95	CREATE INDEX ix_blocks_tool_use ON blocks(tool_use_id);
96	
97	-- One row per messages.create: the "LLM turn". Retries on 429/5xx get attempt > 1.
98	CREATE TABLE llm_calls (
99	  id                 TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                -- l_…
100	  run_id             TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES runs(id),
101	  step               INTEGER NOT NULL,
102	  attempt            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
103	  model              TEXT NOT NULL,
104	  request_id         TEXT,                            -- anthropic `request-id` response header
105	  stop_reason        TEXT,                            -- end_turn | tool_use | max_tokens | …
106	  input_tokens       INTEGER,
107	  output_tokens      INTEGER,
108	  cache_read_tokens  INTEGER,
109	  cache_write_tokens INTEGER,
110	  started_at         TEXT NOT NULL,
111	  duration_ms        INTEGER,
112	  error              TEXT,
113	  UNIQUE (run_id, step, attempt)
114	);

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:10.196Z
1	"""SQLite engine behind the harness `Store` (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4).
2	
3	This module is plain Python: no Modal, no network, no FastAPI. `modal_app.Store` is a thin
4	`@app.cls(max_containers=1)` wrapper that owns one instance of `SqliteStore` per container and
5	exposes each method as a `@modal.method`, so everything here is unit-testable on a temp dir.
6	
7	Shape (ARCHITECTURE §4.3):
8	  * the hot database lives on the container's own disk (`/tmp/faultline.sqlite3`) with ordinary
9	    SQLite settings — WAL, synchronous=NORMAL, foreign_keys=ON — so none of the FUSE questions a
10	    Modal Volume raises (locking, mmap, a background commit capturing a half-written file) apply;
11	  * a checkpoint is `VACUUM INTO <snapshot>.tmp` → `os.replace` → `volume.commit()`. Only complete
12	    files are ever renamed into place, so a snapshot is always openable;
13	  * `@modal.enter` restores the snapshot into /tmp and runs migrations (`PRAGMA user_version` gates
14	    `migrations/000N_*.sql`);
15	  * every write marks the DB dirty; `checkpoint()` is called after each `run.finished`, every
16	    `checkpoint_interval_s` while dirty (background thread), and from `@modal.exit`.
17	
18	`events` is the source of truth; `messages` / `blocks` / `llm_calls` are a projection written in the
19	SAME transaction as the event insert (§4.5), and `append_events` is idempotent on `(run_id, seq)` —
20	`run_episode` re-sends its whole in-memory event list at `run.finished`, which back-fills anything a
21	Store restart lost, and the projection is skipped for rows that were already there.
22	"""
23	
24	from __future__ import annotations
25	
26	import contextlib
27	import json
28	import os
29	import pathlib
30	import re
31	import secrets
32	import shutil
33	import sqlite3
34	import threading
35	import time
36	from datetime import datetime, timezone
37	from typing import Any, Callable, Iterator
38	
39	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
40	
41	from . import config
42	
43	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
44	
45	# `Image.add_local_python_source` ships only the package's .py files — verified on Modal 1.5.5: the
46	# container had harness/*.py and NO harness/migrations/. So modal_app.py copies the directory in
47	# explicitly (outside the package, where the python-source mount cannot shadow it) and points here.
48	MIGRATIONS_DIR = pathlib.Path(
49	    os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_MIGRATIONS_DIR") or (pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "migrations")
50	)
51	
52	# Identity is scoping, not authentication (PLAN.md §1 non-goal / §2.9.1): `u_` + a uuid4.
53	USER_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^u_[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$")
54	# Requests that carry no X-Faultline-User (the CLI, smoke scripts) are still persisted, under one
55	# well-known anonymous id, so a run always has a conversation and the FKs hold.
56	ANON_USER_ID = "u_00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000"
57	
58	TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
59	
60	# Columns of `runs` that finish_run()/create_run() may set directly.
61	RUN_COLUMNS = {
62	    "conversation_id", "user_id", "scenario_id", "model", "seed", "max_steps", "task_prompt",
63	    "episode_id", "status", "score", "error", "created_at", "started_at", "finished_at",
64	    "steps", "summary", "input_tokens", "output_tokens",
65	}
66	# Anything else a caller passes (usage detail, error_class, interruptions, worker_generation …)
67	# is preserved verbatim in `extra_json` and merged back into the record on read, so a RunRecord
68	# never loses a field this store does not know about.
69	_RESERVED = RUN_COLUMNS | {"run_id", "id", "evaluation", "events", "usage"}
70	
71	
72	def now_iso() -> str:
73	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
74	
75	
76	def new_id(prefix: str) -> str:
77	    """`<prefix>_<hex ms timestamp><12 hex random>` — sortable, no extra dependency (§4.3)."""
78	    return f"{prefix}_{int(time.time() * 1000):x}{secrets.token_hex(6)}"
79	
80	
81	def valid_user_id(value: str | None) -> bool:
82	    return bool(value) and bool(USER_ID_RE.match(value or ""))
83	
84	
85	def _json(value: Any) -> str | None:
86	    return None if value is None else json.dumps(value, default=str)
87	
88	
89	def _unjson(value: Any) -> Any:
90	    if not value:
91	        return None
92	    try:
93	        return json.loads(value)
94	    except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover - only a corrupted row gets here
95	        return None
96	
97	
98	class SqliteStore:
99	    """The single writer. One connection, one lock; every op is sub-millisecond on local disk."""
100	
101	    def __init__(
102	        self,
103	        db_path: str | os.PathLike[str],
104	        *,
105	        snapshot_path: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
106	        commit: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
107	        reload: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
108	        checkpoint_interval_s: float = 15.0,
109	    ):
110	        self.db_path = str(db_path)
111	        self.snapshot_path = str(snapshot_path) if snapshot_path else None
112	        self._commit_volume = commit
113	        self._reload_volume = reload
114	        self.checkpoint_interval_s = checkpoint_interval_s
115	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
116	        self._ckpt_lock = threading.Lock()
117	        self._conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None
118	        self._dirty = False
119	        self._last_checkpoint_at: str | None = None
120	        self._last_checkpoint_error: str | None = None
121	        self._restored_from_snapshot = False
122	        self._stop = threading.Event()
123	        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
124	
125	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ lifecycle
126	    def open(self) -> "SqliteStore":
127	        with self._lock:
128	            if self._conn is not None:
129	                return self
130	            if self.db_path not in (":memory:", ""):
131	                pathlib.Path(self.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
132	            self._restore()
133	            self._conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path, check_same_thread=False, isolation_level=None)
134	            self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
135	            if self.db_path not in (":memory:", ""):
136	                self._conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
137	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
138	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
139	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
140	            applied = self._migrate()
141	            self._assert_schema()
142	            log.info(
143	                "store.open",
144	                "sqlite ready",
145	                db=self.db_path,
146	                snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
147	                restored=self._restored_from_snapshot,
148	                user_version=self.user_version(),
149	                migrations_applied=applied or None,
150	            )
151	        return self
152	
153	    def _restore(self) -> None:
154	        """Copy the Volume snapshot onto local disk, unless a hot DB is already there."""
155	        if not self.snapshot_path or self.db_path in (":memory:", ""):
156	            return
157	        if os.path.exists(self.db_path):
158	            return
159	        if self._reload_volume is not None:
160	            try:
161	                self._reload_volume()
162	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a fresh Volume has nothing to reload
163	                log.warn("store.reload_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
164	        if not os.path.exists(self.snapshot_path):
165	            log.info("store.restore", "no snapshot on the volume; starting empty",
166	                     snapshot=self.snapshot_path)
167	            return
168	        pathlib.Path(self.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
169	        shutil.copyfile(self.snapshot_path, self.db_path)
170	        self._restored_from_snapshot = True
171	        log.info("store.restore", "snapshot copied to local disk", snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
172	                 db=self.db_path, bytes=os.path.getsize(self.db_path))
173	
174	    def _migrate(self) -> list[str]:
175	        version = self.user_version()
176	        applied: list[str] = []
177	        for path in sorted(MIGRATIONS_DIR.glob("[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.sql")):
178	            n = int(path.name.split("_", 1)[0])
179	            if n <= version:
180	                continue
181	            assert self._conn is not None
182	            self._conn.executescript(path.read_text())
183	            self._conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {n}")
184	            applied.append(path.name)
185	        return applied
186	
187	    def _assert_schema(self) -> None:
188	        """Fail at @modal.enter, not on the first request.
189	
190	        A missing migrations directory used to leave an EMPTY database that answered every call with
191	        `no such table` 500s; crashing here makes that a container-start failure instead.
192	        """
193	        assert self._conn is not None
194	        tables = {r[0] for r in self._conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
195	        missing = {"users", "conversations", "runs", "events", "messages", "blocks", "llm_calls"} - tables
196	        if missing:
197	            raise RuntimeError(
198	                f"store schema is incomplete: missing {sorted(missing)}; "
199	                f"migrations dir {MIGRATIONS_DIR} contains "
200	                f"{sorted(p.name for p in MIGRATIONS_DIR.glob('*')) if MIGRATIONS_DIR.is_dir() else 'NOTHING (dir absent)'}"
201	            )
202	
203	    def user_version(self) -> int:
204	        assert self._conn is not None
205	        return int(self._conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
206	
207	    def close(self) -> None:
208	        self.stop_autocheckpoint()
209	        with self._lock:
210	            if self._conn is not None:
211	                self._conn.close()
212	                self._conn = None
213	
214	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ plumbing
215	    @property
216	    def conn(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
217	        if self._conn is None:
218	            self.open()
219	        assert self._conn is not None
220	        return self._conn
221	
222	    @contextlib.contextmanager
223	    def _tx(self) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Cursor]:
224	        with self._lock:
225	            conn = self.conn
226	            cur = conn.cursor()
227	            cur.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
228	            try:
229	                yield cur
230	                conn.commit()
231	                self._dirty = True
232	            except BaseException:
233	                conn.rollback()
234	                raise
235	            finally:
236	                cur.close()
237	
238	    def _query(self, sql: str, params: tuple[Any, ...] = ()) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:
239	        with self._lock:
240	            return list(self.conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall())
241	
242	    def _one(self, sql: str, params: tuple[Any, ...] = ()) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
243	        rows = self._query(sql, params)
244	        return rows[0] if rows else None
245	
246	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ users
247	    def upsert_user(self, user_id: str, user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
248	        ts = now_iso()
249	        with self._tx() as cur:
250	            cur.execute(
251	                "INSERT INTO users (id, created_at, last_seen_at, user_agent) VALUES (?,?,?,?) "
252	                "ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET last_seen_at=excluded.last_seen_at, "
253	                "user_agent=COALESCE(excluded.user_agent, users.user_agent)",
254	                (user_id, ts, ts, user_agent),
255	            )
256	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (user_id,))
257	        n = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM conversations WHERE user_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL",
258	                      (user_id,))
259	        assert row is not None
260	        return {**dict(row), "conversations": int(n["n"] if n else 0)}
261	
262	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ conversations
263	    def create_conversation(self, user_id: str, scenario_id: str, title: str | None = None,
264	                            user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
265	        self.upsert_user(user_id, user_agent)
266	        cid = new_id("c")
267	        ts = now_iso()
268	        with self._tx() as cur:
269	            cur.execute(
270	                "INSERT INTO conversations (id, user_id, scenario_id, title, created_at, updated_at) "
271	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
272	                (cid, user_id, scenario_id, (title or scenario_id).strip()[:200] or scenario_id, ts, ts),
273	            )
274	        log.info("conversation.created", "conversation created", conversation_id=cid,
275	                 user_id=user_id, scenario_id=scenario_id)
276	        return self._conversation(cid) or {}
277	
278	    def _conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
279	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE id=?", (conversation_id,))
280	        return dict(row) if row else None
281	
282	    def _last_run(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
283	        row = self._one(
284	            "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE conversation_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1",
285	            (conversation_id,),
286	        )
287	        return self._run_summary(row) if row else None
288	
289	    @staticmethod
290	    def _run_summary(row: sqlite3.Row, n_events: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
291	        """RunSummary (schemas.py keys on `id`; ARCHITECTURE §5.1 and the CLI key on `run_id`)."""
292	        out = {
293	            "id": row["id"],
294	            "run_id": row["id"],
295	            "status": row["status"],
296	            "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
297	            "model": row["model"],
298	            "score": row["score"],
299	            "created_at": row["created_at"],
300	            "finished_at": row["finished_at"],
301	        }
302	        if n_events is not None:
303	            out["events"] = n_events
304	        return out
305	
306	    def list_conversations(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
307	        rows = self._query(
308	            "SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE user_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL "
309	            "ORDER BY updated_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT ?",
310	            (user_id, int(limit)),
311	        )
312	        return [
313	            {
314	                "id": r["id"],
315	                "title": r["title"],
316	                "scenario_id": r["scenario_id"],
317	                "created_at": r["created_at"],
318	                "updated_at": r["updated_at"],
319	                "last_run": self._last_run(r["id"]),
320	            }
321	            for r in rows
322	        ]
323	
324	    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
325	        conv = self._conversation(conversation_id)
326	        if conv is None or (user_id is not None and conv["user_id"] != user_id):
327	            return None
328	        runs = [
329	            self._run_summary(r, n_events=int(r["n_events"]))
330	            for r in self._query(
331	                "SELECT r.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events e WHERE e.run_id=r.id) AS n_events "
332	                "FROM runs r WHERE r.conversation_id=? ORDER BY r.created_at, r.rowid",
333	                (conversation_id,),
334	            )
335	        ]
336	        return {"conversation": conv, "runs": runs, "messages": self._messages(conversation_id)}
337	
338	    def _messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
339	        messages = [dict(r) for r in self._query(
340	            "SELECT * FROM messages WHERE conversation_id=? ORDER BY seq", (conversation_id,)
341	        )]
342	        by_id = {m["id"]: m for m in messages}
343	        for m in messages:
344	            m["blocks"] = []
345	        if not messages:
346	            return messages
347	        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in messages)
348	        rows = self._query(
349	            f"SELECT * FROM blocks WHERE message_id IN ({placeholders}) ORDER BY message_id, seq",
350	            tuple(m["id"] for m in messages),
351	        )
352	        for b in rows:
353	            by_id[b["message_id"]]["blocks"].append(
354	                {
355	                    "id": b["id"],
356	                    "message_id": b["message_id"],
357	                    "seq": b["seq"],
358	                    "type": b["type"],
359	                    "text": b["text"],
360	                    "tool_name": b["tool_name"],
361	                    "tool_use_id": b["tool_use_id"],
362	                    "input": _unjson(b["input_json"]),
363	                    "is_error": None if b["is_error"] is None else bool(b["is_error"]),
364	                    "exit_code": b["exit_code"],
365	                    "duration_ms": b["duration_ms"],
366	                    "fault": _unjson(b["fault_json"]),
367	                    "truncated": bool(b["truncated"]),
368	                }
369	            )
370	        return messages
371	
372	    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None, *,
373	                            title: str | None = None, archived: bool | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
374	        conv = self._conversation(conversation_id)
375	        if conv is None or (user_id is not None and conv["user_id"] != user_id):
376	            return None
377	        sets: list[str] = []
378	        params: list[Any] = []
379	        if title is not None:
380	            sets.append("title=?")
381	            params.append(title.strip()[:200] or conv["title"])
382	        if archived is not None:
383	            sets.append("archived_at=?")
384	            params.append(now_iso() if archived else None)
385	        if sets:
386	            sets.append("updated_at=?")
387	            params.append(now_iso())
388	            params.append(conversation_id)
389	            with self._tx() as cur:
390	                cur.execute(f"UPDATE conversations SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id=?", tuple(params))
391	        return self._conversation(conversation_id)
392	
393	    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
394	        """Soft archive — nothing is ever hard-deleted (ARCHITECTURE §4.3 'Ops')."""
395	        conv = self.update_conversation(conversation_id, user_id, archived=True)
396	        return None if conv is None else {"archived": True, "id": conv["id"]}
397	
398	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
399	    def create_run(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
400	        rec = dict(record or {})
401	        run_id = rec.get("run_id") or rec.get("id") or new_id("r")
402	        user_id = rec.get("user_id") or ANON_USER_ID
403	        scenario_id = rec.get("scenario_id") or ""
404	        self.upsert_user(user_id)
405	        conversation_id = rec.get("conversation_id")
406	        if not conversation_id or self._conversation(conversation_id) is None:
407	            conversation_id = self.create_conversation(
408	                user_id, scenario_id, rec.get("title") or scenario_id
409	            )["id"]
410	        extra = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in _RESERVED}
411	        usage = rec.get("usage") or {}
412	        with self._tx() as cur:
413	            cur.execute(
414	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO runs (id, conversation_id, user_id, scenario_id, model, seed, "
415	                "max_steps, task_prompt, episode_id, status, score, evaluation_json, input_tokens, "
416	                "output_tokens, error, created_at, started_at, finished_at, steps, summary, extra_json) "
417	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
418	                (
419	                    run_id, conversation_id, user_id, scenario_id,
420	                    rec.get("model") or config.DEFAULT_MODEL, rec.get("seed"),
421	                    int(rec.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS),
422	                    rec.get("task_prompt") or "", rec.get("episode_id"),
423	                    rec.get("status") or "queued",
424	                    rec.get("score") if rec.get("score") is not None
425	                    else (rec.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
426	                    _json(rec.get("evaluation")),
427	                    int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0),
428	                    rec.get("error"), rec.get("created_at") or now_iso(), rec.get("started_at"),
429	                    rec.get("finished_at"), rec.get("steps"), rec.get("summary"),
430	                    _json(extra) if extra else None,
431	                ),
432	            )
433	        log.info("run.created", "run row stored", run_id=run_id, conversation_id=conversation_id,
434	                 user_id=user_id, status=rec.get("status"))
435	        return self.get_run(run_id) or {}
436	
437	    def finish_run(self, run_id: str, fields: dict[str, Any] | None = None, **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
438	        """Merge scalar fields into a run row (status, finished_at, evaluation, usage, …).
439	
440	        Named `finish_run` because that is what PLAN.md §3.2 calls it, but it is the general
441	        update: `RunStore.update()` routes here so `api.py` and `loop.py` keep one verb.
442	        """
443	        merged: dict[str, Any] = {**(fields or {}), **kw}
444	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
445	        if row is None:
446	            raise KeyError(run_id)
447	        sets: list[str] = []
448	        params: list[Any] = []
449	        for key, value in merged.items():
450	            if key in ("run_id", "id", "events"):
451	                continue
452	            if key == "evaluation":
453	                sets.append("evaluation_json=?")
454	                params.append(_json(value))
455	                if isinstance(value, dict) and value.get("score") is not None:
456	                    sets.append("score=?")
457	                    params.append(value.get("score"))
458	                continue
459	            if key == "usage" and isinstance(value, dict):
460	                sets += ["input_tokens=?", "output_tokens=?"]
461	                params += [int(value.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(value.get("output_tokens") or 0)]
462	                extra = {**(_unjson(row["extra_json"]) or {}), "usage": value}
463	                sets.append("extra_json=?")
464	                params.append(_json(extra))
465	                continue
466	            if key in RUN_COLUMNS:
467	                sets.append(f"{key}=?")
468	                params.append(value)
469	                continue
470	            extra = {**(_unjson(row["extra_json"]) or {}), key: value}
471	            sets.append("extra_json=?")
472	            params.append(_json(extra))
473	        if sets:
474	            params.append(run_id)
475	            with self._tx() as cur:
476	                cur.execute(f"UPDATE runs SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id=?", tuple(params))
477	            self._touch_conversation(run_id)
478	        if merged.get("status") in TERMINAL_STATUSES:
479	            self.checkpoint()
480	        return self.get_run(run_id) or {}
481	
482	    def get_run(self, run_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
483	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
484	        if row is None:
485	            return None
486	        extra = _unjson(row["extra_json"]) or {}
487	        usage = extra.pop("usage", None) or {}
488	        record: dict[str, Any] = {
489	            "run_id": row["id"],
490	            "status": row["status"],
491	            "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
492	            "model": row["model"],
493	            "seed": row["seed"],
494	            "max_steps": row["max_steps"],
495	            "episode_id": row["episode_id"],
496	            "created_at": row["created_at"],
497	            "started_at": row["started_at"],
498	            "finished_at": row["finished_at"],
499	            "events": [self._event(r) for r in self._query(
500	                "SELECT * FROM events WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY seq", (run_id,))],
501	            "evaluation": _unjson(row["evaluation_json"]),
502	            "usage": {
503	                "input_tokens": int(usage.get("input_tokens") or row["input_tokens"] or 0),
504	                "output_tokens": int(usage.get("output_tokens") or row["output_tokens"] or 0),
505	                **({"cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]}
506	                   if usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens") else {}),
507	                **({"cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"]}
508	                   if usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens") else {}),
509	            },
510	            "error": row["error"],
511	            "conversation_id": row["conversation_id"],
512	            "user_id": row["user_id"],
513	            "task_prompt": row["task_prompt"],
514	            "steps": row["steps"],
515	            "summary": row["summary"],
516	            "score": row["score"],
517	        }
518	        record.update(extra)
519	        return record
520	
521	    @staticmethod
522	    def _event(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict[str, Any]:
523	        return {
524	            "id": row["seq"],
525	            "ts": row["ts"],
526	            "run_id": row["run_id"],
527	            "type": row["type"],
528	            "step": row["step"],
529	            "data": _unjson(row["data"]) or {},
530	        }
531	
532	    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 50, user_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
533	        sql = ("SELECT r.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events e WHERE e.run_id=r.id) AS n_events FROM runs r ")
534	        params: list[Any] = []
535	        if user_id:
536	            sql += "WHERE r.user_id=? "
537	            params.append(user_id)
538	        sql += "ORDER BY r.created_at DESC, r.rowid DESC LIMIT ?"
539	        params.append(int(limit))
540	        return [
541	            {**self._run_summary(r, n_events=int(r["n_events"])), "conversation_id": r["conversation_id"]}
542	            for r in self._query(sql, tuple(params))
543	        ]
544	
545	    def events_after(self, run_id: str, after: int = -1, limit: int = 2000) -> dict[str, Any]:
546	        """The SSE tail: everything with `seq > after`, plus enough state to close the stream."""
547	        row = self._one("SELECT status FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
548	        if row is None:
549	            return {"run_id": run_id, "exists": False, "status": None, "events": []}
550	        rows = self._query(
551	            "SELECT * FROM events WHERE run_id=? AND seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?",
552	            (run_id, int(after), int(limit)),
553	        )
554	        return {
555	            "run_id": run_id,
556	            "exists": True,
557	            "status": row["status"],
558	            "events": [self._event(r) for r in rows],
559	        }
560	
561	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ events + projection
562	    def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
563	        """Insert events idempotently on (run_id, seq) and project them into the transcript.
564	
565	        A row that was already there is skipped entirely — insert AND projection — which is what
566	        makes `run_episode`'s full re-send at `run.finished` a no-op for everything already stored.
567	        """
568	        row = self._one("SELECT conversation_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
569	        if row is None:
570	            raise KeyError(run_id)
571	        conversation_id = row["conversation_id"]
572	        appended = 0
573	        finished = False
574	        with self._tx() as cur:
575	            for event in events or []:
576	                seq = event.get("id")
577	                if seq is None:
578	                    continue
579	                cur.execute(
580	                    "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO events (run_id, seq, ts, type, step, data) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
581	                    (run_id, int(seq), event.get("ts") or now_iso(), event.get("type") or "log",
582	                     event.get("step"), _json(event.get("data") or {}) or "{}"),
583	                )
584	                if cur.rowcount == 0:  # already stored: never project twice
585	                    continue
586	                appended += 1
587	                if event.get("type") == "run.finished":
588	                    finished = True
589	                self._project(cur, conversation_id, run_id, event)
590	            if appended:
591	                cur.execute("UPDATE conversations SET updated_at=? WHERE id=?", (now_iso(), conversation_id))
592	        total = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM events WHERE run_id=?", (run_id,))
593	        if finished:
594	            self.checkpoint(force=True)
595	        return {"run_id": run_id, "appended": appended, "count": int(total["n"]) if total else 0}
596	
597	    # --- projection helpers (ARCHITECTURE §4.5) -------------------------------------------------
598	    def _message(self, cur: sqlite3.Cursor, conversation_id: str, run_id: str, role: str,
599	                 step: int | None, ts: str) -> str:
600	        if step is None:
601	            found = cur.execute(
602	                "SELECT id FROM messages WHERE run_id=? AND role=? AND step IS NULL LIMIT 1",
603	                (run_id, role),
604	            ).fetchone()
605	        else:
606	            found = cur.execute(
607	                "SELECT id FROM messages WHERE run_id=? AND role=? AND step=? LIMIT 1",
608	                (run_id, role, int(step)),
609	            ).fetchone()
610	        if found:
611	            return found["id"]
612	        seq_row = cur.execute(
613	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1) + 1 AS n FROM messages WHERE conversation_id=?",
614	            (conversation_id,),
615	        ).fetchone()
616	        mid = new_id("m")
617	        cur.execute(
618	            "INSERT INTO messages (id, conversation_id, run_id, seq, role, step, created_at) "
619	            "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
620	            (mid, conversation_id, run_id, int(seq_row["n"]), role, step, ts),
621	        )
622	        return mid
623	
624	    @staticmethod
625	    def _block(cur: sqlite3.Cursor, message_id: str, btype: str, **cols: Any) -> None:
626	        seq_row = cur.execute(
627	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1) + 1 AS n FROM blocks WHERE message_id=?", (message_id,)
628	        ).fetchone()
629	        cur.execute(
630	            "INSERT INTO blocks (id, message_id, seq, type, text, tool_name, tool_use_id, input_json, "
631	            "is_error, exit_code, duration_ms, fault_json, truncated) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
632	            (
633	                new_id("b"), message_id, int(seq_row["n"]), btype, cols.get("text"),
634	                cols.get("tool_name"), cols.get("tool_use_id"), _json(cols.get("input")),
635	                None if cols.get("is_error") is None else int(bool(cols.get("is_error"))),
636	                cols.get("exit_code"), cols.get("duration_ms"), _json(cols.get("fault")),
637	                int(bool(cols.get("truncated"))),
638	            ),
639	        )
640	
641	    def _project(self, cur: sqlite3.Cursor, conversation_id: str, run_id: str,
642	                 event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
643	        etype = event.get("type") or ""
644	        data = event.get("data") or {}
645	        step = event.get("step")
646	        ts = event.get("ts") or now_iso()
647	
648	        if etype == "run.started":
649	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET status='running', started_at=COALESCE(started_at, ?) WHERE id=?",
650	                        (ts, run_id))
651	        elif etype == "episode.reset":
652	            task_prompt = str(data.get("task_prompt") or "")
653	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET episode_id=?, task_prompt=? WHERE id=?",
654	                        (data.get("episode_id"), task_prompt, run_id))
655	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "user", None, ts)
656	            self._block(cur, mid, "text", text=task_prompt)
657	        elif etype in ("turn.text", "turn.thinking"):
658	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "assistant", step, ts)
659	            self._block(cur, mid, "thinking" if etype == "turn.thinking" else "text",
660	                        text=str(data.get("text") or ""))
661	        elif etype == "tool.call":
662	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "assistant", step, ts)
663	            self._block(cur, mid, "tool_use", tool_name=data.get("tool"),
664	                        tool_use_id=data.get("tool_use_id"), input=data.get("input") or {})
665	        elif etype == "tool.result":
666	            # Anthropic requires tool results in a USER turn; one per step, exactly like the
667	            # messages=[…] array the model saw.
668	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "user", step, ts)
669	            output = str(data.get("output") or "")
670	            self._block(
671	                cur, mid, "tool_result",
672	                text=output,
673	                tool_name=data.get("tool"),
674	                tool_use_id=data.get("tool_use_id"),
675	                is_error=bool(data.get("is_error")),
676	                exit_code=_exit_code_of(output),
677	                duration_ms=data.get("duration_ms"),
678	                fault=data.get("fault"),
679	                truncated="…[truncated" in output,
680	            )
681	        elif etype == "llm.call":
682	            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
683	            cur.execute(
684	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO llm_calls (id, run_id, step, attempt, model, request_id, "
685	                "stop_reason, input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_tokens, cache_write_tokens, "
686	                "started_at, duration_ms, error) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
687	                (
688	                    new_id("l"), run_id, int(step or 0), int(data.get("attempt") or 1),
689	                    str(data.get("model") or ""), data.get("request_id"), data.get("stop_reason"),
690	                    usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens"),
691	                    usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens"), usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens"),
692	                    ts, data.get("duration_ms"), data.get("error"),
693	                ),
694	            )
695	            cur.execute(
696	                "UPDATE runs SET input_tokens=input_tokens+?, output_tokens=output_tokens+? WHERE id=?",
697	                (int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0), run_id),
698	            )
699	        elif etype == "episode.evaluated":
700	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET score=?, evaluation_json=? WHERE id=?",
701	                        (data.get("score"), _json(data), run_id))
702	        elif etype == "run.finished":
703	            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
704	            cur.execute(
705	                "UPDATE runs SET status=?, finished_at=?, error=?, steps=COALESCE(?, steps), "
706	                "input_tokens=?, output_tokens=? WHERE id=?",
707	                (
708	                    data.get("status") or "ok", ts, data.get("error"), data.get("steps"),
709	                    int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0), run_id,
710	                ),
711	            )
712	        # fault.fired / workspace.diff / log / interruption / run.resumed / episode.sandbox:
713	        # event row only — the UI reads those straight from the event log (§4.5).
714	
715	    def _touch_conversation(self, run_id: str) -> None:
716	        with self._tx() as cur:
717	            cur.execute(
718	                "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at=? WHERE id=(SELECT conversation_id FROM runs WHERE id=?)",
719	                (now_iso(), run_id),
720	            )
721	
722	    def llm_calls(self, run_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
723	        return [dict(r) for r in self._query(
724	            "SELECT * FROM llm_calls WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY step, attempt", (run_id,))]
725	
726	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ checkpoint
727	    def checkpoint(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
728	        """VACUUM INTO a temp file on the Volume, rename it into place, commit (ARCHITECTURE §4.3)."""
729	        if not self.snapshot_path:
730	            self._dirty = False
731	            return self.health()
732	        # Only the VACUUM touches the database, so the connection lock is held for that alone —
733	        # writing ~1 MB across the FUSE mount and committing the Volume takes seconds, and an SSE
734	        # poll must not queue behind it. `_ckpt_lock` keeps two checkpoints off the same temp file.
735	        with self._ckpt_lock:
736	            tmp = f"{self.snapshot_path}.tmp"
737	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
738	            try:
739	                with self._lock:
740	                    if not self._dirty and not force:
741	                        return self.health()
742	                    pathlib.Path(self.snapshot_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
743	                    if os.path.exists(tmp):
744	                        os.remove(tmp)
745	                    self._vacuum_into(tmp)
746	                    # Cleared inside the lock: no write can slip between the VACUUM and this line,
747	                    # so "not dirty" always means "everything committed is in the snapshot".
748	                    self._dirty = False
749	                vacuum_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
750	                os.replace(tmp, self.snapshot_path)
751	                if self._commit_volume is not None:
752	                    self._commit_volume()
753	                self._last_checkpoint_at = now_iso()
754	                self._last_checkpoint_error = None
755	                log.info("store.checkpoint", "snapshot committed", snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
756	                         bytes=os.path.getsize(self.snapshot_path), vacuum_ms=vacuum_ms,
757	                         dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
758	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed checkpoint must not fail the run
759	                self._dirty = True  # unsaved writes remain unsaved; try again on the next tick
760	                self._last_checkpoint_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
761	                log.error("store.checkpoint_failed", self._last_checkpoint_error)
762	                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
763	                    if os.path.exists(tmp):
764	                        os.remove(tmp)
765	        return self.health()
766	
767	    def _vacuum_into(self, target: str) -> None:
768	        try:
769	            self.conn.execute("VACUUM INTO ?", (target,))
770	        except sqlite3.Error:
771	            # Older SQLite builds reject a bound parameter as the VACUUM target.
772	            self.conn.execute("VACUUM INTO '{}'".format(target.replace("'", "''")))
773	
774	    def start_autocheckpoint(self) -> None:
775	        """Checkpoint every `checkpoint_interval_s` while there are unsaved writes."""
776	        if self._thread is not None or not self.snapshot_path:
777	            return
778	        self._stop.clear()
779	
780	        def _loop() -> None:
781	            while not self._stop.wait(self.checkpoint_interval_s):
782	                if self._dirty:
783	                    self.checkpoint()
784	
785	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, name="store-checkpoint", daemon=True)
786	        self._thread.start()
787	
788	    def stop_autocheckpoint(self) -> None:
789	        self._stop.set()
790	        thread, self._thread = self._thread, None
791	        if thread is not None:
792	            thread.join(timeout=2.0)
793	
794	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
795	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
796	        try:
797	            runs = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs")
798	            events = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM events")
799	            convos = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM conversations")
800	            ok = True
801	        except sqlite3.Error as exc:  # pragma: no cover - only a broken DB gets here
802	            log.error("store.health_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
803	            return {"ok": False, "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:200],
804	                    "last_checkpoint_at": self._last_checkpoint_at}
805	        return {
806	            "ok": ok,
807	            "last_checkpoint_at": self._last_checkpoint_at,
808	            "last_checkpoint_error": self._last_checkpoint_error,
809	            "dirty": self._dirty,
810	            "db_path": self.db_path,
811	            "snapshot_path": self.snapshot_path,
812	            "restored_from_snapshot": self._restored_from_snapshot,
813	            "user_version": self.user_version(),
814	            "runs": int(runs["n"]) if runs else 0,
815	            "events": int(events["n"]) if events else 0,
816	            "conversations": int(convos["n"]) if convos else 0,
817	        }
818	
819	
820	def _exit_code_of(output: str) -> int | None:
821	    """run_command results carry `exit_code` inside the JSON output; blocks store it as a column."""
822	    if not output or not output.lstrip().startswith("{"):
823	        return None
824	    try:
825	        payload = json.loads(output)
826	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
827	        return None
828	    code = payload.get("exit_code") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
829	    return int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else None
