Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:43.902Z
1	"""Browser-facing API for the harness (Modal function `api`, no provider secret).
2	
3	    GET    /health            includes has_provider_key (MUST be false here) + detail.store
4	    GET    /scenarios         proxy to sandbox-env (the browser never talks to the gym directly)
5	    GET    /me                upsert this browser's anonymous user -> {user_id, conversations}
6	    GET    /conversations     this user's conversations, newest first, archived excluded
7	    POST   /conversations     {scenario_id, title?} -> Conversation
8	    GET    /conversations/{id}   conversation + runs + the persisted transcript (messages/blocks)
9	    PATCH  /conversations/{id}   {title?, archived?} -> Conversation
10	    DELETE /conversations/{id}   soft archive -> {archived: true}
11	    POST   /conversations/{id}/runs  {model?, seed?, max_steps?, task_prompt?} -> {run_id, conversation_id}
12	    POST   /runs              validate + create a conversation + a queued run, spawn `run_episode`
13	    GET    /runs              last 50 runs (scoped to the caller when X-Faultline-User is sent)
14	    GET    /runs/{id}         the whole RunRecord (what we also save as evidence)
15	    GET    /runs/{id}/events  SSE tail of the event stream, resumable via Last-Event-ID
16	
17	Identity (PLAN.md §2.9.1) is scoping, not authentication: `X-Faultline-User: u_<uuid4>` is validated,
18	upserted and used to scope conversations and runs; a mismatch is 404, never 403 (no existence
19	oracle). Requests with no header still work — they are attributed to one well-known anonymous user
20	so the CLI and the smoke scripts keep running unchanged.
21	
22	Why SSE and not "run it in the request": Modal caps a web request at 150 s and then 303-redirects,
23	which breaks CORS. So the run happens in a spawned function and the browser tails events out of the
24	Store; each stream closes itself at 110 s and the client reconnects with Last-Event-ID.
25	"""
26	
27	from __future__ import annotations
28	
29	import asyncio
30	import json
31	import os
32	import time
33	import uuid
34	from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable
35	
36	from fastapi import FastAPI, Header, Query, Request
37	from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
38	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, StreamingResponse
39	
40	from faultline_common.log import ctx_request_id, ctx_run_id, get_logger
41	
42	from . import config
43	from .events import now_iso
44	from .gym_client import GymClient, GymError
45	from .store import ANON_USER_ID, TERMINAL_STATUSES, RunStore, valid_user_id
46	
47	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
48	
49	TERMINAL = TERMINAL_STATUSES
50	VERSION = "0.1.0"
51	USER_HEADER = "x-faultline-user"
52	# Routes that must never pay for a user upsert: health checks and the long-lived SSE tail.
53	NO_UPSERT_SUFFIXES = ("/health", "/scenarios", "/events")
54	
55	
56	def new_run_id() -> str:
57	    return f"r_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
58	
59	
60	def default_spawn(run_id: str, req: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
61	    """Spawn the real Modal function (used when the API runs outside modal_app.py)."""
62	    import modal
63	
64	    fn = modal.Function.from_name(
65	        config.APP_NAME, "run_episode", environment_name=config.modal_environment()
66	    )
67	    call = fn.spawn(run_id, req)
68	    return getattr(call, "object_id", None)
69	
70	
71	def sse_frame(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
72	    return f"id: {event['id']}\nevent: {event['type']}\ndata: {json.dumps(event, default=str)}\n\n"
73	
74	
75	def parse_last_event_id(header: str | None, after: int | None, query: str | int | None = None) -> int:
76	    """-1 means "send everything".
77	
78	    Precedence: `Last-Event-ID` header, then `?last_event_id=`, then `?after=`. The browser cannot
79	    set headers on an EventSource, and apps/web re-opens the stream itself, so it sends the resume
80	    point as a query param under BOTH spellings; honour all three (PLAN.md §2.3, docs/web-handoff.md).
81	    """
82	    for candidate in (header, query, after):
83	        if candidate is None or candidate == "":
84	            continue
85	        try:
86	            return int(candidate)
87	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
88	            continue
89	    return -1
90	
91	
92	def user_of(request: Request) -> str | None:
93	    """The validated caller id, or None when the request carried no usable X-Faultline-User."""
94	    return getattr(request.state, "user_id", None)
95	
96	
97	def owns(record: dict[str, Any] | None, user_id: str | None) -> bool:
98	    """Ownership check for runs. Anonymous runs (CLI, smoke scripts) stay readable by anyone."""
99	    if record is None:
100	        return False
101	    owner = record.get("user_id")
102	    if user_id is None or owner in (None, "", ANON_USER_ID):
103	        return True
104	    return owner == user_id
105	
106	
107	def create_app(
108	    *,
109	    store: RunStore | None = None,
110	    gym_factory: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
111	    spawn: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,
112	) -> FastAPI:
113	    """Build the ASGI app. Everything external is injectable so tests need no Modal and no network."""
114	    # Secret boundary (PLAN.md §2.1/§2.4): the browser-facing function must not hold the provider
115	    # key. Fail loudly at startup rather than serving traffic from a process that leaked it.
116	    if "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ:
117	        raise RuntimeError(
118	            "secret boundary violated: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is visible to the harness `api` function; "
119	            "attach the Anthropic secret to `run_episode` only"
120	        )
121	    runs = store or RunStore()
122	    make_gym = gym_factory or (lambda: GymClient())
123	    spawn_fn = spawn or default_spawn
124	
125	    app = FastAPI(title="faultline-harness", version=VERSION)
126	    app.add_middleware(
127	        CORSMiddleware,
128	        allow_origins=["*"],
129	        allow_methods=["*"],
130	        allow_headers=["*"],
131	        expose_headers=["*"],
132	    )
133	
134	    @app.middleware("http")
135	    async def identity_middleware(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
136	        """X-Faultline-User -> validate -> upsert -> request.state.user_id (PLAN.md §2.9.1)."""
137	        raw = (request.headers.get(USER_HEADER) or "").strip()
138	        valid = valid_user_id(raw)
139	        request.state.user_id = raw if valid else None
140	        request.state.user_id_invalid = bool(raw and not valid)
141	        path = request.url.path
142	        if valid and not path.endswith(NO_UPSERT_SUFFIXES):
143	            try:
144	                await asyncio.to_thread(runs.upsert_user, raw, request.headers.get("user-agent"))
145	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - identity is a convenience, never a gate
146	                log.warn("identity.upsert_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", user_id=raw)
147	        return await call_next(request)
148	
149	    @app.middleware("http")
150	    async def request_id_middleware(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
151	        rid = request.headers.get("x-request-id") or f"req_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
152	        ctx_request_id.set(rid)
153	        ctx_run_id.set(None)
154	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
155	        log.info("http.request", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", method=request.method,
156	                 path=request.url.path, query=str(request.url.query) or None)
157	        try:
158	            response = await call_next(request)
159	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - log then re-raise as a 500
160	            log.error("http.error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", method=request.method,
161	                      path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
162	            raise
163	        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = rid
164	        log.info("http.response", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", status=response.status_code,
165	                 path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
166	        return response
167	
168	    def need_user(request: Request) -> tuple[str | None, JSONResponse | None]:
169	        uid = user_of(request)
170	        if uid:
171	            return uid, None
172	        detail = ("X-Faultline-User is malformed (want u_<uuid4>)"
173	                  if getattr(request.state, "user_id_invalid", False)
174	                  else "X-Faultline-User header is required for this route")
175	        return None, JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": detail})
176	
177	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
178	    @app.get("/health")
179	    async def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
180	        reachable: bool | None = None
181	        detail: dict[str, Any] = {}
182	        gym = make_gym()
183	        try:
184	            info = await asyncio.to_thread(gym.health)
185	            reachable = bool(info)
186	            detail["sandbox_env"] = {k: info.get(k) for k in ("svc", "ok", "version") if k in info}
187	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - unreachable is a normal, reportable state
188	            reachable = False
189	            detail["sandbox_env_error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
190	        finally:
191	            try:
192	                gym.close()
193	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
194	                pass
195	        detail["sandbox_env_reachable"] = reachable
196	        try:
197	            detail["store"] = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.health)
198	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the Store being down is reportable, not fatal
199	            detail["store"] = {"ok": False, "last_checkpoint_at": None,
200	                               "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]}
201	        # The secret is attached to `run_episode` only; if this is ever true, the boundary leaked.
202	        has_key = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ
203	        if has_key:
204	            log.error("health.secret_boundary", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY visible to the API function")
205	        return {
206	            "svc": "harness",
207	            "ok": True,
208	            "version": VERSION,
209	            "has_provider_key": has_key,
210	            "model_default": config.model_default(),
211	            "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url(),
212	            "detail": detail,
213	        }
214	
215	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ scenarios
216	    @app.get("/scenarios")
217	    async def scenarios() -> Any:
218	        gym = make_gym()
219	        try:
220	            items = await asyncio.to_thread(gym.scenarios)
221	            return {"scenarios": items}
222	        except GymError as exc:
223	            return JSONResponse(
224	                status_code=502,
225	                content={"error": "sandbox-env unreachable", "detail": str(exc)[:500],
226	                         "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url()},
227	            )
228	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
229	            return JSONResponse(
230	                status_code=502,
231	                content={"error": "sandbox-env unreachable", "detail": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:500]},
232	            )
233	        finally:
234	            try:
235	                gym.close()
236	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
237	                pass
238	
239	    async def scenario_catalogue() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
240	        """Live scenario list, or [] when the gym is unreachable (a blip must not 502 the Run button)."""
241	        gym = make_gym()
242	        try:
243	            return list(await asyncio.to_thread(gym.scenarios) or [])
244	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
245	            log.warn("scenarios.unavailable", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
246	            return []
247	        finally:
248	            try:
249	                gym.close()
250	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
251	                pass
252	
253	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ identity
254	    @app.get("/me")
255	    async def me(request: Request) -> Any:
256	        uid, err = need_user(request)
257	        if err is not None:
258	            return err
259	        assert uid is not None
260	        user = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.upsert_user, uid, request.headers.get("user-agent"))
261	        return {"user_id": uid, "conversations": int((user or {}).get("conversations") or 0)}
262	
263	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ conversations
264	    @app.get("/conversations")
265	    async def list_conversations(request: Request, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)) -> Any:
266	        uid, err = need_user(request)
267	        if err is not None:
268	            return err
269	        assert uid is not None
270	        return await asyncio.to_thread(runs.list_conversations, uid, limit)
271	
272	    @app.post("/conversations")
273	    async def create_conversation(request: Request, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
274	        uid, err = need_user(request)
275	        if err is not None:
276	            return err
277	        assert uid is not None
278	        scenario_id = str(body.get("scenario_id") or "").strip()
279	        if not scenario_id:
280	            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": "scenario_id is required"})
281	        catalogue = await scenario_catalogue()
282	        known = {s.get("id") for s in catalogue if isinstance(s, dict)}
283	        if known and scenario_id not in known:
284	            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={
285	                "error": f"unknown scenario {scenario_id!r}", "known": sorted(k for k in known if k)})
286	        title = body.get("title") or _scenario_title(catalogue, scenario_id)
287	        return await asyncio.to_thread(
288	            runs.create_conversation, uid, scenario_id, title, request.headers.get("user-agent")
289	        )
290	
291	    @app.get("/conversations/{conversation_id}")
292	    async def get_conversation(request: Request, conversation_id: str) -> Any:
293	        uid, err = need_user(request)
294	        if err is not None:
295	            return err
296	        detail = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
297	        if detail is None:
298	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
299	                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
300	        return detail
301	
302	    @app.patch("/conversations/{conversation_id}")
303	    async def patch_conversation(request: Request, conversation_id: str, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
304	        uid, err = need_user(request)
305	        if err is not None:
306	            return err
307	        title = body.get("title")
308	        archived = body.get("archived")
309	        conv = await asyncio.to_thread(
310	            runs.update_conversation, conversation_id, uid,
311	            title=str(title) if isinstance(title, str) else None,
312	            archived=bool(archived) if archived is not None else None,
313	        )
314	        if conv is None:
315	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
316	                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
317	        return conv
318	
319	    @app.delete("/conversations/{conversation_id}")
320	    async def archive_conversation(request: Request, conversation_id: str) -> Any:
321	        uid, err = need_user(request)
322	        if err is not None:
323	            return err
324	        out = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.delete_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
325	        if out is None:
326	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
327	                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
328	        return {"archived": True}
329	
330	    @app.post("/conversations/{conversation_id}/runs")
331	    async def start_conversation_run(request: Request, conversation_id: str,
332	                                     body: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Any:
333	        uid, err = need_user(request)
334	        if err is not None:
335	            return err
336	        detail = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
337	        if detail is None:
338	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
339	                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
340	        conversation = detail.get("conversation") or {}
341	        req = dict(body or {})
342	        out = await _start_run(
343	            scenario_id=str(conversation.get("scenario_id") or ""),
344	            body=req,
345	            user_id=uid,
346	            conversation_id=conversation_id,
347	        )
348	        if isinstance(out, JSONResponse):
349	            return out
350	        return JSONResponse(status_code=202, content=out)
351	
352	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
353	    async def _start_run(*, scenario_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], user_id: str | None,
354	                         conversation_id: str | None) -> Any:
355	        if not scenario_id:
356	            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": "scenario_id is required"})
357	        model = body.get("model") or config.model_default()
358	        if model not in config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST:
359	            return JSONResponse(
360	                status_code=400,
361	                content={"error": f"model {model!r} is not allowed", "allowed": config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST},
362	            )
363	        seed = body.get("seed")
364	        max_steps = body.get("max_steps")
365	        task_prompt: str | None = body.get("task_prompt") or None
366	
367	        # Validate the scenario against the live catalogue; if the gym is unreachable we let the run
368	        # through and let reset fail loudly inside the episode.
369	        catalogue = await scenario_catalogue()
370	        if catalogue:
371	            known = {s.get("id") for s in catalogue if isinstance(s, dict)}
372	            if scenario_id not in known:
373	                return JSONResponse(
374	                    status_code=400,
375	                    content={"error": f"unknown scenario {scenario_id!r}",
376	                             "known": sorted(k for k in known if k)},
377	                )
378	            match = next((s for s in catalogue if s.get("id") == scenario_id), {})
379	            if not max_steps and isinstance(match.get("max_steps"), int):
380	                max_steps = match["max_steps"]
381	            if not task_prompt and isinstance(match.get("task_prompt"), str):
382	                task_prompt = match["task_prompt"]
383	
384	        max_steps = int(max_steps or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS)
385	        max_steps = max(1, min(max_steps, config.HARD_MAX_STEPS))
386	        owner = user_id or ANON_USER_ID
387	
388	        if not conversation_id:
389	            conversation = await asyncio.to_thread(
390	                runs.create_conversation, owner, scenario_id, _scenario_title(catalogue, scenario_id)
391	            )
392	            conversation_id = str((conversation or {}).get("id") or "")
393	
394	        run_id = new_run_id()
395	        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
396	        record = {
397	            "run_id": run_id,
398	            "status": "queued",
399	            "scenario_id": scenario_id,
400	            "model": model,
401	            "seed": seed,
402	            "max_steps": max_steps,
403	            "episode_id": None,
404	            "task_prompt": task_prompt,
405	            "created_at": now_iso(),
406	            "finished_at": None,
407	            "events": [],
408	            "evaluation": None,
409	            "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
410	            "error": None,
411	            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
412	            "user_id": owner,
413	        }
414	        await asyncio.to_thread(runs.create, record)
415	
416	        req = {"scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "seed": seed, "max_steps": max_steps,
417	               "conversation_id": conversation_id, "user_id": owner}
418	        if task_prompt:
419	            req["task_prompt"] = task_prompt
420	        try:
421	            call_id = await asyncio.to_thread(spawn_fn, run_id, req)
422	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the record exists, so fail it visibly
423	            log.error("spawn.failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=run_id)
424	            error = f"spawn failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
425	            await asyncio.to_thread(runs.update, run_id, status="error", error=error,
426	                                    finished_at=now_iso())
427	            return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"error": error, "run_id": run_id})
428	
429	        log.info("run.spawned", "run_episode spawned", run_id=run_id, scenario_id=scenario_id,
430	                 model=model, max_steps=max_steps, conversation_id=conversation_id, user_id=owner,
431	                 call_id=call_id if isinstance(call_id, str) else None)
432	        return {"run_id": run_id, "conversation_id": conversation_id, "status": "queued",
433	                "scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "max_steps": max_steps, "seed": seed}
434	
435	    @app.post("/runs")
436	    async def create_run(request: Request, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
437	        out = await _start_run(
438	            scenario_id=str(body.get("scenario_id") or "").strip(),
439	            body=body,
440	            user_id=user_of(request),
441	            conversation_id=None,
442	        )
443	        return out
444	
445	    @app.get("/runs")
446	    async def list_runs(request: Request, limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200)) -> dict[str, Any]:
447	        # Scoped to the caller when the browser sends its id; unscoped for the CLI/evidence scripts.
448	        items = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.list_runs, limit, user_of(request))
449	        return {"runs": items}
450	
451	    @app.get("/runs/{run_id}")
452	    async def get_run(request: Request, run_id: str) -> Any:
453	        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
454	        record = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get, run_id)
455	        if not owns(record, user_of(request)):
456	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": f"unknown run {run_id!r}"})
457	        return record
458	
459	    @app.get("/runs/{run_id}/events")
460	    async def stream_events(
461	        request: Request,
462	        run_id: str,
463	        after: int | None = Query(None),
464	        last_event_id_q: str | None = Query(None, alias="last_event_id"),
465	        last_event_id: str | None = Header(None, alias="Last-Event-ID"),
466	    ) -> Any:
467	        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
468	        head = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.events_after, run_id, 2**62, 1)
469	        if not (head or {}).get("exists"):
470	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": f"unknown run {run_id!r}"})
471	        uid = user_of(request)
472	        if uid:
473	            record = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get, run_id)
474	            if not owns(record, uid):
475	                return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": f"unknown run {run_id!r}"})
476	        start_after = parse_last_event_id(last_event_id, after, last_event_id_q)
477	        log.info("sse.open", "client attached", run_id=run_id, after=start_after)
478	        return StreamingResponse(
479	            _event_stream(runs, run_id, start_after),
480	            media_type="text/event-stream",
481	            headers={
482	                "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
483	                "Connection": "keep-alive",
484	                "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
485	            },
486	        )
487	
488	    app.state.store = runs
489	    return app
490	
491	
492	def _scenario_title(catalogue: list[dict[str, Any]], scenario_id: str) -> str:
493	    for item in catalogue or []:
494	        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("id") == scenario_id:
495	            title = item.get("title")
496	            if isinstance(title, str) and title.strip():
497	                return title.strip()
498	    return scenario_id
499	
500	
501	async def _event_stream(runs: RunStore, run_id: str, after: int) -> Iterable[str]:
502	    """Replay everything after `after`, then poll Store.events_after until the run ends or the
503	    110 s window closes (Modal kills a web request at 150 s; the client reconnects with
504	    Last-Event-ID and `reason: window` tells it this is NOT the end of the run)."""
505	    t0 = time.monotonic()
506	    last_id = after
507	    last_keepalive = t0
508	    sent = 0
509	    status: str | None = None
510	    yield "retry: 1000\n\n"
511	    try:
512	        while True:
513	            page = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.events_after, run_id, last_id)
514	            for event in (page or {}).get("events") or []:
515	                if not isinstance(event, dict) or event.get("id") is None:
516	                    continue
517	                if event["id"] > last_id:
518	                    last_id = event["id"]
519	                    sent += 1
520	                    yield sse_frame(event)
521	            status = (page or {}).get("status")
522	            now = time.monotonic()
523	            if status in TERMINAL:
524	                yield (
525	                    "event: done\ndata: "
526	                    + json.dumps({"reason": "finished", "status": status, "last_event_id": last_id,
527	                                  "run_id": run_id})
528	                    + "\n\n"
529	                )
530	                log.info("sse.close", "run finished", run_id=run_id, status=status, sent=sent,
531	                         dur_ms=int((now - t0) * 1000))
532	                return
533	            if now - t0 >= config.SSE_WINDOW_S:
534	                yield (
535	                    "event: done\ndata: "
536	                    + json.dumps({"reason": "window", "status": status, "last_event_id": last_id,
537	                                  "run_id": run_id})
538	                    + "\n\n"
539	                )
540	                log.info("sse.close", "window elapsed", run_id=run_id, status=status, sent=sent,
541	                         dur_ms=int((now - t0) * 1000))
542	                return
543	            if now - last_keepalive >= config.SSE_KEEPALIVE_S:
544	                last_keepalive = now
545	                yield ": keepalive\n\n"
546	            await asyncio.sleep(config.SSE_POLL_S)
547	    except asyncio.CancelledError:  # pragma: no cover - client went away
548	        log.info("sse.cancelled", "client disconnected", run_id=run_id, sent=sent)
549	        raise
550

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:44.676Z
1	"""The model loop: one episode from reset to evaluate.
2	
3	Runs inside the Modal function `run_episode` — the only process in Faultline that ever sees
4	ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. It is deliberately synchronous: a Modal function has no ambient event loop and
5	the whole loop is a sequence of blocking round trips (model -> tool -> observe -> model).
6	
7	Shape of one step
8	    1. client.messages.create(...)               -> text blocks + tool_use blocks
9	    2. emit turn.text for prose
10	    3. for each tool_use, in order: tool.call -> execute over MCP -> observe -> tool.result
11	       (+ fault.fired for every new entry in observe().faults_fired, + workspace.diff after mutations)
12	    4. all tool_results for the turn go back in ONE user message (a missing one is a 400)
13	
14	Stops on `submit`, on end_turn without tool use, or at max_steps (status `truncated`).
15	Always evaluates (when an episode exists) and always DELETEs the episode in a finally block.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import os
21	import re
22	import time
23	import uuid
24	from typing import Any, Callable
25	
26	from faultline_common.log import ctx_episode_id, ctx_run_id, ctx_step, get_logger, truncate
27	
28	from . import config
29	from .events import EventSink, now_iso
30	from .gym_client import GymClient
31	from .mcp_client import SyncMCPClient, tool_error_json
32	from .prompts import SUBMIT_TOOL, apply_cache_breakpoint, build_task_message, system_blocks
33	
34	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
35	
36	# GRADING.md "mutating" definition, kept verbatim so harness and grader agree on what a mutation is.
37	MUTATING_RE = re.compile(
38	    r"(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b"
39	    r"|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))"
40	)
41	PY_OPEN_WRITE_RE = re.compile(r"open\s*\([^)]*['\"][wa]")
42	
43	CODE_TO_KIND = {"ENOENT": "missing_file", "EACCES": "denied_write", "ETIMEDOUT": "ack_lost"}
44	RETRYABLE_EXC_NAMES = {"RateLimitError", "APIConnectionError", "APITimeoutError", "InternalServerError"}
45	THINKING_CAP = 8_000  # chars of summarised thinking kept in one `turn.thinking` event
46	TERMINAL = {"ok", "error", "truncated"}
47	
48	
49	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- pure helpers
50	
51	
52	def is_mutating(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
53	    """Does this tool call intend to change the workspace? (GRADING.md ledger semantics)"""
54	    if tool == "write_file":
55	        return True
56	    if tool != "run_command":
57	        return False
58	    command = (args or {}).get("command") or ""
59	    if MUTATING_RE.search(command):
60	        return True
61	    if "-c" in command and "python" in command and PY_OPEN_WRITE_RE.search(command):
62	        return True
63	    return False
64	
65	
66	def call_summary(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
67	    """Short human label for logs/CLI: the command, or the path."""
68	    a = args or {}
69	    if tool == "run_command":
70	        return truncate(str(a.get("command", "")), 200)
71	    if tool == "submit":
72	        return truncate(str(a.get("summary", "")), 200)
73	    return str(a.get("path", ""))
74	
75	
76	def is_retryable_model_error(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
77	    """Retry rate limits, connection blips and 5xx; fail fast on 4xx (bad key, bad model, bad args)."""
78	    name = type(exc).__name__
79	    if name in RETRYABLE_EXC_NAMES:
80	        return True
81	    status = getattr(exc, "status_code", None)
82	    return isinstance(status, int) and status >= 500
83	
84	
85	def new_faults(observed: list[dict[str, Any]], seen: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
86	    """faults_fired is append-only; take the suffix, then guard with a key set for safety."""
87	    if len(observed) <= len(seen):
88	        return []
89	    seen_keys = {(f.get("step"), f.get("kind"), f.get("path"), f.get("mode")) for f in seen}
90	    return [
91	        f
92	        for f in observed[len(seen) :]
93	        if (f.get("step"), f.get("kind"), f.get("path"), f.get("mode")) not in seen_keys
94	    ]
95	
96	
97	def inferred_fault(step: int, error_code: str | None, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
98	    """Fallback used only when observe() is unavailable; ground truth is the ledger delta."""
99	    kind = CODE_TO_KIND.get(error_code or "")
100	    if not kind:
101	        return None
102	    return {
103	        "step": step,
104	        "kind": kind,
105	        "path": (args or {}).get("path"),
106	        "mode": "transient",
107	        "inferred": True,
108	    }
109	
110	
111	def block_attr(block: Any, name: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
112	    """Content blocks are pydantic objects from the SDK and plain dicts in tests."""
113	    if isinstance(block, dict):
114	        return block.get(name, default)
115	    return getattr(block, name, default)
116	
117	
118	def request_id_of(obj: Any) -> str | None:
119	    """Anthropic's `request-id` response header, which the SDK hangs off the parsed object.
120	
121	    Present as `_request_id` on a Message and on APIStatusError; absent on connection errors and on
122	    any test double, so this is best-effort and never raises.
123	    """
124	    for attr in ("_request_id", "request_id"):
125	        value = getattr(obj, attr, None)
126	        if isinstance(value, str) and value:
127	            return value
128	    return None
129	
130	
131	def usage_from(resp: Any) -> dict[str, int]:
132	    u = getattr(resp, "usage", None)
133	    if u is None:
134	        return {}
135	    return {
136	        "input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "input_tokens", 0) or 0),
137	        "output_tokens": int(getattr(u, "output_tokens", 0) or 0),
138	        # Both cache counters: `read` proves the moving breakpoint (prompts.apply_cache_breakpoint)
139	        # is hitting, `creation` proves it is writing. schemas.Usage declares both.
140	        "cache_read_input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "cache_read_input_tokens", 0) or 0),
141	        "cache_creation_input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) or 0),
142	    }
143	
144	
145	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- model call
146	
147	
148	def anthropic_default_headers(env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
149	    """Headers every model call carries.
150	
151	    The key in `anthropic-secret` is org-scoped, not workspace-scoped, so the API requires an
152	    `anthropic-workspace-id` header naming the workspace to bill/attribute to (a 400 otherwise).
153	    The secret ships that id as ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE; it is never logged unmasked.
154	    """
155	    env = os.environ if env is None else env
156	    ws = (env.get("ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE") or "").strip()
157	    return {"anthropic-workspace-id": ws} if ws else {}
158	
159	
160	def default_anthropic_client() -> Any:
161	    import anthropic
162	
163	    return anthropic.Anthropic(default_headers=anthropic_default_headers())
164	
165	
166	def create_message(
167	    client: Any,
168	    *,
169	    model: str,
170	    system: list[dict[str, Any]],
171	    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
172	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
173	    sink: EventSink,
174	    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
175	    retries: int = config.MODEL_RETRIES,
176	) -> Any:
177	    """messages.create with a typed retry chain (rate limit / 5xx / connection)."""
178	    # Two cache breakpoints: the static one on the system block, and a moving one on the last block
179	    # of the newest user turn so the GROWING conversation caches too (see prompts.py for why the
180	    # system breakpoint alone never writes on Haiku 4.5).
181	    apply_cache_breakpoint(messages)
182	    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
183	        "model": model,
184	        "max_tokens": config.MAX_TOKENS,
185	        "system": system,
186	        "tools": tools,
187	        "messages": messages,
188	    }
189	    thinking = config.thinking_for(model)
190	    if thinking is not None:
191	        kwargs["thinking"] = thinking
192	    delay = 1.0
193	    last: BaseException | None = None
194	    for attempt in range(1, retries + 2):
195	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
196	        try:
197	            resp = client.messages.create(**kwargs)
198	        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - classified immediately below
199	            last = exc
200	            # One `llm.call` per attempt, failures included, so the browser can show a retry
201	            # (ARCHITECTURE.md §5.1 / schemas.Event). Usage is unknown for a failed call.
202	            sink.emit(
203	                "llm.call",
204	                {
205	                    "attempt": attempt,
206	                    "model": model,
207	                    "stop_reason": None,
208	                    "request_id": request_id_of(exc),
209	                    "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
210	                    "duration_ms": int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
211	                    "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300],
212	                },
213	            )
214	            if not is_retryable_model_error(exc) or attempt == retries + 1:
215	                raise
216	            sink.warn(
217	                "model.retry",
218	                f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
219	                attempt=attempt,
220	                model=model,
221	                backoff_s=delay,
222	            )
223	            sleep(delay)
224	            delay *= 2
225	            continue
226	        dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
227	        u = usage_from(resp)
228	        sink.info(
229	            "model.call",
230	            "assistant turn",
231	            model=model,
232	            stop_reason=getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
233	            dur_ms=dur,
234	            input_tokens=u.get("input_tokens"),
235	            output_tokens=u.get("output_tokens"),
236	            cache_read_input_tokens=u.get("cache_read_input_tokens") or None,
237	            attempt=attempt if attempt > 1 else None,
238	        )
239	        sink.emit(
240	            "llm.call",
241	            {
242	                "attempt": attempt,
243	                "model": model,
244	                "stop_reason": getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
245	                "request_id": request_id_of(resp),
246	                "usage": u,
247	                "duration_ms": dur,
248	            },
249	        )
250	        return resp
251	    raise last  # pragma: no cover - loop always returns or raises
252	
253	
254	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the loop
255	
256	
257	def run_episode_sync(
258	    run_id: str,
259	    req: dict[str, Any],
260	    *,
261	    store: Any = None,
262	    gym: Any = None,
263	    mcp_factory: Callable[[str], Any] | None = None,
264	    anthropic_client: Any = None,
265	    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
266	) -> dict[str, Any]:
267	    """Run one episode end to end. Returns the final RunRecord dict (also persisted to the Dict)."""
268	    from .store import RunStore
269	
270	    store = store or RunStore()
271	    req = dict(req or {})
272	    scenario_id = req.get("scenario_id") or ""
273	    model = req.get("model") or config.model_default()
274	    seed = req.get("seed")
275	
276	    record = store.get(run_id)
277	    if record is None:
278	        # No row yet (a `modal run` smoke, or a Store that lost the queued record): create one so
279	        # append_events has a parent. create_run provisions the conversation/user if needed.
280	        record = store.create(
281	            {
282	                "run_id": run_id,
283	                "status": "queued",
284	                "scenario_id": scenario_id,
285	                "model": model,
286	                "seed": seed,
287	                "max_steps": req.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS,
288	                "episode_id": None,
289	                "task_prompt": req.get("task_prompt"),
290	                "created_at": now_iso(),
291	                "finished_at": None,
292	                "events": [],
293	                "evaluation": None,
294	                "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
295	                "error": None,
296	                "conversation_id": req.get("conversation_id"),
297	                "user_id": req.get("user_id"),
298	            }
299	        )
300	    record["model"] = model
301	    record["scenario_id"] = scenario_id
302	    sink = EventSink(store, record, flush_interval=config.EVENT_FLUSH_S)
303	
304	    ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
305	    ctx_episode_id.set(None)  # containers are reused across runs; never inherit the last episode
306	    ctx_step.set(None)
307	
308	    usage = {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0, "cache_read_input_tokens": 0,
309	             "cache_creation_input_tokens": 0}
310	    status = "running"
311	    error: str | None = None
312	    episode_id: str | None = None
313	    seen_faults: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
314	    mcp: Any = None
315	    owns_gym = gym is None
316	    gym = gym or GymClient(run_id=run_id)
317	    t_start = time.time()
318	    max_steps = int(req.get("max_steps") or record.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS)
319	    step = 0
320	
321	    def observe() -> dict[str, Any] | None:
322	        if not episode_id:
323	            return None
324	        try:
325	            return gym.observe(episode_id)
326	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - observation is best-effort telemetry
327	            sink.warn("observe.failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", episode_id=episode_id)
328	            return None
329	
330	    try:
331	        sink.set_status("running")
332	        sink.emit(
333	            "run.started",
334	            {
335	                "scenario_id": scenario_id,
336	                "model": model,
337	                "seed": seed,
338	                "max_steps": max_steps,
339	                "anthropic_workspace": config.masked_workspace(),
340	                "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url(),
341	            },
342	        )
343	
344	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- reset
345	        reset = gym.reset(scenario_id, seed)
346	        episode_id = reset.get("episode_id")
347	        ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
348	        scenario = reset.get("scenario") or {}
349	        task_prompt = scenario.get("task_prompt") or req.get("task_prompt") or ""
350	        if not req.get("max_steps") and isinstance(scenario.get("max_steps"), int):
351	            max_steps = min(int(scenario["max_steps"]), config.HARD_MAX_STEPS)
352	        # PLAN.md §2.9: the run row must be self-describing before the first event of the episode.
353	        sink.update(episode_id=episode_id, max_steps=max_steps, task_prompt=task_prompt)
354	        sink.emit(
355	            "episode.reset",
356	            {
357	                "episode_id": episode_id,
358	                "files": reset.get("files") or [],
359	                "task_prompt": task_prompt,
360	                "scenario": scenario,
361	                "workspace_root": reset.get("workspace_root", "/workspace"),
362	            },
363	        )
364	
365	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- tools
366	        factory = mcp_factory or (lambda ep: SyncMCPClient(config.sandbox_env_url(), ep).open())
367	        mcp = factory(episode_id)
368	        tools = list(mcp.list_tools()) + [SUBMIT_TOOL]
369	        sink.info("tools.ready", "tool list built", tools=[t["name"] for t in tools])
370	
371	        client = anthropic_client or default_anthropic_client()
372	        system = system_blocks()
373	        messages: list[Any] = [
374	            {
375	                "role": "user",
376	                "content": build_task_message(
377	                    task_prompt, reset.get("files"), reset.get("workspace_root", "/workspace")
378	                ),
379	            }
380	        ]
381	
382	        submitted = False
383	        while True:
384	            if step >= max_steps:
385	                status = "truncated"
386	                sink.warn("loop.truncated", "step budget exhausted", max_steps=max_steps)
387	                break
388	            step += 1
389	            sink.step = step
390	            ctx_step.set(step)
391	
392	            resp = create_message(
393	                client, model=model, system=system, tools=tools, messages=messages, sink=sink, sleep=sleep
394	            )
395	            u = usage_from(resp)
396	            for k in usage:
397	                usage[k] += u.get(k, 0)
398	            record["usage"] = {"input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"], "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"]}
399	            if usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]:
400	                record["usage"]["cache_read_input_tokens"] = usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]
401	
402	            content = list(getattr(resp, "content", None) or [])
403	            tool_uses = []
404	            for block in content:
405	                btype = block_attr(block, "type")
406	                if btype == "text":
407	                    text = block_attr(block, "text", "") or ""
408	                    if text.strip():
409	                        sink.emit("turn.text", {"text": text}, step=step)
410	                elif btype == "tool_use":
411	                    tool_uses.append(block)
412	                elif btype in ("thinking", "redacted_thinking"):
413	                    thought = str(block_attr(block, "thinking", "") or "")
414	                    sink.debug("turn.thinking", "thinking block", chars=len(thought))
415	                    # The summarised thinking is part of the transcript the browser renders
416	                    # (schemas.Event `turn.thinking`). Redacted blocks carry no readable text, so
417	                    # they stay a log line only. Capped: a run record lives in one Dict value.
418	                    if thought.strip():
419	                        sink.emit("turn.thinking", {"text": truncate(thought, THINKING_CAP)}, step=step)
420	
421	            messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
422	
423	            if not tool_uses:
424	                status = "ok"
425	                sink.info(
426	                    "loop.end_turn",
427	                    "assistant finished without a tool call",
428	                    stop_reason=getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
429	                )
430	                break
431	
432	            tool_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
433	            for block in tool_uses:
434	                name = block_attr(block, "name") or ""
435	                args = block_attr(block, "input") or {}
436	                tuid = block_attr(block, "id") or f"tu_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
437	                mutating = is_mutating(name, args)
438	                sink.emit(
439	                    "tool.call",
440	                    {"tool": name, "input": args, "tool_use_id": tuid, "mutating": mutating},
441	                    step=step,
442	                )
443	
444	                if name == "submit":
445	                    summary = str(args.get("summary", "")).strip()
446	                    text, is_error, dur_ms, code = "submitted", False, 0, None
447	                    record["summary"] = truncate(summary, 4000)
448	                    submitted = True
449	                else:
450	                    t0 = time.perf_counter()
451	                    try:
452	                        res = mcp.call(name, args, timeout=config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S)
453	                        text, is_error, dur_ms, code = res.text, res.is_error, res.duration_ms, res.error_code
454	                    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a tool failure is never fatal
455	                        dur_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
456	                        text = tool_error_json(f"{name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "EINTERNAL")
457	                        is_error, code = True, "EINTERNAL"
458	                        sink.error("tool.exception", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", tool=name)
459	
460	                obs = observe() if (mutating or is_error) and not submitted else None
461	                fired = new_faults(list((obs or {}).get("faults_fired") or []), seen_faults) if obs else []
462	                fault = fired[0] if fired else (inferred_fault(step, code, args) if (is_error and not obs) else None)
463	
464	                sink.emit(
465	                    "tool.result",
466	                    {
467	                        "tool_use_id": tuid,
468	                        "tool": name,
469	                        "output": truncate(text, 8000),
470	                        "is_error": is_error,
471	                        "duration_ms": dur_ms,
472	                        "error_code": code,
473	                        "summary": call_summary(name, args),
474	                        **({"fault": fault} if fault else {}),
475	                    },
476	                    step=step,
477	                )
478	                for f in fired:
479	                    seen_faults.append(f)
480	                    sink.emit("fault.fired", f, step=step)
481	                if mutating and obs:
482	                    sink.emit(
483	                        "workspace.diff",
484	                        {"files": obs.get("files") or [], "diffs": obs.get("diffs") or []},
485	                        step=step,
486	                    )
487	
488	                tool_results.append(
489	                    {
490	                        "type": "tool_result",
491	                        "tool_use_id": tuid,
492	                        "content": text or "(no output)",
493	                        "is_error": bool(is_error),
494	                    }
495	                )
496	
497	            # ALL results for one assistant turn go back in ONE user message.
498	            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": tool_results})
499	            sink.flush(force=True)  # one Store round trip per step, not per event
500	
501	            if submitted:
502	                status = "ok"
503	                sink.info("loop.submitted", "agent called submit", step=step)
504	                break
505	
506	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - any failure fails the run cleanly, with an event
507	        status = "error"
508	        error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
509	        log.error("run.exception", error, run_id=run_id, episode_id=episode_id)
510	        try:
511	            sink.error("run.exception", error)
512	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
513	            pass
514	    finally:
515	        sink.step = None
516	        ctx_step.set(None)
517	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- evaluate
518	        if episode_id:
519	            try:
520	                evaluation = gym.evaluate(episode_id)
521	                record["evaluation"] = evaluation
522	                sink.emit("episode.evaluated", evaluation)
523	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - grading failure must not lose the transcript
524	                msg = f"evaluate failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
525	                sink.error("evaluate.failed", msg, episode_id=episode_id)
526	                error = error or msg
527	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- teardown (always)
528	        if episode_id:
529	            try:
530	                gym.delete(episode_id)
531	                sink.info("episode.deleted", "sandbox terminated", episode_id=episode_id)
532	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
533	                sink.warn("episode.delete_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", episode_id=episode_id)
534	        if mcp is not None:
535	            try:
536	                mcp.close()
537	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
538	                sink.warn("mcp.close_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
539	        if owns_gym:
540	            try:
541	                gym.close()
542	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
543	                pass
544	
545	        duration_ms = int((time.time() - t_start) * 1000)
546	        final_status = status if status in TERMINAL else ("error" if error else "ok")
547	        finished_at = now_iso()
548	        sink.emit(
549	            "run.finished",
550	            {
551	                "status": final_status,
552	                "usage": {
553	                    "input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"],
554	                    "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"],
555	                    **({"cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]} if usage["cache_read_input_tokens"] else {}),
556	                    **({"cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"]} if usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"] else {}),
557	                },
558	                "duration_ms": duration_ms,
559	                "steps": step,
560	                "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
561	                **({"error": error} if error else {}),
562	            },
563	        )
564	        # PLAN.md §2.9: re-send the COMPLETE in-memory event list — `append_events` is idempotent on
565	        # (run_id, seq), so a Store restart that dropped the tail of this run self-heals here — then
566	        # write the final scalars in one update.
567	        sink.resend_all()
568	        sink.update(
569	            status=final_status,
570	            finished_at=finished_at,
571	            error=error,
572	            steps=step,
573	            episode_id=episode_id,
574	            usage={
575	                "input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"],
576	                "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"],
577	                "cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"],
578	                "cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"],
579	            },
580	            **({"summary": record["summary"]} if record.get("summary") else {}),
581	            **({"evaluation": record["evaluation"]} if record.get("evaluation") else {}),
582	        )
583	        log.info(
584	            "run.finished",
585	            final_status,
586	            run_id=run_id,
587	            episode_id=episode_id,
588	            steps=step,
589	            dur_ms=duration_ms,
590	            score=(record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
591	        )
592	    return record

1	"""Modal app `faultline-harness`: the browser-facing API + the model loop + the SQLite Store.
2	
3	    api           ASGI (FastAPI). NO secrets. Spawns runs, serves run state and the SSE tail.
4	                  https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run
5	    Store         @app.cls(max_containers=1, volumes={"/data": faultline-db}) — the ONLY process
6	                  that opens the database. Hot copy on /tmp, consistent snapshots on the Volume
7	                  (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4).
8	    run_episode   the ONLY function with the provider secret attached; runs one episode.
9	
10	Deploy:  modal deploy -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py
11	Smoke:   modal run -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py --scenario lost-ack
12	Backup:  modal run -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py::checkpoint_now
13	         modal volume get -e local faultline-db faultline.sqlite3 ./runs/
14	
15	NOTE on `modal serve`: the served app gets its OWN Store container with the SAME Volume mounted,
16	which would make two writers for one database file. Deploy (or point the served app at a different
17	volume) rather than serving this app while the deployed one is live.
18	
19	Deploy-time env (baked into the image so containers need no lookup):
20	    SANDBOX_ENV_URL             default https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run
21	    ANTHROPIC_MODEL             default claude-haiku-4-5

194	    """Hand the run to the secret-bearing function and return immediately (Modal caps requests at 150 s)."""
195	    call = run_episode.spawn(run_id, req)

199	@app.function(image=IMAGE, min_containers=1, scaledown_window=300, timeout=300)
200	@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=50)
201	@modal.asgi_app()
202	def api():
203	    """FastAPI app. Deliberately has no secrets: /health must report has_provider_key=false."""
204	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger
205	
206	    from harness.api import create_app
207	
208	    log = get_logger(config.SVC)
209	    log.info(
210	        "api.boot",
211	        "asgi app starting",
212	        has_key=bool(os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")),
213	        sandbox_env_url=config.sandbox_env_url(),
214	        model_default=config.model_default(),
215	    )
216	    return create_app(store=_store(), spawn=_spawn)

219	@app.function(
220	    image=IMAGE,
221	    # required_keys makes a misconfigured secret fail at deploy time, not mid-episode.
222	    secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name(SECRET_NAME, required_keys=["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"])],
223	    timeout=900,
224	)
225	def run_episode(run_id: str, req: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
226	    """Run one episode. The only process in Faultline that sees ANTHROPIC_API_KEY."""
227	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger
228	
229	    from harness.loop import run_episode_sync
230	
231	    log = get_logger(config.SVC)
232	    log.info(
233	        "run_episode.start",
234	        "spawned",
235	        run_id=run_id,
236	        has_key=bool(os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")),  # never the value
237	        anthropic_workspace=config.masked_workspace(),
238	        scenario_id=req.get("scenario_id"),
239	        model=req.get("model") or config.model_default(),
240	        sandbox_env_url=config.sandbox_env_url(),
241	    )
242	    record = run_episode_sync(run_id, req, store=_store())
243	    return {
244	        "run_id": record.get("run_id"),
245	        "status": record.get("status"),
246	        "episode_id": record.get("episode_id"),
247	        "steps": record.get("steps"),
248	        "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
249	        "events": len(record.get("events") or []),
250	        "error": record.get("error"),
251	    }
252	
253	
254	def _deployed_store() -> Any:
255	    """The DEPLOYED Store, resolved by name.
256	
257	    Local entrypoints must never touch the in-module `Store`: `modal run` builds an ephemeral app,
258	    so that would start a SECOND container with the same Volume mounted — two writers for one
259	    SQLite file. By name, every caller lands on the one deployed container.
260	    """
261	    return modal.Cls.from_name(
262	        config.APP_NAME, "Store", environment_name=config.modal_environment()
263	    )()
264	
265	
266	@app.local_entrypoint()
267	def run(scenario: str = "lost-ack", model: str = "", max_steps: int = 0, seed: int = -1,
268	        user: str = "") -> None:
269	    """Spawn one episode on the DEPLOYED harness and tail its events from the Store.
270	
271	    Dev smoke test, no web app needed. `--user u_<uuid4>` scopes the run to a browser identity, so
272	    `GET /conversations` with the same header lists it afterwards.
273	    """
274	    import json
275	    import time
276	    import uuid
277	
278	    run_id = f"r_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
279	    req: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": scenario}
280	    if model:
281	        req["model"] = model
282	    if max_steps:
283	        req["max_steps"] = max_steps
284	    if seed >= 0:
285	        req["seed"] = seed
286	    if user:
287	        req["user_id"] = user
288	
289	    store = _deployed_store()
290	    fn = modal.Function.from_name(config.APP_NAME, "run_episode",
291	                                  environment_name=config.modal_environment())
292	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.spawn", "run_id": run_id, "req": req}))
293	    call = fn.spawn(run_id, req)
294	
295	    seen = -1
296	    deadline = time.time() + 900
297	    status = None
298	    while time.time() < deadline:
299	        page = store.events_after.remote(run_id, seen) or {}
300	        for event in page.get("events") or []:
301	            data = event.get("data") or {}
302	            label = data.get("summary") or data.get("msg") or data.get("status") or ""
303	            print(f"[{event.get('id'):>3}] step={event.get('step')} {event.get('type'):<18} {str(label)[:110]}")
304	            seen = max(seen, int(event.get("id", seen)))
305	        status = page.get("status")
306	        if status in {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}:
307	            record = store.get_run.remote(run_id) or {}
308	            print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.finished", "status": status,
309	                              "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
310	                              "conversation_id": record.get("conversation_id"),
311	                              "error": record.get("error")}))
312	            break
313	        time.sleep(1.0)
314	    else:
315	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.timeout", "run_id": run_id}))
316	
317	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.result", "result": call.get(timeout=60)}, default=str))
318	
319	
320	@app.local_entrypoint()
321	def checkpoint_now() -> None:
322	    """Force a consistent snapshot onto the Volume (evidence/export, PLAN.md §5)."""
323	    import json
324

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:17:56.907Z
1	"""Event sink: the single writer of a run's event stream.
2	
3	Every event gets a monotonic 0-based `id` (that is also the SSE `Last-Event-ID`), an ISO-8601 Z
4	timestamp, is validated against `faultline_common.schemas.Event`, appended to the in-memory
5	`RunRecord`, handed to the Store via `append_events` (idempotent on `(run_id, seq)`) and mirrored to
6	stdout as a unified JSON log line.
7	
8	Persistence policy (PLAN.md §2.9): the loop no longer re-`put`s the whole record after every event.
9	Events are appended in small batches — coalesced over `flush_interval` seconds so a 150-event run
10	costs a few dozen round trips instead of 150 — and the sink re-sends its COMPLETE in-memory list at
11	`run.finished`, which back-fills anything a Store restart dropped. A failed flush is never fatal: the
12	batch stays pending and the final re-send closes the gap.
13	
14	`sink.log(...)` is the other direction: a unified log line that is ALSO mirrored into the stream as
15	a `log` event so the browser timeline can show harness-side detail (bounded to 500 chars/value).
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import time
21	from datetime import datetime, timezone
22	from typing import Any, Callable
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
25	from faultline_common.schemas import Event
26	
27	from . import config
28	from .store import RunStore
29	
30	_log = get_logger(config.SVC)
31	
32	TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
33	
34	
35	def now_iso() -> str:
36	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
37	
38	
39	def _bound(value: Any, cap: int = config.LOG_VALUE_CAP) -> Any:
40	    """Bound one payload value so mirrored log events can never blow up the record."""
41	    if isinstance(value, str):
42	        return truncate(value, cap)
43	    if isinstance(value, dict):
44	        return {k: _bound(v, cap) for k, v in list(value.items())[:50]}
45	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
46	        return [_bound(v, cap) for v in list(value)[:50]]
47	    return value
48	
49	
50	class EventSink:
51	    """Owns the RunRecord for the lifetime of a run; appends events to the Store as they happen."""
52	
53	    def __init__(self, store: RunStore, record: dict[str, Any], *,
54	                 flush_interval: float = 0.0, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic):
55	        self.store = store
56	        self.record = record
57	        self.run_id: str = record["run_id"]
58	        self.step: int | None = None
59	        self.flush_interval = float(flush_interval)
60	        self._clock = clock
61	        self._pending: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
62	        self._last_flush: float | None = None
63	        self.flush_failures = 0
64	        record.setdefault("events", [])
65	
66	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ core
67	    def emit(self, type: str, data: dict[str, Any] | None = None, step: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
68	        ev = Event(
69	            id=len(self.record["events"]),
70	            ts=now_iso(),
71	            run_id=self.run_id,
72	            type=type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
73	            step=self.step if step is None else step,
74	            data=data or {},
75	        ).model_dump()
76	        self.record["events"].append(ev)
77	        self._pending.append(ev)
78	        self.flush()
79	        if type != "log":
80	            _log.info(
81	                type,
82	                "event",
83	                run_id=self.run_id,
84	                step=ev["step"],
85	                event_id=ev["id"],
86	                keys=sorted((data or {}).keys()) or None,
87	            )
88	        return ev
89	
90	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ persistence
91	    def flush(self, force: bool = False) -> int:
92	        """Send pending events to the Store. Returns how many were sent (0 = coalesced or failed)."""
93	        if not self._pending:
94	            return 0
95	        now = self._clock()
96	        if not force and self._last_flush is not None and (now - self._last_flush) < self.flush_interval:
97	            return 0
98	        batch = list(self._pending)
99	        try:
100	            self.store.append_events(self.run_id, batch)
101	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a store blip must not kill the episode
102	            self.flush_failures += 1
103	            _log.warn("store.append_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
104	                      pending=len(batch), failures=self.flush_failures)
105	            return 0
106	        self._pending = self._pending[len(batch):]
107	        self._last_flush = now
108	        return len(batch)
109	
110	    def persist(self) -> None:
111	        """Compatibility alias: force the pending batch out now."""
112	        self.flush(force=True)
113	
114	    def resend_all(self) -> int:
115	        """Re-send the COMPLETE in-memory event list (idempotent) so a Store restart self-heals."""
116	        events = list(self.record.get("events") or [])
117	        try:
118	            result = self.store.append_events(self.run_id, events)
119	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
120	            _log.error("store.resend_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
121	                       events=len(events))
122	            return 0
123	        self._pending = []
124	        self._last_flush = self._clock()
125	        appended = int((result or {}).get("appended") or 0) if isinstance(result, dict) else 0
126	        _log.info("store.resend", "full event list re-sent", run_id=self.run_id,
127	                  events=len(events), backfilled=appended or None)
128	        return appended
129	
130	    def update(self, **fields: Any) -> None:
131	        """Merge scalar fields into the record and the run row (status, usage, evaluation, …)."""
132	        for k, v in fields.items():
133	            self.record[k] = v
134	        self.flush(force=True)
135	        try:
136	            self.store.update(self.run_id, **fields)
137	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
138	            _log.warn("store.update_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
139	                      keys=sorted(fields.keys()))
140	
141	    def set_status(self, status: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
142	        self.update(status=status, **fields)
143	        _log.info("run.status", status, run_id=self.run_id, **{k: _bound(v) for k, v in fields.items()})
144	
145	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ unified log mirror
146	    def log(self, lvl: str, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
147	        """Unified JSON log line + a bounded `log` event in the stream (PLAN.md §2.3/§2.5)."""
148	        bounded = {k: _bound(v) for k, v in kw.items() if v is not None}
149	        # callers may pass their own step/run_id; they win, and must not collide with ours
150	        step = bounded.pop("step", self.step)
151	        run_id = bounded.pop("run_id", self.run_id)
152	        rec = getattr(_log, lvl if lvl in {"debug", "info", "warn", "error"} else "info")(
153	            ev, msg, run_id=run_id, step=step, **bounded
154	        )
155	        if not rec:  # filtered out by LOG_LEVEL
156	            return
157	        self.emit("log", {"svc": config.SVC, "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg, **bounded})
158	
159	    def debug(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
160	        self.log("debug", ev, msg, **kw)
161	
162	    def info(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
163	        self.log("info", ev, msg, **kw)
164	
165	    def warn(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
166	        self.log("warn", ev, msg, **kw)
167	
168	    def error(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
169	        self.log("error", ev, msg, **kw)
170

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:02.064Z
1	"""HTTP client for the sandbox-env gym REST surface (PLAN.md §2.2).
2	
3	    POST   /episodes                 reset   -> ResetResponse
4	    GET    /episodes/{id}            observe -> ObserveResponse
5	    POST   /episodes/{id}/evaluate   grade   -> EvaluateResponse
6	    DELETE /episodes/{id}            teardown
7	    GET    /scenarios                catalogue
8	    GET    /health
9	
10	Connection-level failures are retried with backoff; HTTP status errors are not (a 404 on observe
11	means the episode is gone, retrying only wastes the step budget). Every call is logged.
12	"""
13	
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import time
17	from typing import Any
18	
19	import httpx
20	
21	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
22	
23	from . import config
24	
25	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
26	
27	RETRYABLE = (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.ConnectTimeout, httpx.ReadTimeout, httpx.RemoteProtocolError, httpx.ReadError)
28	
29	
30	class GymError(RuntimeError):
31	    def __init__(self, message: str, status: int | None = None, body: str | None = None):
32	        super().__init__(message)
33	        self.status = status
34	        self.body = body
35	
36	
37	class GymClient:
38	    def __init__(
39	        self,
40	        base_url: str | None = None,
41	        timeout: float = config.GYM_TIMEOUT_S,
42	        retries: int = 3,
43	        client: httpx.Client | None = None,
44	        run_id: str | None = None,
45	    ):
46	        self.base_url = (base_url or config.sandbox_env_url()).rstrip("/")
47	        self.timeout = timeout
48	        self.retries = retries
49	        self.run_id = run_id
50	        self._client = client or httpx.Client(timeout=timeout, follow_redirects=True)
51	        self._owns_client = client is None
52	
53	    def close(self) -> None:
54	        if self._owns_client:
55	            self._client.close()
56	
57	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ transport
58	    def _request(
59	        self, method: str, path: str, *, json: Any = None, timeout: float | None = None, retries: int | None = None
60	    ) -> Any:
61	        url = f"{self.base_url}{path}"
62	        last: Exception | None = None
63	        retries = self.retries if retries is None else max(1, retries)
64	        for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
65	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
66	            try:
67	                resp = self._client.request(method, url, json=json, timeout=timeout or self.timeout)
68	            except RETRYABLE as exc:
69	                last = exc
70	                log.warn(
71	                    "gym.retry",
72	                    f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
73	                    run_id=self.run_id,
74	                    method=method,
75	                    path=path,
76	                    attempt=attempt,
77	                )
78	                if attempt == retries:
79	                    break
80	                time.sleep(min(2 ** (attempt - 1), 4))
81	                continue
82	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
83	            if resp.status_code >= 400:
84	                body = truncate(resp.text, 800)
85	                log.error(
86	                    "gym.error",
87	                    f"{method} {path} -> {resp.status_code}",
88	                    run_id=self.run_id,
89	                    status=resp.status_code,
90	                    dur_ms=dur,
91	                    body=body,
92	                )
93	                raise GymError(f"{method} {path} -> {resp.status_code}: {body}", resp.status_code, body)
94	            log.info("gym.call", f"{method} {path}", run_id=self.run_id, status=resp.status_code, dur_ms=dur)
95	            if not resp.content:
96	                return None
97	            try:
98	                return resp.json()
99	            except ValueError as exc:
100	                raise GymError(f"{method} {path}: non-JSON response: {truncate(resp.text, 200)}") from exc
101	        raise GymError(f"{method} {path}: unreachable after {retries} attempts: {last}") from last
102	
103	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ gym surface
104	    def scenarios(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
105	        data = self._request("GET", "/scenarios", timeout=15.0, retries=1)
106	        if isinstance(data, dict):
107	            for key in ("scenarios", "items", "data"):
108	                if isinstance(data.get(key), list):
109	                    return data[key]
110	            return []
111	        return data or []
112	
113	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
114	        return self._request("GET", "/health", timeout=15.0, retries=1) or {}
115	
116	    def reset(self, scenario_id: str, seed: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
117	        body: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": scenario_id}
118	        if seed is not None:
119	            body["seed"] = seed
120	        return self._request("POST", "/episodes", json=body, timeout=max(self.timeout, 120.0))
121	
122	    def observe(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
123	        return self._request("GET", f"/episodes/{episode_id}")
124	
125	    def evaluate(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
126	        return self._request("POST", f"/episodes/{episode_id}/evaluate", json={}, timeout=max(self.timeout, 120.0))
127	
128	    def delete(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
129	        return self._request("DELETE", f"/episodes/{episode_id}") or {"terminated": True}
130	
131	    def __enter__(self) -> "GymClient":
132	        return self
133	
134	    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
135	        self.close()
136
