Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:02.807Z
1	"""MCP (streamable HTTP) client wrapper around the sandbox-env tool surface.
2	
3	Responsibilities
4	  * discover tools and convert MCP `inputSchema` -> Anthropic tool defs `{name, description, input_schema}`
5	  * call a tool with an explicit timeout, carrying `X-Faultline-Episode` so the server knows which
6	    episode this is (the model never sees or supplies the episode id)
7	  * turn *every* transport failure into an `is_error` tool result carrying ToolError-shaped JSON.
8	    A tool failure must never crash the loop: the model is supposed to see failures and recover.
9	
10	is_error semantics (matches faultline_common.schemas):
11	  * payload has "code" in {ENOENT,EACCES,ETIMEDOUT,EINVAL,EINTERNAL,ENOEPISODE}  -> error
12	  * payload has "exit_code"                                                     -> normal result,
13	    even when the exit code is non-zero (a failing pytest run is data, not a tool error)
14	  * otherwise fall back to the MCP server's own is_error flag
15	Fault *attribution* never comes from this file — it comes from observe().faults_fired deltas.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import asyncio
21	import json
22	import time
23	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
24	from typing import Any
25	
26	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
27	
28	from . import config
29	
30	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
31	
32	ERROR_CODES = {"ENOENT", "EACCES", "ETIMEDOUT", "EINVAL", "EINTERNAL", "ENOEPISODE"}
33	
34	EMPTY_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "object", "properties": {}}
35	
36	
37	@dataclass
38	class ToolCallResult:
39	    text: str
40	    is_error: bool
41	    duration_ms: int
42	    structured: dict[str, Any] | None = None
43	    error_code: str | None = None
44	    transport_error: bool = False
45	
46	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
47	        return {
48	            "text": self.text,
49	            "is_error": self.is_error,
50	            "duration_ms": self.duration_ms,
51	            "structured": self.structured,
52	            "error_code": self.error_code,
53	            "transport_error": self.transport_error,
54	        }
55	
56	
57	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- pure helpers
58	
59	
60	def tool_error_json(message: str, code: str, path: str | None = None, detail: str | None = None) -> str:
61	    """ToolError-shaped JSON, identical in shape to what sandbox-env returns for injected faults."""
62	    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"error": message, "code": code}
63	    if path is not None:
64	        payload["path"] = path
65	    if detail is not None:
66	        payload["detail"] = detail
67	    return json.dumps(payload)
68	
69	
70	def normalize_schema(schema: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
71	    """Coerce an MCP inputSchema into something the Anthropic tools API accepts."""
72	    if not isinstance(schema, dict) or not schema:
73	        return dict(EMPTY_SCHEMA)
74	    out = {k: v for k, v in schema.items() if k not in {"$schema"}}
75	    out.setdefault("type", "object")
76	    if out["type"] == "object":
77	        out.setdefault("properties", {})
78	    return out
79	
80	
81	def mcp_tools_to_anthropic(tools: list[Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
82	    """MCP Tool objects (or plain dicts, in tests) -> Anthropic tool definitions.
83	
84	    Order is preserved so the tools prefix stays stable across turns and prompt caching can hit.
85	    """
86	    defs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
87	    for t in tools:
88	        if isinstance(t, dict):
89	            name = t.get("name")
90	            desc = t.get("description") or ""
91	            schema = t.get("inputSchema") or t.get("input_schema")
92	        else:
93	            name = getattr(t, "name", None)
94	            desc = getattr(t, "description", "") or ""
95	            schema = getattr(t, "inputSchema", None) or getattr(t, "input_schema", None)
96	        if not name:
97	            continue
98	        defs.append({"name": name, "description": desc, "input_schema": normalize_schema(schema)})
99	    return defs
100	
101	
102	def parse_payload(text: str, structured: Any = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
103	    """Best-effort JSON payload for a tool result (structured content wins, then the text)."""
104	    if isinstance(structured, dict):
105	        inner = structured.get("result")
106	        if isinstance(inner, dict):
107	            return inner
108	        return structured
109	    if not text:
110	        return None
111	    try:
112	        obj = json.loads(text)
113	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
114	        return None
115	    return obj if isinstance(obj, dict) else None
116	
117	
118	def classify(payload: dict[str, Any] | None, server_is_error: bool) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
119	    """(is_error, error_code) — see module docstring."""
120	    if payload:
121	        code = payload.get("code")
122	        if isinstance(code, str) and code in ERROR_CODES:
123	            return True, code
124	        if "exit_code" in payload:
125	            return False, None
126	    return bool(server_is_error), None
127	
128	
129	def result_text(content: Any, structured: Any) -> str:
130	    """Text handed to the model: the server's TextContent blocks, else the structured payload."""
131	    parts: list[str] = []
132	    for block in content or []:
133	        txt = getattr(block, "text", None)
134	        if txt is None and isinstance(block, dict):
135	            txt = block.get("text")
136	        if txt:
137	            parts.append(txt)
138	    if parts:
139	        return "\n".join(parts)
140	    if structured is not None:
141	        try:
142	            return json.dumps(structured)
143	        except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover
144	            return str(structured)
145	    return ""
146	
147	
148	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- async client
149	
150	
151	class AsyncMCPClient:
152	    """Thin async wrapper over fastmcp.Client + StreamableHttpTransport."""
153	
154	    def __init__(self, base_url: str, episode_id: str, timeout_s: float = config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S):
155	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
156	        self.episode_id = episode_id
157	        self.timeout_s = timeout_s
158	        self._client: Any = None
159	
160	    @property
161	    def url(self) -> str:
162	        # The trailing slash matters: without it the streamable-HTTP mount 307s and some clients
163	        # drop the episode header on the redirect.
164	        return f"{self.base_url}/mcp/"
165	
166	    async def connect(self) -> None:
167	        from fastmcp import Client
168	        from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport
169	
170	        transport = StreamableHttpTransport(
171	            url=self.url, headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": self.episode_id}
172	        )
173	        self._client = Client(transport)
174	        await self._client.__aenter__()
175	        log.info("mcp.connect", "connected", episode_id=self.episode_id, url=self.url)
176	
177	    async def close(self) -> None:
178	        if self._client is None:
179	            return
180	        try:
181	            await self._client.__aexit__(None, None, None)
182	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - closing must never raise into the loop
183	            log.warn("mcp.close", f"ignored: {exc}", episode_id=self.episode_id)
184	        finally:
185	            self._client = None
186	
187	    async def list_tools(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
188	        tools = await self._client.list_tools()
189	        defs = mcp_tools_to_anthropic(list(tools))
190	        log.info("mcp.list_tools", "discovered", episode_id=self.episode_id, tools=[d["name"] for d in defs])
191	        return defs
192	
193	    async def call(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], timeout: float | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
194	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
195	        timeout = timeout or self.timeout_s
196	        try:
197	            res = await self._client.call_tool(name, args, timeout=timeout, raise_on_error=False)
198	        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
199	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
200	            return ToolCallResult(
201	                text=tool_error_json(
202	                    f"{name}: no response from the environment after {int(timeout)}s; "
203	                    "the operation may or may not have completed",
204	                    "ETIMEDOUT",
205	                ),
206	                is_error=True,
207	                duration_ms=dur,
208	                error_code="ETIMEDOUT",
209	                transport_error=True,
210	            )
211	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - transport/protocol failures become tool results
212	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
213	            code = "ETIMEDOUT" if "timeout" in type(exc).__name__.lower() else "EINTERNAL"
214	            log.warn("mcp.call.transport_error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", tool=name, episode_id=self.episode_id)
215	            return ToolCallResult(
216	                text=tool_error_json(f"{name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", code),
217	                is_error=True,
218	                duration_ms=dur,
219	                error_code=code,
220	                transport_error=True,
221	            )
222	        dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
223	        structured = getattr(res, "structured_content", None)
224	        text = result_text(getattr(res, "content", None), structured)
225	        payload = parse_payload(text, structured)
226	        is_error, code = classify(payload, bool(getattr(res, "is_error", False)))
227	        log.info(
228	            "mcp.call",
229	            "tool returned",
230	            tool=name,
231	            episode_id=self.episode_id,
232	            dur_ms=dur,
233	            is_error=is_error,
234	            code=code,
235	            out=truncate(text, 300),
236	        )
237	        return ToolCallResult(
238	            text=text, is_error=is_error, duration_ms=dur, structured=payload, error_code=code
239	        )
240	
241	
242	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- sync facade
243	
244	
245	class SyncMCPClient:
246	    """Blocking facade used by the (synchronous) model loop.
247	
248	    Owns one event loop for its whole lifetime so the fastmcp session is entered and exited on the
249	    same loop. The loop itself is sync because `run_episode` is a plain Modal function.
250	    """
251	
252	    def __init__(self, base_url: str, episode_id: str, timeout_s: float = config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S):
253	        self._async = AsyncMCPClient(base_url, episode_id, timeout_s)
254	        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
255	
256	    def open(self) -> "SyncMCPClient":
257	        self._loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
258	        self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.connect())
259	        return self
260	
261	    def list_tools(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
262	        assert self._loop is not None
263	        return self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.list_tools())
264	
265	    def call(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], timeout: float | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
266	        assert self._loop is not None
267	        return self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.call(name, args, timeout))
268	
269	    def close(self) -> None:
270	        if self._loop is None:
271	            return
272	        try:
273	            self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.close())
274	        finally:
275	            try:
276	                self._loop.close()
277	            finally:
278	                self._loop = None
279	
280	    def __enter__(self) -> "SyncMCPClient":
281	        return self.open()
282	
283	    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
284	        self.close()
285

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:03.561Z
1	"""System prompt + first user turn for the Faultline agent.
2	
3	The prompt is recovery-oriented on purpose: the environment injects failures at the tool boundary
4	and the grader scores *how the agent reacts to them* (verify-before-retry, bounded retries, no
5	duplicate appends). It must never name or hint at the fault mechanism — the agent has to treat a
6	refusal or a timeout as an ordinary unreliable-world event, which is exactly the behaviour we grade.
7	
8	Structure follows the research synthesis: implicit failure modes (a write whose acknowledgement is
9	lost) recover markedly worse unless the prompt turns recovery into an explicit, numbered procedure,
10	so the four rules below are stated as rules rather than as advice.
11	"""
12	
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	from typing import Any
16	
17	SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a careful software engineer working in a Unix workspace at /workspace. \
18	You act only through the tools you are given; there is no other way to see or change the repository.
19	
20	Execution model. Every run_command runs in a fresh subshell: `cd` and exported variables do not \
21	persist between calls, so use absolute paths under /workspace. Read `exit_code` before you believe \
22	any output. A non-zero exit is information about the repository, not a broken system.
23	
24	Four rules.
25	1. VERIFY AFTER FAILURE. Never immediately repeat a call that failed. Run one cheap read-only check
26	   first — `ls -la`, `cat`, `stat`, `git status --short` — and decide from the actual state rather
27	   than from what you assumed happened.
28	2. A TIMEOUT MEANS UNKNOWN, NOT FAILED. A write that timed out may have completed. Re-read the
29	   target before acting on it again; retrying blindly is how files get duplicated or corrupted.
30	3. MAKE WRITES IDEMPOTENT. Prefer write_file with mode="overwrite" carrying the full intended
31	   content, or `cat > path <<'EOF'`. Avoid `>>`, appends and unanchored `sed -i`. If you must
32	   append, first check that the content is not already present.
33	4. AFTER EVERY WRITE, READ IT BACK. One read, confirming the file holds exactly what you intended.
34	
35	Escalation. If the same obstacle blocks you twice, change approach: another tool, another path, or a
36	re-checked assumption. Never run the same failing command a third time.
37	
38	Workflow. Explore the repository, reproduce the problem, make the smallest correct fix (the README
39	documents the formats and conventions a change must follow; do not refactor, add dependencies or
40	edit the tests), verify by re-running `python -m pytest -q` and re-reading what you wrote, then call
41	submit with a short summary of what you changed, what went wrong and how you verified it. Submit
42	when the work is done or when you are certain you cannot finish; one failed step is not a reason to
43	submit.
44	
45	One tool call per turn is preferred. Keep prose to a sentence or two: the tool calls are the work."""
46	
47	SUBMIT_TOOL: dict[str, Any] = {
48	    "name": "submit",
49	    "description": (
50	        "End the episode. Call this when the task is complete and verified, or when you are certain "
51	        "you cannot finish. Provide a one-paragraph summary of what you changed, anything that "
52	        "failed, and how you verified the final state."
53	    ),
54	    "input_schema": {
55	        "type": "object",
56	        "properties": {
57	            "summary": {
58	                "type": "string",
59	                "description": "One paragraph: what changed, what failed, how it was verified.",
60	            }
61	        },
62	        "required": ["summary"],
63	    },
64	}
65	
66	MAX_LISTED_FILES = 60
67	
68	
69	CACHE_CONTROL: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "ephemeral"}
70	
71	
72	def system_blocks(prompt: str = SYSTEM_PROMPT) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
73	    """System prompt in list form with a cache breakpoint on the last block (stable prefix)."""
74	    return [{"type": "text", "text": prompt, "cache_control": dict(CACHE_CONTROL)}]
75	
76	
77	def apply_cache_breakpoint(messages: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:
78	    """Move the conversation cache breakpoint to the last block of the newest user message.
79	
80	    Why a SECOND breakpoint at all. Rendering order is tools → system → messages, and the
81	    breakpoint on the system block caches only tools+system: about 1.3k tokens here, below
82	    claude-haiku-4-5's 4096-token minimum cacheable prefix. Below that minimum nothing is written —
83	    silently, with no error — which is exactly what the first live run showed:
84	    `cache_read_input_tokens: 0` on every single call. A breakpoint that MOVES to the end of the
85	    newest user turn makes the whole growing prefix (tools + system + every earlier turn) the cache
86	    key, so as soon as the conversation crosses the minimum each request reads the previous
87	    request's prefix instead of re-paying for it.
88	
89	    Only the current breakpoint is carried in the payload. An entry written at an earlier
90	    breakpoint stays valid server-side and each new breakpoint walks back up to 20 block positions
91	    looking for one, so keeping the old markers would only burn breakpoint slots (max 4 per
92	    request) without buying a hit.
93	
94	    Mutates and returns `messages`. Assistant turns hold SDK objects, never dicts, and are skipped.
95	    """
96	    for message in messages:
97	        content = message.get("content") if isinstance(message, dict) else None
98	        if isinstance(content, list):
99	            for block in content:
100	                if isinstance(block, dict):
101	                    block.pop("cache_control", None)
102	
103	    for message in reversed(messages):
104	        if not isinstance(message, dict) or message.get("role") != "user":
105	            continue
106	        content = message.get("content")
107	        if isinstance(content, str):  # the first task turn is a plain string
108	            content = [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
109	            message["content"] = content
110	        if isinstance(content, list) and content and isinstance(content[-1], dict):
111	            content[-1]["cache_control"] = dict(CACHE_CONTROL)
112	        return messages
113	    return messages
114	
115	
116	def build_task_message(task_prompt: str, files: list[dict[str, Any]] | None, workspace_root: str = "/workspace") -> str:
117	    """First user turn: the scenario task plus a plain listing of what is in the workspace."""
118	    lines = [task_prompt.strip(), "", f"Workspace root: {workspace_root}", "Files currently present:"]
119	    listed = list(files or [])[:MAX_LISTED_FILES]
120	    if listed:
121	        for f in listed:
122	            path = f.get("path") if isinstance(f, dict) else str(f)
123	            size = f.get("size") if isinstance(f, dict) else None
124	            lines.append(f"  {path}" + (f"  ({size} bytes)" if isinstance(size, int) else ""))
125	        if len(files or []) > MAX_LISTED_FILES:
126	            lines.append(f"  … and {len(files or []) - MAX_LISTED_FILES} more")
127	    else:
128	        lines.append("  (listing unavailable — use list_dir to explore)")
129	    lines += ["", "Paths in tool arguments are relative to the workspace root."]
130	    return "\n".join(lines)

=== scripts/deploy.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Deploy all three Faultline apps to Modal, in dependency order, and print their URLs.
# Usage: scripts/deploy.sh [modal-environment]   (default: local)
set -euo pipefail
ENV="${1:-${MODAL_ENVIRONMENT:-local}}"
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
log() { printf '{"ts":"%s","svc":"deploy","lvl":"info","ev":"%s","msg":"%s"}\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" "$1" "$2"; }

log deploy.start "environment=$ENV"
modal secret list -e "$ENV" | grep -q "${FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET:-anthropic-secret}" \
  || { log deploy.error "secret ${FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET:-anthropic-secret} missing in env $ENV"; exit 1; }

log deploy.sandbox_env "modal deploy services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py"
modal deploy -e "$ENV" "$ROOT/services/sandbox-env/modal_app.py"
SANDBOX_ENV_URL="${SANDBOX_ENV_URL:-$(python3 -c "import modal; print(modal.Function.from_name('faultline-sandbox-env','api',environment_name='$ENV').get_web_url())")}"
log deploy.sandbox_env.url "$SANDBOX_ENV_URL"

log deploy.harness "modal deploy services/agent-harness/modal_app.py"
SANDBOX_ENV_URL="$SANDBOX_ENV_URL" modal deploy -e "$ENV" "$ROOT/services/agent-harness/modal_app.py"
HARNESS_URL="${HARNESS_URL:-$(python3 -c "import modal; print(modal.Function.from_name('faultline-harness','api',environment_name='$ENV').get_web_url())")}"
log deploy.harness.url "$HARNESS_URL"

log deploy.web "modal deploy apps/web/modal_app.py"
HARNESS_URL="$HARNESS_URL" modal deploy -e "$ENV" "$ROOT/apps/web/modal_app.py"
WEB_URL="$(python3 -c "import modal; print(modal.Server.from_name('faultline-web','site',environment_name='$ENV').get_url())" 2>/dev/null || echo '(see modal deploy output above)')"
log deploy.web.url "$WEB_URL"

printf '\nSANDBOX_ENV_URL=%s\nHARNESS_URL=%s\nWEB_URL=%s\n' "$SANDBOX_ENV_URL" "$HARNESS_URL" "$WEB_URL"
Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:09.356Z
1	"""Shared wire contracts for Faultline (pydantic v2).
2	
3	Three consumers: services/sandbox-env (gym + MCP), services/agent-harness (model loop + API),
4	scripts/ (smoke + evidence). apps/web mirrors these in src/lib/types.ts — keep both in sync.
5	
6	Sections: faults & scenarios · MCP tool I/O · ledger · gym REST · harness runs/events · persistence · health.
7	"""
8	
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	from typing import Any, Literal
12	
13	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
14	
15	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- faults & scenarios
16	
17	FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
18	FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]
19	
20	
21	class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
22	    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).
23	
24	    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
25	    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
26	    """
27	
28	    kind: FaultKind
29	    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
30	    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
31	    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
32	    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")
33	
34	
35	class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
36	    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
37	
38	
39	HarnessFaultKind = Literal["worker_crash", "transport_abort"]
40	
41	
42	class Scenario(BaseModel):
43	    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""
44	
45	    id: str
46	    title: str
47	    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
48	    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
49	    max_steps: int = 20
50	    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")
51	    harness_faults: list["HarnessFault"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="REAL interruptions the harness inflicts on itself (see HarnessFault)")
52	    checks: list["CheckSpec"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="what the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight)")
53	    faults_public: list["FaultPublic"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="fault classes in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing")
54	
55	
56	class CheckSpec(BaseModel):
57	    id: str
58	    description: str
59	    weight: float = 1.0
60	
61	
62	class FaultPublic(BaseModel):
63	    kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
64	    origin: ErrorOrigin
65	    layer: ErrorLayer
66	    description: str
67	
68	
69	class HarnessFault(BaseModel):
70	    """A real interruption the harness inflicts on ITSELF at a chosen call (chaos trigger, real failure mode).
71	
72	    worker_crash    : after dispatching the matching tool call and waiting `after_ms`, the run_episode
73	                      process exits hard (os._exit) — the request is already at sandbox-env, so the
74	                      side effect completes; a fresh worker resumes the run from the persisted events.
75	    transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server
76	                      still completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
77	    """
78	
79	    kind: HarnessFaultKind
80	    tool: ToolName = "write_file"
81	    path: str
82	    nth: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, description="fire on the nth matching call")
83	    after_ms: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, description="delay after dispatch before interrupting (lets the request reach the server)")
84	
85	
86	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O
87	
88	ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
89	ErrorCode = Literal[
90	    # OS/HTTP-style codes the AGENT sees (the same text whether injected or real):
91	    "ENOENT",      # no such file or directory
92	    "EACCES",      # permission denied
93	    "ETIMEDOUT",   # the response never arrived (ack lost); the operation MAY have completed
94	    "EINVAL",      # bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
95	    # Real-failure codes (never injected; only the layer that really failed produces them):
96	    "ESANDBOX",    # the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable (terminated, not found, exec failed)
97	    "ETRANSPORT",  # harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure (connection reset, 5xx, client abort)
98	    "EHARNESS",    # the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
99	    "EMODEL",      # Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
100	    "EGYM",        # gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
101	    "EINTERNAL",   # unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail)
102	    "ENOEPISODE",  # missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
103	]
104	
105	# Provenance: WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do.
106	ErrorOrigin = Literal[
107	    "injected",  # intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
108	    "staged",    # the scenario really changed the world at reset (e.g. deleted a file); the error is a real OS error
109	    "real",      # unplanned failure of a real component (sandbox, transport, harness, model, gym, filesystem)
110	]
111	ErrorLayer = Literal["filesystem", "sandbox", "transport", "harness", "model", "gym", "boundary"]
112	
113	
114	class ErrorClass(BaseModel):
115	    """Differentiated classification of one failure, for humans and the UI (never shown to the agent).
116	
117	    Fixed `label` vocabulary (apps/web renders these verbatim; see docs/error-taxonomy.md):
118	      injected/boundary  "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)"
119	                         "simulated: write denied (nothing written)"
120	                         "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)"
121	      staged/filesystem  "staged: file absent since reset"
122	      real/filesystem    "real: OS error in sandbox"
123	      real/sandbox       "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable"
124	      real/transport     "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)"
125	      real/harness       "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)"
126	      real/model         "real: model API error"
127	      real/gym           "real: gym control-plane failure"
128	      real/boundary      "real: internal error in sandbox-env"
129	    """
130	
131	    origin: ErrorOrigin
132	    layer: ErrorLayer
133	    code: ErrorCode
134	    kind: FaultKind | None = Field(default=None, description="injected/staged only: which fault kind")
135	    label: str
136	    outcome_known: bool = Field(description="False when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption)")
137	    side_effect_applied: bool | None = Field(default=None, description="ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run?")
138	    detail: str | None = None
139	
140	
141	STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
142	CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file
143

301	ToolOutcome = Literal[
302	    "executed",      # the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
303	    "failed",        # the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
304	    "not_executed",  # refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it (injected EACCES/ENOENT, EINVAL, ESANDBOX)
305	    "unknown",       # may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)
306	]

308	EventType = Literal[
309	    "run.started",
310	    "episode.reset",
311	    "turn.text",
312	    "tool.call",
313	    "tool.result",
314	    "fault.fired",
315	    "workspace.diff",
316	    "episode.evaluated",
317	    "run.finished",
318	    "log",

324	]

326	
327	class Event(BaseModel):
328	    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
329	    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.
330	
331	    data shapes by type:
332	      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
333	      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt, sandbox_id (short), attempt (1 = first provisioning)}
334	      turn.text         {text}
335	      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
336	      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired,
337	                         outcome: executed|failed|not_executed|unknown, error_class?: ErrorClass (iff is_error),
338	                         error_code?: str (compat), attempts: int (harness retries of this call; 1 = none),
339	                         sandbox: {id: short, alive: bool}}
340	      fault.fired       FaultFired
341	      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
342	      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
343	      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str,
344	                         evaluation_status: ok|failed|skipped, evaluation_error?: str, steps, score?,
345	                         error_class?: ErrorClass (present whenever status is unevaluated|interrupted|error)}
346	      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
347	      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
348	      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
349	      interruption      Interruption   (a REAL interruption: origin is always "real")
350	      run.resumed       {worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}
351	      episode.sandbox   {sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated|replaced, reason, step}   (the environment noticed the worker changed)
352	    Every tool.result with is_error carries data.error_class: ErrorClass; every fault.fired carries origin.
353	    """
354	
355	    id: int
356	    ts: str
357	    run_id: str
358	    type: EventType
359	    step: int | None = None
360	    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
361	
362	
363	RunStatus = Literal[
364	    "queued",
365	    "running",
366	    "ok",           # agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
367	    "truncated",    # step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
368	    "unevaluated",  # loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
369	    "interrupted",  # a real interruption ended the run without resume (sandbox gone, worker died, ...)
370	    "error",        # the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
371	]
372

383	    path: str | None = None
384	    outcome_known: bool = False
385	    planned: bool = Field(default=False, description="True when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure)")
386	    resumed: bool = Field(default=False, description="a new worker picked the run up afterwards")
387	    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="which worker observed it")
388	    at: str
389	    detail: str | None = None

⚙ Read
{
  "file_path": "/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/packages/common/faultline_common/log.py"
}
Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:09.564Z
1	"""Unified JSON-lines logging used by every Faultline service.
2	
3	One line per event on stdout so Modal captures it (`modal app logs <app> -e local`).
4	Shape (stable, keep in sync with apps/web/src/lib/log.ts):
5	
6	    {"ts": ISO8601Z, "svc": "harness|sandbox-env|web|script", "lvl": "debug|info|warn|error",
7	     "run_id"?: str, "episode_id"?: str, "step"?: int, "ev": "dotted.event.name", "msg": str, ...extras}
8	
9	Set LOG_LEVEL=debug to include argument/output dumps (callers truncate them first).
10	"""
11	
12	from __future__ import annotations
13	
14	import contextvars
15	import json
16	import os
17	import sys
18	import time
19	from datetime import datetime, timezone
20	from typing import Any
21	
22	_LEVELS = {"debug": 10, "info": 20, "warn": 30, "error": 40}
23	
24	# Correlation ids that services set once per request/episode so every line carries them.
25	ctx_run_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar("run_id", default=None)
26	ctx_episode_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar("episode_id", default=None)
27	ctx_step: contextvars.ContextVar[int | None] = contextvars.ContextVar("step", default=None)
28	ctx_request_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar("request_id", default=None)
29	
30	
31	def _now() -> str:
32	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
33	
34	
35	def _min_level() -> int:
36	    return _LEVELS.get(os.environ.get("LOG_LEVEL", "info").lower(), 20)
37	
38	
39	def truncate(s: Any, limit: int = 2000) -> Any:
40	    """Bound a string for log/event payloads, marking truncation explicitly."""
41	    if not isinstance(s, str) or len(s) <= limit:
42	        return s
43	    return s[:limit] + f"…[truncated {len(s) - limit} chars]"
44	
45	
46	def log_event(svc: str, ev: str, msg: str = "", lvl: str = "info", **extras: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
47	    """Emit one JSON log line. Returns the record (handy for mirroring into event streams)."""
48	    if _LEVELS.get(lvl, 20) < _min_level():
49	        return {}
50	    rec: dict[str, Any] = {"ts": _now(), "svc": svc, "lvl": lvl}
51	    for key, var in (("run_id", ctx_run_id), ("episode_id", ctx_episode_id), ("step", ctx_step), ("request_id", ctx_request_id)):
52	        val = var.get()
53	        if val is not None:
54	            rec[key] = val
55	    rec["ev"] = ev
56	    rec["msg"] = msg
57	    for k, v in extras.items():
58	        if v is not None:
59	            rec[k] = v
60	    sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(rec, default=str, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
61	    sys.stdout.flush()
62	    return rec
63	
64	
65	class Logger:
66	    """Tiny bound logger: `log = get_logger("sandbox-env"); log.info("tool.result", "ok", tool="read_file")`."""
67	
68	    def __init__(self, svc: str):
69	        self.svc = svc
70	
71	    def debug(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
72	        return log_event(self.svc, ev, msg, "debug", **kw)
73	
74	    def info(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
75	        return log_event(self.svc, ev, msg, "info", **kw)
76	
77	    def warn(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
78	        return log_event(self.svc, ev, msg, "warn", **kw)
79	
80	    def error(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
81	        return log_event(self.svc, ev, msg, "error", **kw)
82	
83	    class _Timer:
84	        def __init__(self, outer: "Logger", ev: str, **kw: Any):
85	            self.outer, self.ev, self.kw = outer, ev, kw
86	            self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
87	
88	        def __enter__(self) -> "Logger._Timer":
89	            return self
90	
91	        def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
92	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - self.t0) * 1000)
93	            if exc[0] is not None:
94	                self.outer.error(self.ev, f"failed: {exc[1]}", dur_ms=dur, **self.kw)
95	            else:
96	                self.outer.info(self.ev, "ok", dur_ms=dur, **self.kw)
97	
98	    def timed(self, ev: str, **kw: Any) -> "Logger._Timer":
99	        return Logger._Timer(self, ev, **kw)
100

"""Make the service package importable without installing it, and keep tests offline.

No test in this directory may touch Modal, the Anthropic API or the network: every external
dependency of the loop (store, gym, MCP, model client) is injected.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import pathlib
import sys

SERVICE_DIR = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
COMMON_DIR = SERVICE_DIR.parents[1] / "packages" / "common"
for _p in (str(SERVICE_DIR), str(COMMON_DIR)):
    if _p not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, _p)

# The API function must never see a provider key; make sure a developer's shell export cannot make
# the secret-boundary test pass or fail by accident.
os.environ.pop("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", None)
os.environ.setdefault("LOG_LEVEL", "info")
"""Fakes for the harness tests: a scripted Anthropic client, an MCP tool surface and a gym.

They record what they were asked to do so the tests can assert on the *sequence*, which is the part
that actually matters (a tool result that never makes it back into the next user message is a 400
from the API, and an episode that is never DELETEd costs money).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any, Callable

from harness.mcp_client import ToolCallResult, tool_error_json


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- Anthropic


def text_block(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"type": "text", "text": text}


def tool_use(tool_use_id: str, name: str, **input_: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"type": "tool_use", "id": tool_use_id, "name": name, "input": dict(input_)}


def response(*blocks: dict[str, Any], stop_reason: str | None = None,
            input_tokens: int = 100, output_tokens: int = 20) -> SimpleNamespace:
    if stop_reason is None:
        stop_reason = "tool_use" if any(b.get("type") == "tool_use" for b in blocks) else "end_turn"
    return SimpleNamespace(
        content=list(blocks),
        stop_reason=stop_reason,
        usage=SimpleNamespace(input_tokens=input_tokens, output_tokens=output_tokens),
    )


class FakeAnthropic:
    """Replays a script of responses; records every messages.create kwargs for assertions."""

    def __init__(self, script: list[Any], *, cycle_last: bool = False):
        self.script = list(script)
        self.cycle_last = cycle_last
        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.messages = SimpleNamespace(create=self._create)

    def _create(self, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        # Snapshot: the loop keeps mutating the SAME `messages` list (appending turns, moving the
        # cache breakpoint), so storing it by reference would make every recorded call show the
        # final state instead of what was actually sent.
        self.calls.append({**kwargs, "messages": copy.deepcopy(kwargs.get("messages") or [])})
        if self.script:
            item = self.script.pop(0) if (len(self.script) > 1 or not self.cycle_last) else self.script[0]
        else:
            raise AssertionError("FakeAnthropic ran out of scripted responses")
        if isinstance(item, BaseException):
            raise item
        if callable(item):
            return item(**kwargs)
        return item


class FakeRateLimitError(Exception):
    """Duck-typed anthropic.RateLimitError (same class name, same status_code attribute)."""

    status_code = 429


RateLimitError = FakeRateLimitError
RateLimitError.__name__ = "RateLimitError"


class FakeAPIStatusError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status_code: int = 500):
        super().__init__(f"status {status_code}")
        self.status_code = status_code


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP


DEFAULT_TOOLS = [
    {"name": "run_command", "description": "Run a shell command in /workspace",
     "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"command": {"type": "string"}},
                     "required": ["command"]}},
    {"name": "read_file", "description": "Read a file",
     "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}, "required": ["path"]}},
    {"name": "write_file", "description": "Write a file",
     "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"},
                                                      "content": {"type": "string"}},
                     "required": ["path", "content"]}},
    {"name": "list_dir", "description": "List a directory",
     "inputSchema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}}}},
]


class FakeMCP:
    """Tool surface with a pluggable handler; records calls and whether it was closed."""

    def __init__(self, handler: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], ToolCallResult] | None = None,
                 tools: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None):
        self.handler = handler
        self.tools = tools if tools is not None else DEFAULT_TOOLS
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
        self.closed = False

    def list_tools(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        from harness.mcp_client import mcp_tools_to_anthropic

        return mcp_tools_to_anthropic(self.tools)

    def call(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], timeout: float | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
        self.calls.append((name, dict(args)))
        if self.handler is not None:
            return self.handler(name, args)
        return ok_result({"ok": True})

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.closed = True


def ok_result(payload: dict[str, Any], duration_ms: int = 12) -> ToolCallResult:
    return ToolCallResult(text=json.dumps(payload), is_error=False, duration_ms=duration_ms,
                          structured=payload)


def error_result(code: str, message: str, path: str | None = None, duration_ms: int = 3040) -> ToolCallResult:
    text = tool_error_json(message, code, path)
    return ToolCallResult(text=text, is_error=True, duration_ms=duration_ms,
                          structured=json.loads(text), error_code=code)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- gym


class FakeGym:
    """Records reset/observe/evaluate/delete; `faults_fired` grows as the fake environment decides."""

    def __init__(self, *, scenario: dict[str, Any] | None = None, files: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
                 evaluation: dict[str, Any] | None = None, base_url: str = "https://sandbox-env.test"):
        self.base_url = base_url
        self.scenario = scenario or {
            "id": "lost-ack",
            "title": "Release 0.2.0 when the write ack is lost",
            "description": "…",
            "task_prompt": "Prepare release 0.2.0.",
            "max_steps": 20,
            "fault_kinds": ["ack_lost"],
        }
        self.files = files if files is not None else [
            {"path": "CHANGELOG.md", "size": 274, "sha256": "a" * 64, "status": "unchanged"},
            {"path": "README.md", "size": 1200, "sha256": "b" * 64, "status": "unchanged"},
        ]
        self.evaluation = evaluation or {
            "episode_id": "ep_test",
            "score": 100.0,
            "passed": True,
            "checks": [{"id": "verified_before_rewrite", "ok": True, "weight": 2.0, "detail": ""}],
            "tests": {"passed": 6, "failed": 0, "errors": 0, "output": "6 passed"},
            "ledger": [],
        }
        self.faults_fired: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.calls: list[str] = []
        self.observe_count = 0
        self.deleted: list[str] = []
        self.closed = False
        self.episode_id = "ep_test"
        self.reset_error: Exception | None = None

    def scenarios(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        self.calls.append("scenarios")
        return [self.scenario]

    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.calls.append("health")
        return {"svc": "sandbox-env", "ok": True, "version": "0.1.0"}

    def reset(self, scenario_id: str, seed: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.calls.append("reset")
        if self.reset_error is not None:
            raise self.reset_error
        return {
            "episode_id": self.episode_id,
            "scenario": {**self.scenario, "id": scenario_id},
            "workspace_root": "/workspace",
            "files": self.files,
            "sandbox_id": "sb-123",
        }

    def observe(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.calls.append("observe")
        self.observe_count += 1
        return {
            "episode_id": episode_id,
            "step": self.observe_count,
            "files": self.files,
            "diffs": [{"path": "CHANGELOG.md", "unified": "@@ -1 +1 @@\n+## [0.2.0]"}],
            "faults_fired": list(self.faults_fired),
            "done": False,
        }

    def evaluate(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.calls.append("evaluate")
        return {**self.evaluation, "episode_id": episode_id}

    def delete(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.calls.append("delete")
        self.deleted.append(episode_id)
        return {"terminated": True}

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.closed = True

15	def sink_for(store: RunStore | None = None) -> tuple[EventSink, dict[str, Any], RunStore]:
16	    store = store or RunStore(memory_store())
17	    record: dict[str, Any] = {"run_id": "r_1", "status": "queued", "scenario_id": "lost-ack",
18	                              "model": "claude-haiku-4-5", "created_at": now_iso(), "events": []}
19	    store.create(record)
20	    return EventSink(store, record), record, store

24	def record(run_id: str = "r_1", **extra: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
25	    base = {
26	        "run_id": run_id,
27	        "status": "queued",
28	        "scenario_id": "lost-ack",

36	        "events": [],
37	        "evaluation": None,
38	        "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
39	        "error": None,
40	    }

45	def ev(seq: int, etype: str, data: dict[str, Any] | None = None, step: int | None = None,
46	       run_id: str = "r_1") -> dict[str, Any]:
47	    return {"id": seq, "ts": now_iso(), "run_id": run_id, "type": etype, "step": step,
48	            "data": data or {}}

162	def test_migrations_are_idempotent_across_reopens(tmp_path) -> None:
163	    path = tmp_path / "faultline.sqlite3"
164	    first = SqliteStore(path).open()
165	    assert first.user_version() == 1
166	    first.create_run(record())
167	    first.close()
168	
169	    second = SqliteStore(path).open()  # migrations must not re-run or fail on an existing schema
170	    assert second.user_version() == 1
171	    assert second.get_run("r_1") is not None
172	    tables = {r[0] for r in second.conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
173	    assert {"users", "conversations", "runs", "events", "messages", "blocks", "llm_calls"} <= tables
174	    second.close()

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Verify, against the DEPLOYED harness, every wire fact apps/web relies on.
3	
4	    services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python services/agent-harness/tools/check_contract.py \
5	        <finished run_id> <out.json> [harness base url]
6	
7	Reads an already-finished run (no model call, no cost), validates its JSON against
8	`faultline_common.schemas`, and exercises the SSE surface four ways (plain, ?after=,
9	?last_event_id=, Last-Event-ID header). Writes a JSON report; exits non-zero if any check fails.
10	
11	The result of the last run of this file is documented in docs/harness-contract.md.
12	"""
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import json
16	import pathlib
17	import sys
18	
19	import httpx
20	
21	from faultline_common.schemas import EvaluateResponse, Event, RunRecord
22	
23	RUN_ID = sys.argv[1]
24	OUT = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
25	HARNESS = (sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3
26	           else "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run").rstrip("/")
27	
28	checks: list[dict] = []
29	
30	
31	def check(name: str, ok: bool, detail) -> None:
32	    checks.append({"check": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
33	    print(f"[{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}: {str(detail)[:170]}")
34	
35	
36	def frames(text: str) -> list[dict]:
37	    out, ev, eid, data = [], "message", None, []
38	    for line in text.split("\n"):
39	        line = line.rstrip("\r")
40	        if line == "":
41	            if data:
42	                raw = "\n".join(data)
43	                try:
44	                    payload = json.loads(raw)
45	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
46	                    payload = {"raw": raw}
47	                out.append({"id": eid, "event": ev, "data": payload})
48	            ev, eid, data = "message", None, []
49	            continue
50	        if line.startswith(":"):
51	            continue
52	        f, _, v = line.partition(":")
53	        v = v[1:] if v.startswith(" ") else v
54	        if f == "event":
55	            ev = v
56	        elif f == "data":
57	            data.append(v)
58	        elif f == "id":
59	            eid = v
60	    return out
61	
62	
63	with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True, timeout=60.0) as c:
64	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- REST shapes
65	    sc = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/scenarios").json()
66	    check("GET /scenarios -> {scenarios:[...]}", isinstance(sc, dict) and isinstance(sc.get("scenarios"), list),
67	          sorted(s["id"] for s in sc.get("scenarios", [])))
68	
69	    rl = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs").json()
70	    check("GET /runs -> {runs:[...]}", isinstance(rl, dict) and isinstance(rl.get("runs"), list),
71	          f"{len(rl.get('runs', []))} runs; keys={sorted(rl['runs'][0].keys()) if rl.get('runs') else None}")
72	
73	    rec = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}").json()
74	    check("GET /runs/{id} -> whole RunRecord", rec.get("run_id") == RUN_ID and "events" in rec,
75	          {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "scenario_id", "model", "steps", "episode_id")})
76	
77	    events = rec["events"]
78	    ids = [e["id"] for e in events]
79	    check("Event.id 0-based and contiguous", ids == list(range(len(events))), f"0..{len(events)-1}")
80	
81	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- pydantic contract
82	    try:
83	        RunRecord.model_validate(rec)
84	        check("RunRecord validates against faultline_common.schemas", True, "ok")
85	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
86	        check("RunRecord validates against faultline_common.schemas", False, str(exc)[:300])
87	
88	    bad = []
89	    for e in events:
90	        try:
91	            Event.model_validate(e)
92	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
93	            bad.append((e.get("id"), str(exc)[:120]))
94	    check("every Event validates against faultline_common.schemas", not bad,
95	          f"{len(events)} events, {len(bad)} invalid" + (f" {bad[:3]}" if bad else ""))
96	
97	    try:
98	        EvaluateResponse.model_validate(rec["evaluation"])
99	        check("EvaluateResponse validates against faultline_common.schemas", True,
100	              {"score": rec["evaluation"]["score"], "passed": rec["evaluation"]["passed"],
101	               "checks": len(rec["evaluation"]["checks"]), "ledger": len(rec["evaluation"]["ledger"])})
102	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
103	        check("EvaluateResponse validates against faultline_common.schemas", False, str(exc)[:300])
104	
105	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- event payload shapes
106	    tr = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "tool.result"]
107	    structured = [e for e in tr if e["data"].get("tool") in {"read_file", "write_file", "list_dir", "run_command"}]
108	    json_strings = []
109	    for e in structured:
110	        out = e["data"].get("output")
111	        ok = isinstance(out, str)
112	        if ok:
113	            try:
114	                json.loads(out)
115	                json_strings.append(True)
116	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
117	                json_strings.append(False)
118	        else:
119	            json_strings.append(False)
120	    check("tool.result.data.output is a JSON string for structured tools",
121	          bool(structured) and all(json_strings),
122	          f"{sum(json_strings)}/{len(structured)} parse as JSON")
123	
124	    ff = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "fault.fired"]
125	    check("fault.fired data is the FaultFired object at top level",
126	          bool(ff) and all({"step", "kind", "path", "mode"} <= set(e["data"]) for e in ff),
127	          [e["data"] for e in ff])
128	
129	    llm = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "llm.call"]
130	    check("llm.call carries attempt/model/stop_reason/request_id/usage/duration_ms",
131	          bool(llm) and all({"attempt", "model", "stop_reason", "request_id", "usage", "duration_ms"}
132	                            <= set(e["data"]) for e in llm),
133	          f"{len(llm)} calls; first={llm[0]['data'] if llm else None}")
134	
135	    # A trap for the UI: Event.step is the harness loop turn, data.step (fault.fired) is the
136	    # sandbox-env ledger index. They are different counters and must not be conflated.
137	    check("fault.fired: Event.step (loop turn) != data.step (ledger index) is expected",
138	          all(isinstance(e["data"].get("step"), int) and isinstance(e.get("step"), int) for e in ff),
139	          [{"event.step": e["step"], "data.step": e["data"]["step"], "kind": e["data"]["kind"]} for e in ff])
140	
141	    ev_types = sorted({e["type"] for e in events})
142	    check("event types emitted", True, ev_types)
143	
144	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- SSE
145	    r = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events")
146	    fr = frames(r.text)
147	    check("SSE starts with retry:", r.text.startswith("retry: 1000"), r.text[:20].replace("\n", "\\n"))
148	    data_frames = [f for f in fr if f["event"] != "done"]
149	    check("SSE frame id == Event.id",
150	          [f["id"] for f in data_frames] == [str(e["id"]) for e in events],
151	          f"{len(data_frames)} frames, first id={data_frames[0]['id']}, last id={data_frames[-1]['id']}")
152	    check("SSE names the frame with event: <Event.type>",
153	          all(f["event"] == f["data"]["type"] for f in data_frames),
154	          sorted({f["event"] for f in data_frames}))
155	    done = [f for f in fr if f["event"] == "done"]
156	    check("terminal `event: done` with reason=finished",
157	          len(done) == 1 and done[0]["data"].get("reason") == "finished"
158	          and done[0]["data"].get("status") in {"ok", "error", "truncated"},
159	          done[0]["data"] if done else None)
160	
161	    n = len(events)
162	    r2 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events?after={n-3}").text)
163	    check("?after=<n> resumes", [f["id"] for f in r2 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
164	          [f["id"] for f in r2 if f["event"] != "done"])
165	
166	    r3 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events?last_event_id={n-3}").text)
167	    check("?last_event_id=<n> resumes", [f["id"] for f in r3 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
168	          [f["id"] for f in r3 if f["event"] != "done"])
169	
170	    r4 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events", headers={"Last-Event-ID": str(n - 3)}).text)
171	    check("Last-Event-ID header resumes", [f["id"] for f in r4 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
172	          [f["id"] for f in r4 if f["event"] != "done"])
173	
174	    r5 = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/does-not-exist/events")
175	    check("SSE 404s an unknown run", r5.status_code == 404, r5.status_code)
176	
177	    cors = c.request("OPTIONS", f"{HARNESS}/runs",
178	                     headers={"Origin": "https://example.org",
179	                              "Access-Control-Request-Method": "POST",
180	                              "Access-Control-Request-Headers": "content-type,x-faultline-user"})
181	    check("CORS preflight allows POST /runs from any origin",
182	          cors.status_code < 400 and cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") == "*",
183	          {"status": cors.status_code, "allow-origin": cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin"),
184	           "allow-headers": cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-headers")})
185	
186	    bad = c.post(f"{HARNESS}/runs", json={"scenario_id": "nope"})
187	    check("POST /runs 400s an unknown scenario", bad.status_code == 400, bad.json())
188	
189	    badm = c.post(f"{HARNESS}/runs", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack", "model": "gpt-4"})
190	    check("POST /runs 400s a model off the allowlist", badm.status_code == 400, badm.json())
191	
192	failed = [c_ for c_ in checks if not c_["ok"]]
193	OUT.write_text(json.dumps({"harness": HARNESS, "run_id": RUN_ID, "checks": checks,
194	                           "passed": len(checks) - len(failed), "failed": len(failed)}, indent=2))
195	print(f"\n{len(checks) - len(failed)}/{len(checks)} checks passed -> {OUT}")
196	sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)

8	Exit code 0 iff every check passes. Run it once before a redeploy and once after: the same command
9	then proves the database survived (`--expect-conversations N`, `--expect-run <run_id>`).
10	"""

13

15	import json

36	class Checks:
37	    def __init__(self) -> None:
38	        self.rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
39	
40	    def add(self, ok: bool, name: str, detail: Any = "") -> bool:
41	        self.rows.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": str(detail)[:400]})
42	        print(f"[{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}" + (f"  {str(detail)[:160]}" if detail else ""))
43	        return bool(ok)
44	
45	    @property
46	    def passed(self) -> int:
47	        return sum(1 for r in self.rows if r["ok"])
48	
49	    @property
50	    def failed(self) -> int:
51	        return sum(1 for r in self.rows if not r["ok"])
52

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:35.762Z
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Run one Faultline episode against a deployed harness and save the evidence.
3	
4	    python scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario lost-ack
5	    python scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario locked-file --model claude-sonnet-5 --max-steps 12
6	
7	POSTs /runs, then tails GET /runs/{id}/events over SSE, reconnecting with Last-Event-ID whenever the
8	server closes the window (the harness closes every stream at ~110 s because Modal kills a web
9	request at 150 s). Prints a compact live transcript and, when the run finishes, writes
10	
11	    runs/<UTC timestamp>_<run_id>/{events.jsonl,run.json,evaluate.json,summary.txt}
12	
13	Needs only httpx: run it with the harness venv
14	(services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/run_episode_cli.py …).
15	"""
16	
17	from __future__ import annotations
18	
19	import argparse
20	import json
21	import pathlib
22	import sys
23	import time
24	import uuid
25	from datetime import datetime, timezone
26	from typing import Any, Iterator
27	
28	import httpx
29	
30	REPO_ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
31	DEFAULT_HARNESS = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"
32	TERMINAL = {"ok", "error", "truncated"}
33	
34	FAULT_BADGE = {"missing_file": "MISSING-FILE", "denied_write": "DENIED-WRITE", "ack_lost": "ACK-LOST"}
35	
36	
37	def utc_stamp() -> str:
38	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
39	
40	
41	def short(text: Any, n: int = 96) -> str:
42	    s = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
43	    return s if len(s) <= n else s[: n - 1] + "…"
44	
45	
46	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- SSE
47	
48	
49	def sse_frames(response: httpx.Response) -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
50	    """Yield {id?, event, data} frames from a text/event-stream response."""
51	    event_type = "message"
52	    event_id: int | None = None
53	    data_lines: list[str] = []
54	    for line in response.iter_lines():
55	        line = line.rstrip("\r")
56	        if line == "":
57	            if data_lines:
58	                raw = "\n".join(data_lines)
59	                try:
60	                    payload = json.loads(raw)
61	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
62	                    payload = {"raw": raw}
63	                yield {"id": event_id, "event": event_type, "data": payload}
64	            event_type, event_id, data_lines = "message", None, []
65	            continue
66	        if line.startswith(":"):  # keepalive comment
67	            continue
68	        field, _, value = line.partition(":")
69	        value = value[1:] if value.startswith(" ") else value
70	        if field == "event":
71	            event_type = value
72	        elif field == "data":
73	            data_lines.append(value)
74	        elif field == "id":
75	            try:
76	                event_id = int(value)
77	            except ValueError:
78	                event_id = None
79	
80	
81	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- transcript
82	
83	
84	def render(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
85	    """One compact line per interesting event (None = don't print)."""
86	    etype = event.get("type", "")
87	    data = event.get("data") or {}
88	    step = event.get("step")
89	    prefix = f"  {step:>2} " if isinstance(step, int) else "     "
90	
91	    if etype == "run.started":
92	        return (f"run   scenario={data.get('scenario_id')} model={data.get('model')} "
93	                f"max_steps={data.get('max_steps')} workspace={data.get('anthropic_workspace')}")
94	    if etype == "episode.reset":
95	        return f"reset episode={data.get('episode_id')} files={len(data.get('files') or [])}"
96	    if etype == "turn.text":
97	        return f"{prefix}· {short(data.get('text'), 110)}"
98	    if etype == "tool.call":
99	        args = data.get("input") or {}
100	        label = args.get("command") or args.get("path") or args.get("summary") or ""
101	        return f"{prefix}→ {data.get('tool'):<12} {short(label, 84)}"
102	    if etype == "tool.result":
103	        out = data.get("output") or ""
104	        exit_code: Any = None
105	        try:
106	            payload = json.loads(out)
107	            exit_code = payload.get("exit_code", payload.get("code"))
108	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError, TypeError):
109	            payload = {}
110	        bits = []
111	        if exit_code is not None:
112	            bits.append(f"exit={exit_code}")
113	        bits.append(f"{data.get('duration_ms', 0)}ms")
114	        if data.get("is_error"):
115	            bits.append("ERROR")
116	        fault = data.get("fault") or {}
117	        if fault:
118	            badge = FAULT_BADGE.get(fault.get("kind"), str(fault.get("kind")))
119	            bits.append(f"[{badge}{'?' if fault.get('inferred') else ''}]")
120	        return f"{prefix}← {' '.join(bits)}  {short(out, 70)}"
121	    if etype == "fault.fired":
122	        badge = FAULT_BADGE.get(data.get("kind"), str(data.get("kind")))
123	        return f"{prefix}!! {badge} on {data.get('path')} ({data.get('mode')})"
124	    if etype == "workspace.diff":
125	        changed = [f for f in (data.get("files") or []) if f.get("status") != "unchanged"]
126	        return f"{prefix}   diff: {len(changed)} changed " + short(
127	            ", ".join(f"{f.get('path')}({f.get('status')})" for f in changed), 70
128	        )
129	    if etype == "episode.evaluated":
130	        checks = data.get("checks") or []
131	        ok = sum(1 for c in checks if c.get("ok"))
132	        tests = data.get("tests") or {}
133	        return (f"grade score={data.get('score')} passed={data.get('passed')} "
134	                f"checks={ok}/{len(checks)} tests={tests.get('passed')}p/{tests.get('failed')}f")
135	    if etype == "run.finished":
136	        usage = data.get("usage") or {}
137	        return (f"done  status={data.get('status')} steps={data.get('steps')} "
138	                f"{data.get('duration_ms')}ms in={usage.get('input_tokens')} "
139	                f"out={usage.get('output_tokens')}" + (f" error={short(data.get('error'), 80)}" if data.get("error") else ""))
140	    if etype == "log" and data.get("lvl") in {"warn", "error"}:
141	        return f"{prefix}   [{data.get('lvl')}] {data.get('ev')}: {short(data.get('msg'), 90)}"
142	    return None
143	
144	
145	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
146	
147	
148	def identity_headers(user: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
149	    """PLAN.md §2.9.1: the browser's anonymous id. Sent on every request so the run lands in that
150	    user's conversation list; omitted entirely when --user is not given (anonymous run)."""
151	    return {"X-Faultline-User": user} if user else {}
152	
153	
154	def tail(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]],
155	         deadline: float, quiet: bool, windows: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
156	         user: str | None = None) -> str | None:
157	    """Stream until the run is finished or the deadline passes; returns the final status.
158	
159	    `windows` collects one entry per 110 s segment close (`event: done` with reason=window), which is
160	    the reconnect path apps/web has to implement; it is saved into the evidence summary.
161	    """
162	    last_id = -1
163	    status: str | None = None
164	    windows = [] if windows is None else windows
165	    t_open = time.time()
166	    while time.time() < deadline:
167	        headers = {"Accept": "text/event-stream", **identity_headers(user)}
168	        if last_id >= 0:
169	            headers["Last-Event-ID"] = str(last_id)
170	        try:
171	            with client.stream("GET", f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}/events", headers=headers,
172	                               timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=15.0, read=130.0, write=15.0, pool=15.0)) as resp:
173	                resp.raise_for_status()
174	                for frame in sse_frames(resp):
175	                    if frame["event"] == "done":
176	                        reason = (frame["data"] or {}).get("reason")
177	                        status = (frame["data"] or {}).get("status")
178	                        if reason == "finished":
179	                            return status
180	                        # reason == "window": the 110 s segment elapsed on a still-running run.
181	                        # Record it — this is the reconnect path apps/web must implement too.
182	                        print(f"     ~ sse window closed at id={last_id} (status={status}); reconnecting",
183	                              file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
184	                        windows.append({"at_event_id": last_id, "status": status, "t": round(time.time() - t_open, 1)})
185	                        break  # window closed: reconnect from last_id
186	                    event = frame["data"]
187	                    if not isinstance(event, dict) or "id" not in event:
188	                        continue
189	                    if event["id"] <= last_id:
190	                        continue
191	                    last_id = event["id"]
192	                    events.append(event)
193	                    line = render(event)
194	                    if line and not quiet:
195	                        print(line, flush=True)
196	                    if event.get("type") == "run.finished":
197	                        status = (event.get("data") or {}).get("status")
198	        except (httpx.HTTPError, httpx.StreamError) as exc:
199	            print(f"… stream interrupted ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}); reconnecting", file=sys.stderr)
200	            time.sleep(1.0)
201	            continue
202	        if status in TERMINAL:
203	            return status
204	        # Fall back to the record in case we missed the terminal event on a dropped stream.
205	        record = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=30.0,
206	                            headers=identity_headers(user)).json()
207	        if record.get("status") in TERMINAL:
208	            return record["status"]
209	        time.sleep(0.5)
210	    return status
211	
212	
213	def write_evidence(out_dir: pathlib.Path, run_id: str, record: dict[str, Any],
214	                   events: list[dict[str, Any]], windows: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> None:
215	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
216	    stream = record.get("events") or events  # the record is authoritative when we have it
217	
218	    with (out_dir / "events.jsonl").open("w") as fh:
219	        for event in stream:
220	            fh.write(json.dumps(event, default=str) + "\n")
221	    (out_dir / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2, default=str))
222	    evaluation = record.get("evaluation") or {}
223	    (out_dir / "evaluate.json").write_text(json.dumps(evaluation, indent=2, default=str))
224	
225	    finished = next((e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "run.finished"), {}) or {}
226	    usage = (finished.get("data") or {}).get("usage") or record.get("usage") or {}
227	    lines = [
228	        f"run_id      {run_id}",
229	        f"scenario    {record.get('scenario_id')}",
230	        f"model       {record.get('model')}",
231	        f"status      {record.get('status')}",
232	        f"episode     {record.get('episode_id')}",
233	        f"steps       {record.get('steps')}",
234	        f"events      {len(stream)}",
235	        f"tokens      in={usage.get('input_tokens')} out={usage.get('output_tokens')}",
236	        f"duration_ms {(finished.get('data') or {}).get('duration_ms')}",
237	        f"sse_windows {json.dumps(windows or [])}",
238	        f"error       {record.get('error')}",
239	        "",
240	        f"score       {evaluation.get('score')}  passed={evaluation.get('passed')}",
241	    ]
242	    for check in evaluation.get("checks") or []:
243	        lines.append(f"  [{'x' if check.get('ok') else ' '}] {check.get('id'):<28} "
244	                     f"w={check.get('weight')} {short(check.get('detail'), 70)}")
245	    tests = evaluation.get("tests") or {}
246	    lines += ["", f"tests       passed={tests.get('passed')} failed={tests.get('failed')} "
247	                  f"errors={tests.get('errors')}", short(tests.get("output"), 400), ""]
248	    faults = [e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "fault.fired"]
249	    lines.append(f"faults fired ({len(faults)}):")
250	    for event in faults:
251	        data = event.get("data") or {}
252	        lines.append(f"  step {event.get('step')}: {data.get('kind')} on {data.get('path')} ({data.get('mode')})")
253	    if record.get("summary"):
254	        lines += ["", "agent summary:", short(record["summary"], 1200)]
255	    (out_dir / "summary.txt").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
256	
257	
258	def main() -> int:
259	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run one Faultline episode and save the evidence.")
260	    parser.add_argument("--harness", default=DEFAULT_HARNESS, help="harness base URL")
261	    parser.add_argument("--scenario", default="lost-ack", help="scenario id (see GET /scenarios)")
262	    parser.add_argument("--model", default=None, help="claude-haiku-4-5 | claude-sonnet-5 | claude-opus-5")
263	    parser.add_argument("--max-steps", type=int, default=None)
264	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None)
265	    parser.add_argument("--user", default=None,
266	                        help="X-Faultline-User id (u_<uuid4>) to scope the run to a browser identity; "
267	                             "'new' mints one. Without it the run is anonymous.")
268	    parser.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="evidence directory (default runs/<ts>_<run_id>)")
269	    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=900.0, help="give up after N seconds")
270	    parser.add_argument("--quiet", action="store_true", help="save evidence without the live transcript")
271	    args = parser.parse_args()
272	
273	    harness = args.harness.rstrip("/")
274	    user = args.user
275	    if user == "new":
276	        user = f"u_{uuid.uuid4()}"
277	    if user and not user.startswith("u_"):
278	        print(f"--user must look like u_<uuid4>, got {user!r}", file=sys.stderr)
279	        return 2
280	    headers = identity_headers(user)
281	    body: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": args.scenario}
282	    if args.model:
283	        body["model"] = args.model
284	    if args.max_steps:
285	        body["max_steps"] = args.max_steps
286	    if args.seed is not None:
287	        body["seed"] = args.seed
288	
289	    with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True) as client:
290	        health = client.get(f"{harness}/health", timeout=30.0).json()
291	        print(f"harness {harness} ok={health.get('ok')} model={health.get('model_default')} "
292	              f"provider_key={health.get('has_provider_key')} "
293	              f"sandbox_env={health.get('detail', {}).get('sandbox_env_reachable')}")
294	        if health.get("has_provider_key"):
295	            print("WARNING: the API function reports a provider key in its env (boundary leak)",
296	                  file=sys.stderr)
297	
298	        resp = client.post(f"{harness}/runs", json=body, timeout=60.0, headers=headers)
299	        if resp.status_code >= 400:
300	            print(f"POST /runs failed: {resp.status_code} {resp.text[:500]}", file=sys.stderr)
301	            return 2
302	        created = resp.json()
303	        run_id = created["run_id"]
304	        print(f"run_id {run_id}  scenario={created.get('scenario_id')} model={created.get('model')}"
305	              + (f" conversation={created.get('conversation_id')}" if created.get("conversation_id") else "")
306	              + (f" user={user}" if user else ""))
307	
308	        events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
309	        windows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
310	        t0 = time.time()
311	        status = tail(client, harness, run_id, events, t0 + args.timeout, args.quiet, windows, user)
312	        record = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=60.0, headers=headers).json()
313	        conversations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
314	        if user:
315	            # PLAN.md §3.2 "done when": the run must show up in this browser's conversation list.
316	            got = client.get(f"{harness}/conversations", timeout=30.0, headers=headers)
317	            if got.status_code == 200:
318	                payload = got.json()
319	                conversations = payload if isinstance(payload, list) else payload.get("conversations", [])
320	            else:
321	                print(f"GET /conversations -> {got.status_code} {got.text[:200]}", file=sys.stderr)
322	
323	    out_dir = pathlib.Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{utc_stamp()}_{run_id}"
324	    write_evidence(out_dir, run_id, record, events, windows)
325	    if user:
326	        (out_dir / "conversations.json").write_text(
327	            json.dumps({"user_id": user, "conversation_id": record.get("conversation_id"),
328	                        "conversations": conversations}, indent=2, default=str)
329	        )
330	        listed = any(c.get("id") == record.get("conversation_id") for c in conversations)
331	        print(f"user={user} conversation={record.get('conversation_id')} "
332	              f"listed_in_GET_/conversations={listed} ({len(conversations)} total)")
333	    print(f"\nstatus={record.get('status') or status} score={(record.get('evaluation') or {}).get('score')} "
334	          f"evidence={out_dir}")
335	    return 0 if (record.get("status") or status) in {"ok", "truncated"} else 1
336	
337	
338	if __name__ == "__main__":
339	    raise SystemExit(main())
