1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Export a captured run as a bundled browser replay.
3	
4	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc
5	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/<dir> --out-name gauntlet --pretty
6	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/<dir> --check
7	
8	Reads ``<run_dir>/run.json`` (a ``faultline_common.schemas.RunRecord``, written by
9	``scripts/run_episode_cli.py``) and writes ``apps/web/public/demo/<scenario_id>.json``.
10	
11	The browser folds that file through the same reducer a live SSE run goes through, so the replay is
12	pixel-identical to the real run — see ``apps/web/public/demo/README.md``. Register the new file in
13	``apps/web/src/lib/replay.ts`` (``DEMOS``) so the home page offers it.
14	
15	Owned by the apps/web workstream; writes only into apps/web/public/demo/.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import argparse
21	import json
22	import sys
23	from pathlib import Path
24	from typing import Any
25	
26	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
27	DEMO_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "apps" / "web" / "public" / "demo"
28	
29	# Keep the shared logger optional: this script must run from a bare checkout.
30	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"))
31	try:
32	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # type: ignore
33	
34	    log = get_logger("script")
35	except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - fallback when the shared package is absent
36	
37	    class _Fallback:
38	        def _emit(self, lvl: str, ev: str, msg: str, **kw: Any) -> None:
39	            rec = {"svc": "script", "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg, **kw}
40	            print(json.dumps(rec, default=str), file=sys.stderr)
41	
42	        def info(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
43	            self._emit("info", ev, msg, **kw)
44	
45	        def warn(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
46	            self._emit("warn", ev, msg, **kw)
47	
48	        def error(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
49	            self._emit("error", ev, msg, **kw)
50	
51	    log = _Fallback()
52	
53	REQUIRED_RECORD_FIELDS = ("run_id", "status", "scenario_id", "model", "created_at", "events")
54	EVENT_TYPES = {
55	    "run.started",
56	    "episode.reset",
57	    "turn.text",
58	    "tool.call",
59	    "tool.result",
60	    "fault.fired",
61	    "workspace.diff",
62	    "episode.evaluated",
63	    "run.finished",
64	    "log",
65	    # added once the harness started emitting them (faultline_common.schemas.EventType)
66	    "llm.call",
67	    "turn.thinking",
68	}
69	
70	
71	class ExportError(RuntimeError):
72	    pass
73	
74	
75	def load_record(run_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
76	    path = run_dir / "run.json" if run_dir.is_dir() else run_dir
77	    if not path.exists():
78	        raise ExportError(f"{path} not found — pass a runs/<ts>_<run_id> directory or a run.json")
79	    try:
80	        rec = json.loads(path.read_text())
81	    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
82	        raise ExportError(f"{path} is not valid JSON: {e}") from e
83	    if not isinstance(rec, dict):
84	        raise ExportError(f"{path} does not contain a RunRecord object")
85	    log.info("demo.read", "loaded run record", path=str(path), bytes=path.stat().st_size)
86	    return rec
87	
88	
89	def validate(rec: dict[str, Any], *, allow_unevaluated: bool) -> list[str]:
90	    """Return a list of problems. Empty means the record is fit to ship."""
91	    problems: list[str] = []
92	
93	    for field in REQUIRED_RECORD_FIELDS:
94	        if field not in rec:
95	            problems.append(f"missing RunRecord.{field}")
96	
97	    events = rec.get("events")
98	    if not isinstance(events, list) or not events:
99	        problems.append("RunRecord.events is empty — nothing to replay")
100	        return problems
101	
102	    run_id = rec.get("run_id")
103	    seen_types: set[str] = set()
104	    for i, ev in enumerate(events):
105	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
106	            problems.append(f"event[{i}] is not an object")
107	            continue
108	        if ev.get("id") != i:
109	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].id is {ev.get('id')!r}; ids must run 0..n-1 (SSE Last-Event-ID)")
110	        if run_id is not None and ev.get("run_id") != run_id:
111	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].run_id {ev.get('run_id')!r} != record run_id {run_id!r}")
112	        if not isinstance(ev.get("ts"), str):
113	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].ts is missing or not a string")
114	        etype = ev.get("type")
115	        if etype not in EVENT_TYPES:
116	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].type {etype!r} is not a known EventType")
117	        else:
118	            seen_types.add(etype)
119	        if not isinstance(ev.get("data"), dict):
120	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].data must be an object")
121	
122	    if "episode.evaluated" not in seen_types and not allow_unevaluated:
123	        problems.append(
124	            "no episode.evaluated event — an unfinished run makes a poor demo "
125	            "(pass --allow-unevaluated to ship it anyway)"
126	        )
127	    return problems
128	
129	
130	def sanitise(rec: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
131	    """Mask the few fields that could carry account identifiers into a public bundle."""
132	    out = json.loads(json.dumps(rec))  # deep copy via round-trip
133	    masked = 0
134	
135	    def mask(d: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
136	        nonlocal masked
137	        for key in ("anthropic_workspace", "api_key", "authorization"):
138	            if key in d and d[key]:
139	                d[key] = "****"
140	                masked += 1
141	
142	    mask(out)
143	    for ev in out.get("events", []):
144	        data = ev.get("data")
145	        if isinstance(data, dict):
146	            mask(data)
147	    # sandbox ids are ops detail, not part of the story
148	    for ev in out.get("events", []):
149	        data = ev.get("data")
150	        if isinstance(data, dict) and "sandbox_id" in data:
151	            data.pop("sandbox_id", None)
152	    if masked:
153	        log.info("demo.sanitised", "masked account fields", fields=masked)
154	    return out
155	
156	
157	def summarise(rec: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
158	    events = rec.get("events", [])
159	    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
160	    for ev in events:
161	        t = ev.get("type", "?")
162	        counts[t] = counts.get(t, 0) + 1
163	    evaluation = rec.get("evaluation") or {}
164	    return {
165	        "run_id": rec.get("run_id"),
166	        "scenario_id": rec.get("scenario_id"),
167	        "model": rec.get("model"),
168	        "status": rec.get("status"),
169	        "events": len(events),
170	        "event_types": counts,
171	        "score": evaluation.get("score"),
172	        "passed": evaluation.get("passed"),
173	    }
174	
175	
176	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
177	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
178	    ap.add_argument("run_dir", type=Path, help="runs/<ts>_<run_id> directory (or a run.json path)")
179	    ap.add_argument("--out-name", help="basename without .json (default: the run's scenario_id)")
180	    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, default=DEMO_DIR, help=f"default: {DEMO_DIR}")
181	    ap.add_argument("--pretty", action="store_true", help="indent the JSON (bigger file, readable diff)")
182	    ap.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="validate only; write nothing")
183	    ap.add_argument("--allow-unevaluated", action="store_true", help="ship a run with no episode.evaluated")
184	    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="overwrite an existing demo file")
185	    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
186	
187	    try:
188	        rec = load_record(args.run_dir)
189	    except ExportError as e:
190	        log.error("demo.error", str(e))
191	        return 2
192	
193	    problems = validate(rec, allow_unevaluated=args.allow_unevaluated)
194	    if problems:
195	        for p in problems:
196	            log.error("demo.invalid", p)
197	        return 1
198	
199	    summary = summarise(rec)
200	    log.info("demo.valid", "record passed validation", **summary)
201	
202	    if args.check:
203	        print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
204	        return 0
205	
206	    name = args.out_name or str(rec.get("scenario_id") or "run")
207	    out_path = args.out_dir / f"{name}.json"
208	    if out_path.exists() and not args.force:
209	        log.error("demo.exists", f"{out_path} already exists; pass --force to overwrite")
210	        return 1
211	
212	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
213	    payload = sanitise(rec)
214	    text = json.dumps(payload, indent=2) if args.pretty else json.dumps(payload)
215	    out_path.write_text(text + "\n")
216	
217	    log.info("demo.written", "demo bundled", path=str(out_path), bytes=out_path.stat().st_size, **summary)
218	    print(f"wrote {out_path} ({out_path.stat().st_size} bytes, {summary['events']} events)")
219	    print(
220	        "remember: add it to DEMOS in apps/web/src/lib/replay.ts so the home page offers it, "
221	        "then `pnpm test` in apps/web."
222	    )
223	    return 0
224	
225	
226	if __name__ == "__main__":
227	    raise SystemExit(main())

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:40.123Z
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""One real round trip against a deployed (or local) sandbox-env.
3	
4	    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python scripts/smoke_roundtrip.py
5	    ... --scenario missing-config --fault-proof
6	    ... --base http://127.0.0.1:8000 --out runs/local_smoke
7	
8	What it does, in order:
9	
10	    GET  /health                      the secret boundary: has_provider_key must be false
11	    GET  /scenarios                   public catalogue
12	    POST /episodes                    reset -> a real Modal Sandbox with the fixture in it
13	    MCP  list_tools                   over streamable HTTP, episode via X-Faultline-Episode
14	    MCP  run_command                  `echo hello from $(hostname) && ls -la && python -m pytest -q`
15	    MCP  read_file README.md
16	    MCP  write_file scratch/note.txt
17	    GET  /episodes/{id}               observe: the new file shows up as `added`, with a diff
18	    POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate      hidden tests + recovery checks + score
19	    DELETE /episodes/{id}             terminate the sandbox (always, even on failure)
20	
21	Every response is written to the output directory as JSON, plus a `summary.json` with one
22	pass/fail row per assertion, and the last line on stdout is `SMOKE PASS` / `SMOKE FAIL`.
23	
24	`--fault-proof` additionally proves the fault boundary on `missing-config`: `read_file README.md`
25	must come back ENOENT the first time and with real content the second, from the same sandbox.
26	"""
27	
28	from __future__ import annotations
29	
30	import argparse
31	import asyncio
32	import json
33	import sys
34	import time
35	from datetime import datetime, timezone
36	from pathlib import Path
37	from typing import Any
38	
39	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
40	for _p in (str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"), str(REPO_ROOT / "services" / "sandbox-env")):
41	    if _p not in sys.path:
42	        sys.path.insert(0, _p)
43	
44	import httpx  # noqa: E402
45	from fastmcp import Client  # noqa: E402
46	from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport  # noqa: E402
47	
48	from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402
49	
50	log = get_logger("script")
51	
52	DEFAULT_BASE = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run"
53	SHELL_CMD = "echo hello from $(hostname) && ls -la && python -m pytest -q"
54	NOTE_PATH = "scratch/note.txt"
55	NOTE_BODY = "faultline smoke test\n"
56	
57	#: scenarios whose fault plan hides README.md on the first read (see sandbox_env/scenarios/)
58	TRANSIENT_README_SCENARIOS = ("missing-config",)
59	#: scenarios that delete config/settings.json at reset (sticky missing_file)
60	STICKY_CONFIG_SCENARIOS = ("missing-config", "gauntlet")
61	
62	
63	def utc_stamp() -> str:
64	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
65	
66	
67	class Smoke:
68	    def __init__(self, base: str, scenario: str, out: Path, fault_proof: bool):
69	        self.base = base.rstrip("/")
70	        self.scenario = scenario
71	        self.out = out
72	        self.fault_proof = fault_proof
73	        self.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
74	        self.http = httpx.Client(base_url=self.base, timeout=180.0)
75	        self.checks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
76	        self.episode_id: str | None = None
77	        self.sandbox_id: str | None = None
78	        self.t0 = time.perf_counter()
79	
80	    # -- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------
81	
82	    def save(self, name: str, obj: Any) -> Any:
83	        (self.out / f"{name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=2, default=str))
84	        return obj
85	
86	    def check(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "") -> bool:
87	        self.checks.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
88	        if ok:
89	            log.info("smoke.check", name, ok=True)
90	        else:
91	            log.error("smoke.check_failed", name, ok=False, detail=detail or None)
92	        return bool(ok)
93	
94	    @staticmethod
95	    def tool_payload(result: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
96	        """Normalise a CallToolResult into something JSON-serialisable and assertable."""
97	        text = result.content[0].text if result.content else ""
98	        try:
99	            body = json.loads(text)
100	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
101	            body = {"raw": text}
102	        return {"is_error": bool(result.is_error), "body": body}
103	
104	    def mcp_client(self) -> Client:
105	        return Client(
106	            StreamableHttpTransport(
107	                f"{self.base}/mcp",
108	                headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": self.episode_id or ""},
109	            )
110	        )
111	
112	    # -- phases ----------------------------------------------------------
113	
114	    def phase_rest_preflight(self) -> None:
115	        health = self.save("health", self.http.get("/health").json())
116	        self.check("health.ok", health.get("ok") is True, str(health.get("detail"))[:200])
117	        self.check("health.no_provider_key", health.get("has_provider_key") is False,
118	                   "sandbox-env must never see ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
119	
120	        catalogue = self.save("scenarios", self.http.get("/scenarios").json())
121	        ids = [s["id"] for s in catalogue]
122	        self.check("scenarios.contains_target", self.scenario in ids, f"known: {ids}")
123	        blob = json.dumps(catalogue)
124	        self.check("scenarios.no_plan_leak",
125	                   "fault_plan" not in blob and "hidden_tests" not in blob,
126	                   "the public catalogue must not carry the fault plan")
127	
128	    def phase_reset(self) -> None:
129	        r = self.http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": self.scenario})
130	        reset = self.save("reset", r.json() if r.content else {"status": r.status_code})
131	        # claim the episode id BEFORE asserting anything, so a half-successful reset still gets
132	        # cleaned up by the finally-DELETE instead of leaking a sandbox for 30 minutes
133	        self.episode_id = reset.get("episode_id") if isinstance(reset, dict) else None
134	        self.sandbox_id = reset.get("sandbox_id") if isinstance(reset, dict) else None
135	        ok = r.status_code == 200 and bool(self.episode_id)
136	        self.check("reset.created", ok, f"HTTP {r.status_code}")
137	        if not ok:
138	            raise RuntimeError(f"reset failed: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:400]}")
139	        paths = {f["path"] for f in reset["files"]}
140	        self.check("reset.fixture_present", {"README.md", "CHANGELOG.md", "pytest.ini"} <= paths,
141	                   f"{len(paths)} files")
142	        if self.scenario in STICKY_CONFIG_SCENARIOS:
143	            self.check("reset.sticky_fault_applied", "config/settings.json" not in paths,
144	                       "config/settings.json should already be gone")
145	
146	    async def phase_mcp(self) -> None:
147	        async with self.mcp_client() as c:
148	            tools = await c.list_tools()
149	            self.save("tools", [{"name": t.name, "description": t.description,
150	                                 "input_schema": t.inputSchema} for t in tools])
151	            names = {t.name for t in tools}
152	            self.check("mcp.list_tools", names == {"run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"},
153	                       str(sorted(names)))
154	            self.check("mcp.no_episode_arg",
155	                       all("episode" not in json.dumps(t.inputSchema).lower() for t in tools),
156	                       "the episode must come from the header, not a tool argument")
157	
158	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
159	            res = await c.call_tool("run_command", {"command": SHELL_CMD, "timeout_s": 60},
160	                                    raise_on_error=False)
161	            shell = self.save("run_command", self.tool_payload(res))
162	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
163	            body = shell["body"]
164	            stdout = body.get("stdout", "")
165	            self.check("run_command.executed", not shell["is_error"] and "exit_code" in body,
166	                       str(body)[:200])
167	            self.check("run_command.real_shell", "hello from" in stdout, stdout[:200])
168	            self.check("run_command.sees_the_fixture", "CHANGELOG.md" in stdout, stdout[:200])
169	            # NB: the fixture's pytest.ini already sets `addopts = -q`, so the agent's own
170	            # `pytest -q` runs at -qq and prints the progress line without a summary line.
171	            self.check("run_command.ran_pytest",
172	                       "[100%]" in stdout or any(w in stdout for w in ("passed", "failed", "error")),
173	                       stdout[-200:])
174	            log.info("smoke.run_command", "shell round trip", dur_ms=dur,
175	                     exit_code=body.get("exit_code"), bytes_out=len(stdout))
176	
177	            read = self.save("read_file", self.tool_payload(
178	                await c.call_tool("read_file", {"path": "README.md"}, raise_on_error=False)))
179	            # Only `missing-config` plants a *transient* missing_file on README.md. `gauntlet`
180	            # shares its sticky config fault but not that one, so expecting an ENOENT here would
181	            # be asserting a fault the scenario never planned.
182	            if self.scenario in TRANSIENT_README_SCENARIOS:
183	                # the first README read is the transient missing_file fault; this is expected
184	                self.check("read_file.transient_fault_fired",
185	                           read["is_error"] and read["body"].get("code") == "ENOENT",
186	                           str(read["body"])[:200])
187	                read = self.save("read_file_retry", self.tool_payload(
188	                    await c.call_tool("read_file", {"path": "README.md"}, raise_on_error=False)))
189	            self.check("read_file.content", not read["is_error"]
190	                       and "ratelimiter" in read["body"].get("content", ""),
191	                       str(read["body"])[:200])
192	
193	            wrote = self.save("write_file", self.tool_payload(
194	                await c.call_tool("write_file", {"path": NOTE_PATH, "content": NOTE_BODY},
195	                                  raise_on_error=False)))
196	            self.check("write_file.landed",
197	                       not wrote["is_error"] and len(wrote["body"].get("sha256", "")) == 64,
198	                       str(wrote["body"])[:200])
199	
200	            listing = self.save("list_dir", self.tool_payload(
201	                await c.call_tool("list_dir", {"path": "scratch"}, raise_on_error=False)))
202	            self.check("list_dir.sees_the_new_file",
203	                       not listing["is_error"]
204	                       and any(e["name"] == "note.txt" for e in listing["body"].get("entries", [])),
205	                       str(listing["body"])[:200])
206	
207	    async def phase_fault_proof(self) -> None:
208	        """A second, dedicated episode that proves the ENOENT is injected, not real."""
209	        r = self.http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": "missing-config"})
210	        reset = r.json()
211	        ep = reset["episode_id"]
212	        record: dict[str, Any] = {"episode_id": ep, "sandbox_id": reset.get("sandbox_id"),
213	                                  "scenario_id": "missing-config"}
214	        try:
215	            client = Client(StreamableHttpTransport(f"{self.base}/mcp",
216	                                                    headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": ep}))
217	            async with client as c:
218	                first = self.tool_payload(
219	                    await c.call_tool("read_file", {"path": "README.md"}, raise_on_error=False))
220	                second = self.tool_payload(
221	                    await c.call_tool("read_file", {"path": "README.md"}, raise_on_error=False))
222	                shell = self.tool_payload(
223	                    await c.call_tool("run_command", {"command": "ls -la README.md && wc -c README.md"},
224	                                      raise_on_error=False))
225	            record.update(first_read=first, second_read=second, proof_command=shell)
226	            observe = self.http.get(f"/episodes/{ep}").json()
227	            record["faults_fired"] = observe.get("faults_fired")
228	
229	            self.check("fault_proof.first_read_enoent",
230	                       first["is_error"] and first["body"].get("code") == "ENOENT",
231	                       str(first["body"])[:200])
232	            self.check("fault_proof.second_read_ok",
233	                       not second["is_error"] and "ratelimiter" in second["body"].get("content", ""),
234	                       str(second["body"])[:200])
235	            self.check("fault_proof.file_was_always_there",
236	                       not shell["is_error"] and shell["body"].get("exit_code") == 0,
237	                       "the shell proves README.md never actually left the disk")
238	            self.check("fault_proof.ledger_records_the_fault",
239	                       any(f.get("kind") == "missing_file" and f.get("mode") == "transient"
240	                           for f in record["faults_fired"] or []),
241	                       str(record["faults_fired"]))
242	        finally:
243	            record["delete"] = self.http.delete(f"/episodes/{ep}").json()
244	            self.save("fault_proof", record)
245	
246	    def phase_observe_evaluate(self) -> None:
247	        observe = self.save("observe", self.http.get(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}").json())
248	        by_path = {f["path"]: f["status"] for f in observe["files"]}
249	        self.check("observe.new_file_is_added", by_path.get(NOTE_PATH) == "added", str(by_path)[:300])
250	        self.check("observe.has_a_diff",
251	                   any(d["path"] == NOTE_PATH and NOTE_BODY.strip() in d["unified"]
252	                       for d in observe["diffs"]),
253	                   str(observe["diffs"])[:300])
254	        self.check("observe.step_counted", observe["step"] >= 4, f"step={observe['step']}")
255	
256	        ev = self.save("evaluate", self.http.post(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}/evaluate").json())
257	        self.check("evaluate.has_score", isinstance(ev.get("score"), (int, float))
258	                   and 0 <= ev["score"] <= 100, str(ev.get("score")))
259	        self.check("evaluate.ran_hidden_tests",
260	                   (ev["tests"]["passed"] + ev["tests"]["failed"] + ev["tests"]["errors"]) > 0,
261	                   str(ev["tests"])[:300])
262	        self.check("evaluate.has_checks", len(ev.get("checks", [])) > 0,
263	                   str([c["id"] for c in ev.get("checks", [])]))
264	        self.check("evaluate.releases_the_ledger", len(ev.get("ledger", [])) >= 4,
265	                   f"{len(ev.get('ledger', []))} entries")
266	
267	    def phase_delete(self) -> None:
268	        if not self.episode_id:
269	            return
270	        r = self.http.delete(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}")
271	        body = self.save("delete", r.json() if r.content else {"status": r.status_code})
272	        self.check("delete.terminated", body.get("terminated") is True, f"HTTP {r.status_code}")
273	
274	    # -- driver ----------------------------------------------------------
275	
276	    async def run(self) -> bool:
277	        log.info("smoke.start", self.base, scenario=self.scenario, out=str(self.out),
278	                 fault_proof=self.fault_proof)
279	        try:
280	            self.phase_rest_preflight()
281	            self.phase_reset()
282	            await self.phase_mcp()
283	            self.phase_observe_evaluate()
284	        except Exception as exc:
285	            self.check("smoke.no_exception", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
286	            log.error("smoke.exception", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
287	        finally:
288	            try:
289	                self.phase_delete()
290	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
291	                self.check("delete.terminated", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
292	
293	        if self.fault_proof:
294	            try:
295	                await self.phase_fault_proof()
296	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
297	                self.check("fault_proof", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
298	
299	        ok = all(c["ok"] for c in self.checks)
300	        summary = {
301	            "ok": ok,
302	            "base": self.base,
303	            "scenario": self.scenario,
304	            "episode_id": self.episode_id,
305	            "sandbox_id": self.sandbox_id,
306	            "fault_proof": self.fault_proof,
307	            "duration_ms": int((time.perf_counter() - self.t0) * 1000),
308	            "checks": self.checks,
309	            "failed": [c["name"] for c in self.checks if not c["ok"]],
310	            "ts": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z"),
311	        }
312	        self.save("summary", summary)
313	        self.http.close()
314	        return ok
315	
316	
317	def main() -> int:
318	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Faultline sandbox-env smoke round trip")
319	    ap.add_argument("--base", default=DEFAULT_BASE, help=f"service base URL (default: {DEFAULT_BASE})")
320	    ap.add_argument("--scenario", default="lost-ack", help="scenario id to reset (default: lost-ack)")
321	    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="output directory (default: runs/<ts>_smoke_<scenario>)")
322	    ap.add_argument("--fault-proof", action="store_true",
323	                    help="also prove the injected ENOENT on missing-config (fails once, then reads fine)")
324	    args = ap.parse_args()
325	
326	    out = Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{utc_stamp()}_smoke_{args.scenario}"
327	    if not out.is_absolute():
328	        out = REPO_ROOT / out
329	
330	    smoke = Smoke(args.base, args.scenario, out, args.fault_proof)
331	    ok = asyncio.run(smoke.run())
332	    failed = [c["name"] for c in smoke.checks if not c["ok"]]
333	    print(
334	        f"SMOKE {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} "
335	        f"scenario={args.scenario} base={args.base} "
336	        f"checks={sum(1 for c in smoke.checks if c['ok'])}/{len(smoke.checks)} "
337	        f"episode={smoke.episode_id} out={out}"
338	        + (f" failed={failed}" if failed else "")
339	    )
340	    return 0 if ok else 1
341	
342	
343	if __name__ == "__main__":
344	    raise SystemExit(main())

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Contract check for the browser edge: is the deployed SPA served, and can it talk to the harness?
3	
4	Plain httpx — no browser, no playwright. It checks the three things that break the web app in ways
5	unit tests cannot see, because they only exist once both apps are deployed to Modal:
6	
7	  1. the site is served at all, and `/config.json` carries a usable `harnessUrl`
8	  2. the harness answers `/health` and `/scenarios` *with CORS headers that allow the site origin*
9	     — including the preflight for `X-Faultline-User`, which the SPA sends on every JSON request
10	     (ARCHITECTURE.md §6). A missing `access-control-allow-headers` here is invisible to curl and
11	     fatal in a browser.
12	  3. the SSE route (`GET /runs/{id}/events`) answers with `text/event-stream`, an `access-control-
13	     allow-origin` the site can use, and honours the resume point as a QUERY param — EventSource
14	     cannot set `Last-Event-ID`, so `?last_event_id=` is the only resume the browser has.
15	
16	Everything is reported, nothing is fatal: a missing web deployment is a normal outcome here (the
17	SPA is owned by another process) and is recorded as `skipped`, not as a failure.
18	
19	Usage:
20	    python scripts/web_check.py [--web-url URL] [--harness-url URL] [--run-id r_...] [--label web]
21	
22	Writes runs/<UTC ts>_<label>/{summary.json,checks.json,config.json,health.json,scenarios.json,
23	sse_head.txt} and exits 0 when every non-skipped check passed, 1 otherwise.
24	"""
25	
26	from __future__ import annotations
27	
28	import argparse
29	import json
30	import pathlib
31	import sys
32	from datetime import datetime, timezone
33	from typing import Any
34	
35	import httpx
36	
37	REPO = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
38	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "packages" / "common"))
39	
40	from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402
41	
42	log = get_logger("web-check")
43	
44	DEFAULT_WEB_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.modal.run"
45	DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"
46	
47	# Headers the SPA actually sends on its JSON requests (src/lib/api.ts).
48	REQUEST_HEADERS = "content-type,x-faultline-user"
49	
50	
51	def now_stamp() -> str:
52	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
53	
54	
55	class Checks:
56	    """A flat list of {name, ok, detail} plus `skip`, so one bad URL never aborts the run."""
57	
58	    def __init__(self) -> None:
59	        self.items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
60	
61	    def add(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "", **extra: Any) -> bool:
62	        self.items.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail[:500], **extra})
63	        log.info("check", name, ok=bool(ok), detail=detail[:200] or None)
64	        return bool(ok)
65	
66	    def skip(self, name: str, why: str) -> None:
67	        self.items.append({"name": name, "ok": None, "skipped": True, "detail": why[:500]})
68	        log.warn("check.skipped", name, detail=why[:200])
69	
70	    @property
71	    def failed(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
72	        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is False]
73	
74	    @property
75	    def passed(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
76	        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is True]
77	
78	    @property
79	    def skipped(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
80	        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is None]
81	
82	
83	def cors_origin_allows(headers: httpx.Headers, origin: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
84	    """`*` or an exact echo of our origin both work for a `credentials:'omit'` fetch."""
85	    allow = headers.get("access-control-allow-origin")
86	    if allow is None:
87	        return False, "no access-control-allow-origin header"
88	    if allow == "*" or allow.rstrip("/") == origin.rstrip("/"):
89	        return True, allow
90	    return False, f"allow-origin is {allow!r}, not {origin!r} or '*'"
91	
92	
93	# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- the web app
94	
95	
96	def check_web(client: httpx.Client, web_url: str, checks: Checks, out: pathlib.Path) -> str | None:
97	    """Returns the harnessUrl advertised by the site's /config.json, if it is reachable."""
98	    try:
99	        index = client.get(web_url, timeout=45.0, follow_redirects=True)
100	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - an undeployed site is a normal outcome
101	        checks.skip("web.index", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
102	        checks.skip("web.config_json", "site unreachable")
103	        return None
104	
105	    if index.status_code == 404 and "modal-http" in index.text[:300]:
106	        checks.skip("web.index", f"no web app deployed at {web_url} (404 modal-http)")
107	        checks.skip("web.config_json", "site not deployed")
108	        return None
109	
110	    ok = checks.add("web.index", index.status_code == 200,
111	                    f"GET {web_url} -> {index.status_code}", status=index.status_code)
112	    (out / "index.html").write_text(index.text[:200_000], encoding="utf-8")
113	    if ok:
114	        body = index.text.lower()
115	        checks.add("web.index_is_html", "<div id=\"root\"" in body or "<script" in body,
116	                   "index.html carries the SPA mount point / bundle script")
117	
118	    try:
119	        cfg_res = client.get(f"{web_url.rstrip('/')}/config.json", timeout=30.0)
120	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
121	        checks.add("web.config_json", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
122	        return None
123	
124	    if cfg_res.status_code != 200:
125	        checks.add("web.config_json", False, f"GET /config.json -> {cfg_res.status_code}")
126	        return None
127	
128	    (out / "config.json").write_text(cfg_res.text, encoding="utf-8")
129	    try:
130	        cfg = cfg_res.json()
131	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
132	        checks.add("web.config_json", False, f"not JSON: {exc}")
133	        return None
134	
135	    advertised = cfg.get("harnessUrl")
136	    checks.add(
137	        "web.config_json",
138	        isinstance(advertised, str) and advertised.startswith("http"),
139	        f"harnessUrl={advertised!r} (src/lib/config.ts requires an http(s) URL)",
140	    )
141	    return advertised if isinstance(advertised, str) else None
142	
143	
144	# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- the harness
145	
146	
147	def check_harness(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, origin: str, checks: Checks,
148	                  out: pathlib.Path) -> None:
149	    hdr = {"Origin": origin}
150	
151	    # --- preflight: the one the browser sends before POST /runs with X-Faultline-User
152	    try:
153	        pre = client.options(
154	            f"{harness}/runs",
155	            headers={**hdr, "Access-Control-Request-Method": "POST",
156	                     "Access-Control-Request-Headers": REQUEST_HEADERS},
157	            timeout=45.0,
158	        )
159	        allow_ok, detail = cors_origin_allows(pre.headers, origin)
160	        checks.add("harness.preflight_status", pre.status_code in (200, 204),
161	                   f"OPTIONS /runs -> {pre.status_code}")
162	        checks.add("harness.preflight_origin", allow_ok, detail)
163	        allowed = (pre.headers.get("access-control-allow-headers") or "").lower()
164	        checks.add(
165	            "harness.preflight_allows_user_header",
166	            allowed == "*" or "x-faultline-user" in allowed,
167	            f"access-control-allow-headers: {allowed or '(none)'}",
168	        )
169	        methods = (pre.headers.get("access-control-allow-methods") or "").upper()
170	        checks.add("harness.preflight_allows_methods",
171	                   all(m in methods or methods == "*" for m in ("GET", "POST")),
172	                   f"access-control-allow-methods: {methods or '(none)'}")
173	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
174	        checks.add("harness.preflight_status", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
175	
176	    # --- /health
177	    health: dict[str, Any] = {}
178	    try:
179	        res = client.get(f"{harness}/health", headers=hdr, timeout=60.0)
180	        (out / "health.json").write_text(res.text, encoding="utf-8")
181	        checks.add("harness.health_status", res.status_code == 200, f"GET /health -> {res.status_code}")
182	        ok, detail = cors_origin_allows(res.headers, origin)
183	        checks.add("harness.health_cors", ok, detail)
184	        health = res.json() if res.status_code == 200 else {}
185	        checks.add("harness.health_ok", health.get("ok") is True, f"ok={health.get('ok')!r}")
186	        checks.add(
187	            "harness.secret_boundary",
188	            health.get("has_provider_key") is False,
189	            "the browser-facing function must never hold ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
190	            has_provider_key=health.get("has_provider_key"),
191	        )
192	        checks.add(
193	            "harness.health_shape",
194	            all(k in health for k in ("svc", "ok", "version", "has_provider_key", "detail")),
195	            "Health fields the banner reads (schemas.Health)",
196	            model_default=health.get("model_default"),
197	        )
198	        checks.add(
199	            "harness.sandbox_env_reachable",
200	            (health.get("detail") or {}).get("sandbox_env_reachable") is True,
201	            "health.detail.sandbox_env_reachable",
202	        )
203	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
204	        checks.add("harness.health_status", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
205	
206	    # --- /scenarios
207	    try:
208	        res = client.get(f"{harness}/scenarios", headers=hdr, timeout=60.0)
209	        (out / "scenarios.json").write_text(res.text, encoding="utf-8")
210	        checks.add("harness.scenarios_status", res.status_code == 200,
211	                   f"GET /scenarios -> {res.status_code}")
212	        ok, detail = cors_origin_allows(res.headers, origin)
213	        checks.add("harness.scenarios_cors", ok, detail)
214	        body = res.json() if res.status_code == 200 else {}
215	        items = body.get("scenarios") if isinstance(body, dict) else body
216	        items = items if isinstance(items, list) else []
217	        checks.add("harness.scenarios_nonempty", len(items) > 0, f"{len(items)} scenarios")
218	        required = {"id", "title", "description", "task_prompt", "max_steps", "fault_kinds"}
219	        missing = sorted({f for s in items for f in required - set(s)})
220	        checks.add("harness.scenario_fields", not missing,
221	                   f"missing fields: {missing}" if missing else "all Scenario fields present",
222	                   ids=[s.get("id") for s in items])
223	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
224	        checks.add("harness.scenarios_status", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
225	
226	
227	def check_sse(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, origin: str, run_id: str | None,
228	              checks: Checks, out: pathlib.Path) -> None:
229	    """SSE framing + resume, against a real finished run (the UI's live path in miniature)."""
230	    if run_id is None:
231	        try:
232	            res = client.get(f"{harness}/runs", timeout=45.0)
233	            runs = (res.json() or {}).get("runs") or []
234	            run_id = next((r["run_id"] for r in runs if r.get("status") == "ok"), None)
235	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
236	            checks.skip("harness.sse", f"could not pick a run: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
237	            return
238	    if run_id is None:
239	        checks.skip("harness.sse", "no finished run on this deployment to stream")
240	        return
241	
242	    url = f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}/events"
243	    try:
244	        full = client.get(url, headers={"Origin": origin, "Accept": "text/event-stream"}, timeout=90.0)
245	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
246	        checks.add("harness.sse_status", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
247	        return
248	
249	    (out / "sse_head.txt").write_text(
250	        "\n".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in full.headers.items()) + "\n\n" + full.text[:20_000],
251	        encoding="utf-8",
252	    )
253	    checks.add("harness.sse_status", full.status_code == 200, f"GET {url} -> {full.status_code}",
254	               run_id=run_id)
255	    checks.add("harness.sse_content_type",
256	               full.headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("text/event-stream"),
257	               f"content-type: {full.headers.get('content-type')}")
258	    ok, detail = cors_origin_allows(full.headers, origin)
259	    checks.add("harness.sse_cors", ok, detail)
260	
261	    ids = [int(line.split(":", 1)[1]) for line in full.text.splitlines()
262	           if line.startswith("id: ") and line.split(":", 1)[1].strip().isdigit()]
263	    checks.add("harness.sse_ids_contiguous", ids == list(range(len(ids))),
264	               f"{len(ids)} frames, 0-based contiguous ids (Last-Event-ID resume depends on this)")
265	    checks.add("harness.sse_named_events", "\nevent: " in full.text,
266	               "frames carry `event: <EventType>` (the client listens per type and on `message`)")
267	    checks.add("harness.sse_done_frame", "event: done" in full.text,
268	               "terminal `event: done` frame — the only thing that stops the client reconnecting")
269	    if "event: done" in full.text:
270	        tail = full.text.rsplit("event: done", 1)[1]
271	        payload = tail.split("data:", 1)[1].strip().splitlines()[0] if "data:" in tail else ""
272	        try:
273	            done = json.loads(payload)
274	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
275	            done = {}
276	        checks.add("harness.sse_done_reason", done.get("reason") in ("finished", "window"),
277	                   f"done payload: {payload[:200]}")
278	
279	    # Resume as the browser does it: a query param, because EventSource cannot set headers.
280	    if len(ids) >= 2:
281	        cut = ids[-2]
282	        try:
283	            resumed = client.get(f"{url}?last_event_id={cut}",
284	                                 headers={"Origin": origin, "Accept": "text/event-stream"}, timeout=90.0)
285	            got = [int(line.split(":", 1)[1]) for line in resumed.text.splitlines()
286	                   if line.startswith("id: ") and line.split(":", 1)[1].strip().isdigit()]
287	            checks.add("harness.sse_resume_query_param", got == [i for i in ids if i > cut],
288	                       f"?last_event_id={cut} replayed {got} (expected only ids > {cut})")
289	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
290	            checks.add("harness.sse_resume_query_param", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
291	    else:
292	        checks.skip("harness.sse_resume_query_param", "run too short to test a resume point")
293	
294	
295	def main() -> int:
296	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
297	    ap.add_argument("--web-url", default=DEFAULT_WEB_URL)
298	    ap.add_argument("--harness-url", default=DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL)
299	    ap.add_argument("--run-id", default=None, help="run to stream; default = newest finished run")
300	    ap.add_argument("--label", default="web")
301	    ap.add_argument("--out-root", default=str(REPO / "runs"))
302	    args = ap.parse_args()
303	
304	    web_url = args.web_url.rstrip("/")
305	    harness_url = args.harness_url.rstrip("/")
306	    out = pathlib.Path(args.out_root) / f"{now_stamp()}_{args.label}"
307	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
308	
309	    checks = Checks()
310	    log.info("start", "web contract check", web_url=web_url, harness_url=harness_url, out=str(out))
311	
312	    with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=False) as client:
313	        advertised = check_web(client, web_url, checks, out)
314	        if advertised:
315	            checks.add(
316	                "web.config_points_at_harness",
317	                advertised.rstrip("/") == harness_url,
318	                f"/config.json harnessUrl={advertised!r} vs checked harness {harness_url!r}",
319	            )
320	            harness_url = advertised.rstrip("/")
321	        # The SPA's origin is what the harness must allow; when the site is not deployed we still
322	        # check CORS against the URL it *will* have.
323	        origin = web_url
324	        check_harness(client, harness_url, origin, checks, out)
325	        check_sse(client, harness_url, origin, args.run_id, checks, out)
326	
327	    summary = {
328	        "ts": now_stamp(),
329	        "web_url": web_url,
330	        "harness_url": harness_url,
331	        "origin_checked": web_url,
332	        "passed": len(checks.passed),
333	        "failed": len(checks.failed),
334	        "skipped": len(checks.skipped),
335	        "failures": [c["name"] for c in checks.failed],
336	        "skips": [c["name"] for c in checks.skipped],
337	        "ok": not checks.failed,
338	    }
339	    (out / "checks.json").write_text(json.dumps(checks.items, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
340	    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
341	
342	    for c in checks.items:
343	        mark = "SKIP" if c["ok"] is None else ("PASS" if c["ok"] else "FAIL")
344	        print(f"{mark:4}  {c['name']:38}  {c['detail']}")
345	    print(f"\n{summary['passed']} passed, {summary['failed']} failed, {summary['skipped']} skipped")
346	    print(f"evidence: {out}")
347	    log.info("done", "web contract check finished", **{k: summary[k] for k in ("passed", "failed", "skipped", "ok")})
348	    return 0 if summary["ok"] else 1
349	
350	
351	if __name__ == "__main__":
352	    raise SystemExit(main())

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:18:45.925Z
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""V3 fault proofs (PLAN.md §4) — scripted, **no-model** episodes against a live sandbox-env.
3	
4	    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python scripts/fault_proofs.py
5	    ... --only lost-ack_careless --base http://127.0.0.1:8000 --out runs/local_faults
6	
7	One episode per case, driven entirely from this script through the real MCP streamable-HTTP tool
8	surface. No Claude, no harness: every tool call here is chosen by the script, so whatever the
9	evidence shows is a property of the *environment*, not of a model that happened to behave well.
10	
11	What each case proves
12	---------------------
13	`missing-config_careful`  missing_file, both modes.
14	    transient: `read_file README.md` -> ENOENT, the *next* identical read -> the real file, and a
15	    shell `sha256sum README.md` in the same sandbox exits 0 the whole time — the file never left
16	    the disk, so the fault provably lives at the tool boundary and not in the filesystem. Listings
17	    hide the name while the fault is pending (and do not consume its single hit).
18	    sticky: `config/settings.json` is simply gone from the reset tree; the ENOENT is real.
19	    Then a careful recovery (recreate the file from the README's documented schema) + evaluate.
20	
21	`locked-file_careful`     denied_write.
22	    Two `write_file` calls come back EACCES, and after each one `GET /episodes/{id}` shows
23	    `src/ratelimiter/limits.py` with status `unchanged` and the *baseline* sha — nothing executed.
24	    The third write lands; the fix is then verified by re-reading and re-running pytest.
25	
26	`lost-ack_careful`        ack_lost, recovered well.
27	    The write is held for ~`delay_ms` and then fails with ETIMEDOUT, but observe shows CHANGELOG.md
28	    `modified` against the baseline — the write *did* land. The careful path reads the file back,
29	    sees the section already present, and does not write it again.
30	
31	`lost-ack_careless`       ack_lost, recovered badly (the control).
32	    Identical up to the lost ack, then a blind second append with no verification. Same fault, same
33	    environment, and the grader must separate them: `verified_before_rewrite` and
34	    `no_duplicate_entry` both fail and the hidden release test fails.
35	
36	`gauntlet_careful`        all three faults in one episode.
37	
38	`secret-boundary`         V6, sandbox half: `env | grep -i anthropic` finds nothing, and both curl
39	    and a raw python socket fail to reach the public internet (`block_network=True`).
40	
41	Exit code 0 iff every case's assertions hold. Evidence (one JSON per case, a combined trace and a
42	summary) is written to `runs/<UTC timestamp>_faults/`.
43	"""
44

70	DEFAULT_BASE = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run"

79	VERSION_BODY = '__version__ = "0.2.0"\n'

86	#: at which pytest prints no summary line at all (same reason grader.py does it).

94	def utc_stamp() -> str:
95	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
96	
97	
98	def now_iso() -> str:
99	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
100	
101	
102	def cap(value: Any, limit: int = ARG_CAP) -> Any:
103	    if isinstance(value, str) and len(value) > limit:
104	        return value[:limit] + f"…[truncated {len(value) - limit} chars]"
105	    if isinstance(value, dict):
106	        return {k: cap(v, limit) for k, v in value.items()}
107	    return value
