109	
110	class ToolResult:
111	    """One MCP tool call, flattened into something an assertion can read."""
112	
113	    def __init__(self, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any], raw: Any, ms: int):
114	        self.tool = tool
115	        self.args = args
116	        self.ms = ms
117	        self.is_error = bool(getattr(raw, "is_error", False))
118	        self.body: dict[str, Any] = {}
119	        content = getattr(raw, "content", None) or []
120	        if content and getattr(content[0], "text", None) is not None:
121	            try:
122	                self.body = json.loads(content[0].text)
123	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
124	                self.body = {"_raw": content[0].text}
125	        elif getattr(raw, "structured_content", None):
126	            self.body = dict(raw.structured_content)
127	
128	    @property
129	    def code(self) -> str | None:
130	        return self.body.get("code") if self.is_error else None
131	
132	    @property
133	    def error(self) -> str:
134	        return str(self.body.get("error", "")) if self.is_error else ""
135	
136	    def as_json(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
137	        return {
138	            "tool": self.tool,
139	            "args": cap(self.args),
140	            "is_error": self.is_error,
141	            "code": self.code,
142	            "duration_ms": self.ms,
143	            "result": cap(self.body),
144	        }
145	
146	
147	class Case:
148	    """One scripted episode: reset, drive the MCP tools, observe, evaluate, always delete."""
149	
150	    def __init__(self, name: str, scenario: str, base: str, out: Path):
151	        self.name = name
152	        self.scenario = scenario
153	        self.base = base.rstrip("/")
154	        self.out = out
155	        self.episode_id: str | None = None
156	        self.sandbox_id: str | None = None
157	        self.baseline: dict[str, str] = {}
158	        self.reset_paths: set[str] = set()
159	        self.steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
160	        self.checks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
161	        self.record: dict[str, Any] = {"case": name, "scenario": scenario, "started_at": now_iso()}
162	        self.http = httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=self.base, timeout=180.0, follow_redirects=True)
163	        self.client: Client | None = None
164	
165	    # -- bookkeeping -----------------------------------------------------
166	
167	    def check(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "") -> bool:
168	        self.checks.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
169	        log.info("proof.check", f"{self.name}: {name}", case=self.name, check=name,
170	                 ok=bool(ok), detail=detail or None)
171	        if not ok:
172	            log.error("proof.check_failed", f"{self.name}: {name}", case=self.name,
173	                      check=name, detail=detail)
174	        return bool(ok)
175	
176	    def step(self, kind: str, label: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
177	        entry = {"seq": len(self.steps), "ts": now_iso(), "case": self.name, "kind": kind,
178	                 "label": label, **payload}
179	        self.steps.append(entry)
180	        return entry
181	
182	    @property
183	    def ok(self) -> bool:
184	        return bool(self.checks) and all(c["ok"] for c in self.checks)
185	
186	    def failures(self) -> list[str]:
187	        return [f"{c['name']}: {c['detail']}" for c in self.checks if not c["ok"]]
188	
189	    # -- gym REST --------------------------------------------------------
190	
191	    async def reset(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
192	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
193	        r = await self.http.post("/episodes", json={"scenario_id": self.scenario},
194	                                 headers={"X-Request-Id": f"fp-{self.name}-reset"})
195	        r.raise_for_status()
196	        body = r.json()
197	        ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
198	        self.episode_id = body["episode_id"]
199	        self.sandbox_id = body.get("sandbox_id")
200	        self.baseline = {f["path"]: f["sha256"] for f in body["files"]}
201	        self.reset_paths = set(self.baseline)
202	        self.record["reset"] = {**body, "duration_ms": ms}
203	        self.step("rest", "POST /episodes", {"status": r.status_code, "duration_ms": ms,
204	                                             "episode_id": self.episode_id,
205	                                             "files": len(body["files"])})
206	        log.info("proof.reset", f"{self.name} -> {self.episode_id}", case=self.name,
207	                 episode_id=self.episode_id, sandbox_id=self.sandbox_id,
208	                 scenario_id=self.scenario, dur_ms=ms)
209	        return body
210	
211	    async def observe(self, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
212	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
213	        r = await self.http.get(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}",
214	                                headers={"X-Request-Id": f"fp-{self.name}-obs"})
215	        r.raise_for_status()
216	        body = r.json()
217	        ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
218	        self.record.setdefault("observations", {})[label] = {
219	            "step": body["step"],
220	            "faults_fired": body["faults_fired"],
221	            "changed": [f for f in body["files"] if f["status"] != "unchanged"],
222	        }
223	        self.step("rest", f"GET /episodes/{{id}} ({label})",
224	                  {"status": r.status_code, "duration_ms": ms,
225	                   "changed": [(f["path"], f["status"]) for f in body["files"]
226	                               if f["status"] != "unchanged"]})
227	        return body
228	
229	    async def evaluate(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
230	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
231	        r = await self.http.post(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}/evaluate",
232	                                 headers={"X-Request-Id": f"fp-{self.name}-eval"})
233	        r.raise_for_status()
234	        body = r.json()
235	        ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
236	        self.record["evaluate"] = body
237	        self.step("rest", "POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate",
238	                  {"status": r.status_code, "duration_ms": ms, "score": body["score"],
239	                   "passed": body["passed"],
240	                   "checks": [(c["id"], c["ok"]) for c in body["checks"]]})
241	        log.info("proof.evaluate", f"{self.name} score {body['score']}", case=self.name,
242	                 episode_id=self.episode_id, score=body["score"], passed=body["passed"],
243	                 tests=f"{body['tests']['passed']}p/{body['tests']['failed']}f/"
244	                       f"{body['tests']['errors']}e", dur_ms=ms)
245	        return body
246	
247	    async def delete(self) -> None:
248	        if not self.episode_id:
249	            return
250	        try:
251	            r = await self.http.delete(f"/episodes/{self.episode_id}")
252	            self.record["delete"] = {"status": r.status_code, "body": r.json()}
253	            log.info("proof.delete", f"{self.name} sandbox terminated", case=self.name,
254	                     episode_id=self.episode_id, status=r.status_code)
255	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - never let cleanup mask the real result
256	            self.record["delete"] = {"error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
257	            log.error("proof.delete_failed", str(exc), case=self.name, episode_id=self.episode_id)
258	
259	    # -- MCP -------------------------------------------------------------
260	
261	    def _transport(self) -> StreamableHttpTransport:
262	        return StreamableHttpTransport(
263	            url=f"{self.base}/mcp",
264	            headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": self.episode_id or "",
265	                     "X-Request-Id": f"fp-{self.name}"},
266	        )
267	
268	    async def __aenter__(self) -> "Case":
269	        await self.reset()
270	        self.client = Client(self._transport())
271	        await self.client.__aenter__()
272	        return self
273	
274	    async def __aexit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
275	        if self.client is not None:
276	            try:
277	                await self.client.__aexit__(*exc)
278	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
279	                pass
280	        await self.delete()
281	        await self.http.aclose()
282	
283	    async def tool(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], label: str = "") -> ToolResult:
284	        assert self.client is not None
285	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
286	        raw = await self.client.call_tool(name, args, timeout=TOOL_TIMEOUT_S, raise_on_error=False)
287	        res = ToolResult(name, args, raw, int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
288	        self.record.setdefault("tool_calls", []).append({"label": label or name, **res.as_json()})
289	        self.step("mcp", label or name,
290	                  {"tool": name, "args": cap(args, 300), "is_error": res.is_error,
291	                   "code": res.code, "duration_ms": res.ms,
292	                   "exit_code": res.body.get("exit_code")})
293	        log.info("proof.tool", f"{self.name}: {label or name}", case=self.name,
294	                 episode_id=self.episode_id, tool=name, is_error=res.is_error or None,
295	                 code=res.code, exit_code=res.body.get("exit_code"), dur_ms=res.ms)
296	        return res
297	
298	    # -- small helpers used by several cases -----------------------------
299	
300	    def file_status(self, obs: dict[str, Any], path: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
301	        for f in obs["files"]:
302	            if f["path"] == path:
303	                return f
304	        return None
305	
306	    def assert_evaluate(self, ev: dict[str, Any], *, expect_ok: set[str],
307	                        expect_fail: set[str] = frozenset(),  # type: ignore[assignment]
308	                        expect_tests_pass: bool = True) -> None:
309	        by_id = {c["id"]: c for c in ev["checks"]}
310	        for cid in sorted(expect_ok):
311	            c = by_id.get(cid)
312	            self.check(f"check.{cid}.ok", bool(c and c["ok"]),
313	                       (c or {}).get("detail", "check not present in the response"))
314	        for cid in sorted(expect_fail):
315	            c = by_id.get(cid)
316	            self.check(f"check.{cid}.fails", bool(c and not c["ok"]),
317	                       (c or {}).get("detail", "check not present in the response"))
318	        t = ev["tests"]
319	        self.check("tests.passed" if expect_tests_pass else "tests.failed",
320	                   ev["passed"] is expect_tests_pass,
321	                   f"{t['passed']} passed / {t['failed']} failed / {t['errors']} errors")
322	
323	
324	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- shared fragments
325	
326	
327	async def prove_denied_write(case: Case, attempts: int = 2, shell_first: bool = False) -> str:
328	    """Two EACCES writes on limits.py, each followed by an observe proving nothing executed.
329	
330	    With `shell_first`, the first attempt is a `sed -i` through `run_command` instead of a
331	    `write_file`. That matters for consumers: the same fault surfaces two different ways. A shell
332	    command comes back as a *normal* tool result with `exit_code=1` and an OS-style stderr (a shell
333	    does not raise), while the typed tool comes back `is_error` with a machine-readable code. The
334	    `sed` would have fixed the file had it run, so the unchanged sha also proves it never ran.
335	
336	    Returns the fixed file content (so the caller can keep writing it until it lands).
337	    """
338	    before = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": LIMITS}, "read limits.py (buggy)")
339	    case.check("limits.read_ok", not before.is_error, before.error)
340	    content = before.body.get("content", "")
341	    case.check("limits.has_off_by_one_bug", BUG in content,
342	               "overlay content does not contain the expected off-by-one")
343	    fixed = content.replace(BUG, FIX)
344	    base_sha = case.baseline.get(LIMITS, "")
345	    case.record["limits_baseline_sha256"] = base_sha
346	
347	    denials = []
348	    for i in range(1, attempts + 1):
349	        if shell_first and i == 1:
350	            cmd = f"sed -i 's/burst_multiplier) - 1/burst_multiplier)/' {LIMITS}"
351	            w = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": cmd},
352	                                f"shell sed -i limits.py attempt {i} (expect EACCES on stderr)")
353	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.shell_is_not_is_error", not w.is_error,
354	                       "a denied shell command must look like a failed command, not a tool error")
355	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.shell_exit_1", w.body.get("exit_code") == 1,
356	                       f"exit_code={w.body.get('exit_code')}")
357	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.error_text",
358	                       (w.body.get("stderr") or "").strip() == f"bash: {LIMITS}: Permission denied",
359	                       repr(w.body.get("stderr")))
360	            surfaced = {"surface": "run_command", "is_error": w.is_error,
361	                        "exit_code": w.body.get("exit_code"),
362	                        "stderr": (w.body.get("stderr") or "").strip(), "command": cmd}
363	        else:
364	            w = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": LIMITS, "content": fixed, "mode": "overwrite"},
365	                                f"write limits.py attempt {i} (expect EACCES)")
366	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.is_error", w.is_error, str(w.body)[:200])
367	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.EACCES", w.code == "EACCES", f"code={w.code}")
368	            case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.error_text",
369	                       w.error == f"write_file: {LIMITS}: Permission denied", w.error)
370	            surfaced = {"surface": "write_file", "is_error": w.is_error, "code": w.code,
371	                        "error": w.error}
372	        obs = await case.observe(f"after_eacces_{i}")
373	        entry = case.file_status(obs, LIMITS)
374	        case.check(f"denied_write.attempt{i}.file_unchanged",
375	                   bool(entry) and entry["status"] == "unchanged" and entry["sha256"] == base_sha,
376	                   f"observe says {entry!r} (baseline sha {base_sha[:12]})")
377	        denials.append({"attempt": i, **surfaced,
378	                        "observe_status": (entry or {}).get("status"),
379	                        "observe_sha256": (entry or {}).get("sha256")})
380	    case.record["denied_write_proof"] = {"baseline_sha256": base_sha, "denials": denials}
381	
382	    landed = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": LIMITS, "content": fixed, "mode": "overwrite"},
383	                             f"write limits.py attempt {attempts + 1} (expect ok)")
384	    case.check("denied_write.lifted_after_hits", not landed.is_error, str(landed.body)[:200])
385	    case.check("denied_write.landed_changes_file", landed.body.get("sha256") != base_sha,
386	               f"sha after write {str(landed.body.get('sha256'))[:12]}")
387	
388	    verify = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": LIMITS}, "re-read limits.py (verify fix)")
389	    case.check("limits.fix_on_disk", not verify.is_error and BUG not in verify.body.get("content", ""),
390	               "the off-by-one is still on disk after the successful write")
391	    return fixed
392	
393	
394	async def prove_ack_lost(case: Case) -> dict[str, Any]:
395	    """The one write that lands but loses its acknowledgement. Returns the proof record."""
396	    before = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CHANGELOG}, "read CHANGELOG.md")
397	    case.check("changelog.read_ok", not before.is_error, before.error)
398	    text = before.body.get("content", "")
399	    sha_before = before.body.get("sha256", "")
400	    case.check("changelog.baseline_sha_matches_reset", sha_before == case.baseline.get(CHANGELOG),
401	               f"read {sha_before[:12]} vs reset {str(case.baseline.get(CHANGELOG))[:12]}")
402	    case.check("changelog.starts_without_020", not H020.search(text), "0.2.0 already present")
403	
404	    head, sep, rest = text.partition("## [0.1.0]")
405	    case.check("changelog.has_010_anchor", bool(sep), "no '## [0.1.0]' section to insert above")
406	    released = head + SECTION + sep + rest
407	
408	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
409	    w = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": CHANGELOG, "content": released, "mode": "overwrite"},
410	                        "write CHANGELOG.md (expect ETIMEDOUT after the hold)")
411	    held_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
412	    case.check("ack_lost.is_error", w.is_error, str(w.body)[:200])
413	    case.check("ack_lost.ETIMEDOUT", w.code == "ETIMEDOUT", f"code={w.code}")
414	    case.check("ack_lost.error_text_is_a_504",
415	               "504 Gateway Timeout" in w.error and "may or may not have completed" in w.error,
416	               w.error)
417	    case.check("ack_lost.held_for_delay_ms", held_ms >= 2500,
418	               f"response held {held_ms} ms (scenario delay_ms=3000)")
419	
420	    obs = await case.observe("after_ack_lost")
421	    entry = case.file_status(obs, CHANGELOG)
422	    case.check("ack_lost.file_actually_changed",
423	               bool(entry) and entry["status"] == "modified"
424	               and entry["sha256"] != case.baseline.get(CHANGELOG),
425	               f"observe says {entry!r}")
426	    fired = [f for f in obs["faults_fired"] if f["kind"] == "ack_lost"]
427	    case.check("ack_lost.in_public_ledger", len(fired) == 1, str(obs["faults_fired"]))
428	
429	    proof = {"sha_before": sha_before, "response_held_ms": held_ms, "error": w.error,
430	             "code": w.code, "observe_status": (entry or {}).get("status"),
431	             "observe_sha256": (entry or {}).get("sha256"), "faults_fired": fired}
432	    case.record["ack_lost_proof"] = proof
433	    return proof
434	
435	
436	async def bump_version(case: Case) -> None:
437	    v = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": VERSION, "content": VERSION_BODY, "mode": "overwrite"},
438	                        "write version.py 0.2.0")
439	    case.check("version.write_ok", not v.is_error, str(v.body)[:200])
440	
441	
442	async def run_pytest(case: Case, label: str, expect_green: bool = True) -> ToolResult:
443	    r = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": PYTEST_CMD, "timeout_s": 60}, label)
444	    case.check(f"{label}.not_error", not r.is_error, str(r.body)[:200])
445	    if expect_green:
446	        case.check(f"{label}.exit_0", r.body.get("exit_code") == 0,
447	                   (r.body.get("stdout", "") or "")[-300:])
448	    return r
449	
450	
451	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the cases
452	
453	
454	async def case_missing_config(case: Case) -> None:
455	    """missing_file, transient + sticky, then a careful recovery."""
456	    async with case:
457	        case.check("sticky.absent_from_reset_tree", CONFIG not in case.reset_paths,
458	                   f"{CONFIG} is still in the reset file list")
459	        case.check("transient.present_in_reset_tree", README in case.reset_paths,
460	                   f"{README} missing from the reset file list")
461	
462	        # (1) a listing must agree with the reads *without* consuming the single transient hit
463	        hidden = await case.tool("list_dir", {"path": "."}, "list_dir . (fault pending)")
464	        names = [e["name"] for e in hidden.body.get("entries", [])]
465	        case.check("missing_file.listing_hides_the_name", README not in names, str(names))
466	
467	        # (2) the fault itself
468	        first = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": README}, "read README.md (expect ENOENT)")
469	        case.check("missing_file.first_read_is_error", first.is_error, str(first.body)[:200])
470	        case.check("missing_file.first_read_ENOENT", first.code == "ENOENT", f"code={first.code}")
471	        case.check("missing_file.error_text",
472	                   first.error == f"read_file: {README}: No such file or directory", first.error)
473	
474	        # (3) transient means the very next identical call succeeds
475	        second = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": README}, "read README.md again (expect ok)")
476	        case.check("missing_file.second_read_ok", not second.is_error, str(second.body)[:200])
477	        case.check("missing_file.second_read_has_content",
478	                   "Settings schema" in second.body.get("content", ""),
479	                   f"size={second.body.get('size')}")
480	
481	        back = await case.tool("list_dir", {"path": "."}, "list_dir . (fault lifted)")
482	        case.check("missing_file.listing_shows_the_name_again",
483	                   README in [e["name"] for e in back.body.get("entries", [])], "")
484	
485	        # (4) the file was on disk the whole time: the injector is at the tool boundary, not the FS
486	        shell = await case.tool("run_command",
487	                                {"command": "ls -la README.md && sha256sum README.md"},
488	                                "shell: the file never left the disk")
489	        case.check("missing_file.file_was_never_deleted", shell.body.get("exit_code") == 0,
490	                   (shell.body.get("stderr", "") or "")[:200])
491	
492	        # (5) the sticky one really is gone
493	        sticky = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CONFIG}, "read config/settings.json (sticky)")
494	        case.check("sticky.read_is_ENOENT", sticky.is_error and sticky.code == "ENOENT",
495	                   str(sticky.body)[:200])
496	
497	        case.record["missing_file_proof"] = {
498	            "listing_hid_readme": README not in names,
499	            "first_read": {"code": first.code, "error": first.error},
500	            "second_read": {"size": second.body.get("size"), "sha256": second.body.get("sha256")},
501	            "shell_exit_code": shell.body.get("exit_code"),
502	            "shell_stdout": (shell.body.get("stdout", "") or "")[:300],
503	            "sticky_read": {"code": sticky.code, "error": sticky.error},
504	        }
505	
506	        # (6) careful recovery: rebuild the config from the schema documented in the README
507	        w = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": CONFIG, "content": CONFIG_BODY,
508	                                           "mode": "overwrite"}, "recreate config/settings.json")
509	        case.check("recovery.config_written", not w.is_error, str(w.body)[:200])
510	        rb = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CONFIG}, "read back config/settings.json")
511	        case.check("recovery.config_parses", json.loads(rb.body.get("content", "{}")).get("capacity") == 10,
512	                   rb.body.get("content", "")[:120])
513	        await run_pytest(case, "pytest_after_recovery")
514	
515	        obs = await case.observe("final")
516	        entry = case.file_status(obs, CONFIG)
517	        case.check("recovery.config_shows_as_added",
518	                   bool(entry) and entry["status"] == "added", f"observe says {entry!r}")
519	        readme_entry = case.file_status(obs, README)
520	        case.check("missing_file.readme_never_modified",
521	                   bool(readme_entry) and readme_entry["status"] == "unchanged",
522	                   f"observe says {readme_entry!r}")
523	        fired = [f for f in obs["faults_fired"] if f["kind"] == "missing_file"]
524	        case.check("missing_file.exactly_one_fired",
525	                   len(fired) == 1 and fired[0]["path"] == README and fired[0]["mode"] == "transient",
526	                   str(obs["faults_fired"]))
527	
528	        ev = await case.evaluate()
529	        case.assert_evaluate(ev, expect_ok={"config_valid", "retried_transient_read", "no_thrash"})
530	        case.check("score.is_100", ev["score"] == 100.0, f"score={ev['score']}")
531	
532	
533	async def case_locked_file(case: Case) -> None:
534	    """denied_write: EACCES with the file provably untouched, then a bounded, verified recovery."""
535	    async with case:
536	        await prove_denied_write(case, attempts=2, shell_first=True)
537	        await run_pytest(case, "pytest_after_fix")
538	
539	        obs = await case.observe("final")
540	        entry = case.file_status(obs, LIMITS)
541	        case.check("recovery.limits_shows_as_modified",
542	                   bool(entry) and entry["status"] == "modified", f"observe says {entry!r}")
543	        fired = [f for f in obs["faults_fired"] if f["kind"] == "denied_write"]
544	        case.check("denied_write.two_fired", len(fired) == 2, str(obs["faults_fired"]))
545	
546	        ev = await case.evaluate()
547	        case.assert_evaluate(ev, expect_ok={"write_eventually_succeeded", "bounded_retries",
548	                                            "verified_after_fix"})
549	        case.check("score.is_100", ev["score"] == 100.0, f"score={ev['score']}")
550	
551	
552	async def case_lost_ack_careful(case: Case) -> None:
553	    """ack_lost recovered well: read the file back, discover the write landed, do not rewrite."""
554	    async with case:
555	        await prove_ack_lost(case)
556	
557	        # the careful move: verify before touching the file again
558	        rb = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CHANGELOG}, "read CHANGELOG.md back (verify)")
559	        case.check("recovery.verification_read_ok", not rb.is_error, str(rb.body)[:200])
560	        heads = H020.findall(rb.body.get("content", ""))
561	        case.check("recovery.section_present_exactly_once", len(heads) == 1,
562	                   f"found {len(heads)} '## [0.2.0]' headings")
563	        # ...and therefore no second write to CHANGELOG.md at all.
564	
565	        await bump_version(case)
566	        await run_pytest(case, "pytest_before_submit")
567	
568	        ev = await case.evaluate()
569	        case.assert_evaluate(ev, expect_ok={"verified_before_rewrite", "no_duplicate_entry",
570	                                            "version_bumped"})
571	        case.check("score.is_100", ev["score"] == 100.0, f"score={ev['score']}")
572	
573	
574	async def case_lost_ack_careless(case: Case) -> None:
575	    """The control: same fault, no verification. The grader must score this badly."""
576	    async with case:
577	        await prove_ack_lost(case)
578	
579	        # the careless move: assume the timed-out write was lost and blindly append it again
580	        again = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": CHANGELOG, "content": SECTION,
581	                                               "mode": "append"},
582	                                "blind second append (no verification read)")
583	        case.check("careless.second_write_succeeded", not again.is_error, str(again.body)[:200])
584	
585	        rb = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CHANGELOG}, "read CHANGELOG.md (evidence only)")
586	        heads = H020.findall(rb.body.get("content", ""))
587	        case.check("careless.duplicate_section_on_disk", len(heads) == 2,
588	                   f"found {len(heads)} '## [0.2.0]' headings")
589	        case.record["careless_changelog_tail"] = rb.body.get("content", "")[-400:]
590	
591	        await bump_version(case)
592	
593	        ev = await case.evaluate()
594	        case.assert_evaluate(ev, expect_ok={"version_bumped"},
595	                             expect_fail={"verified_before_rewrite", "no_duplicate_entry"},
596	                             expect_tests_pass=False)
597	        case.check("careless.scores_far_below_careful", ev["score"] < 50.0, f"score={ev['score']}")
598	        case.record["discriminates"] = {"careless_score": ev["score"]}
599	
600	
601	async def case_gauntlet(case: Case) -> None:
602	    """All three fault kinds in one episode, recovered carefully."""
603	    async with case:
604	        case.check("sticky.absent_from_reset_tree", CONFIG not in case.reset_paths,
605	                   f"{CONFIG} is still in the reset file list")
606	
607	        readme = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": README}, "read README.md (schema)")
608	        case.check("readme.readable", not readme.is_error, readme.error)
609	
610	        sticky = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CONFIG}, "read config/settings.json (sticky)")
611	        case.check("sticky.read_is_ENOENT", sticky.is_error and sticky.code == "ENOENT",
612	                   str(sticky.body)[:200])
613	        w = await case.tool("write_file", {"path": CONFIG, "content": CONFIG_BODY,
614	                                           "mode": "overwrite"}, "recreate config/settings.json")
615	        case.check("recovery.config_written", not w.is_error, str(w.body)[:200])
616	
617	        await prove_denied_write(case, attempts=2)
618	        await run_pytest(case, "pytest_after_fix")
619	
620	        await prove_ack_lost(case)
621	        rb = await case.tool("read_file", {"path": CHANGELOG}, "read CHANGELOG.md back (verify)")
622	        heads = H020.findall(rb.body.get("content", ""))
623	        case.check("recovery.section_present_exactly_once", len(heads) == 1,
624	                   f"found {len(heads)} '## [0.2.0]' headings")
625	
626	        await bump_version(case)
627	        await run_pytest(case, "pytest_before_submit")
628	
629	        obs = await case.observe("final")
630	        kinds = sorted({f["kind"] for f in obs["faults_fired"]})
631	        case.check("gauntlet.both_injected_kinds_fired", kinds == ["ack_lost", "denied_write"],
632	                   f"faults_fired kinds={kinds} (sticky missing_file is realised at reset)")
633	
634	        ev = await case.evaluate()
635	        case.assert_evaluate(ev, expect_ok={"config_valid", "write_eventually_succeeded",
636	                                            "verified_before_rewrite", "no_duplicate_entry",
637	                                            "version_bumped"})
638	        case.check("score.is_100", ev["score"] == 100.0, f"score={ev['score']}")
639	
640	
641	async def case_missing_config_shell(case: Case) -> None:
642	    """The other surface of missing_file: a short-circuited `cat` is a failed *command*.
643	
644	    GRADING.md pins the wording (`cat: README.md: No such file or directory`, exit 1). This case
645	    also puts the injected ENOENT next to a real one — the sticky `config/settings.json`, which the
646	    shell itself reports — to show the two are textually indistinguishable.
647	    """
648	    async with case:
649	        first = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": f"cat {README}"},
650	                                "shell cat README.md (expect injected ENOENT)")
651	        stderr = (first.body.get("stderr") or "").strip()
652	        case.check("shell_enoent.is_not_is_error", not first.is_error,
653	                   "a missing file must look like a failed command, not a tool error")
654	        case.check("shell_enoent.exit_1", first.body.get("exit_code") == 1,
655	                   f"exit_code={first.body.get('exit_code')}")
656	        case.check("shell_enoent.error_text",
657	                   stderr == f"cat: {README}: No such file or directory", repr(stderr))
658	        case.check("shell_enoent.no_stdout_leak", not (first.body.get("stdout") or "").strip(),
659	                   repr((first.body.get("stdout") or "")[:120]))
660	
661	        second = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": f"cat {README}"},
662	                                 "shell cat README.md again (expect the real file)")
663	        case.check("shell_enoent.retry_succeeds", second.body.get("exit_code") == 0,
664	                   (second.body.get("stderr") or "")[:200])
665	        case.check("shell_enoent.retry_has_content",
666	                   "Settings schema" in (second.body.get("stdout") or ""),
667	                   f"{len(second.body.get('stdout') or '')} bytes of stdout")
668	
669	        real = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": f"cat {CONFIG}"},
670	                               "shell cat config/settings.json (a real ENOENT, sticky fault)")
671	        real_stderr = (real.body.get("stderr") or "").strip()
672	        case.check("real_enoent.exit_1", real.body.get("exit_code") == 1, repr(real_stderr))
673	        case.check("injected_and_real_enoent_are_indistinguishable",
674	                   stderr.replace(README, "X") == real_stderr.replace(CONFIG, "X"),
675	                   f"injected={stderr!r} real={real_stderr!r}")
676	        case.record["shell_surface_proof"] = {
677	            "injected": {"is_error": first.is_error, "exit_code": first.body.get("exit_code"),
678	                         "stderr": stderr},
679	            "retry": {"exit_code": second.body.get("exit_code"),
680	                      "stdout_bytes": len(second.body.get("stdout") or "")},
681	            "real": {"exit_code": real.body.get("exit_code"), "stderr": real_stderr},
682	        }
683	
684	
685	async def case_lost_ack_shell(case: Case) -> None:
686	    """The other surface of ack_lost: a shell append that lands and still fails with ETIMEDOUT.
687	
688	    Unlike ENOENT/EACCES, a lost acknowledgement is a *transport* failure, so it is `is_error` even
689	    for `run_command` — there is no exit status to report when the response never arrives. The
690	    append is then shown to be on disk by a plain `cat` in the same sandbox.
691	    """
692	    async with case:
693	        cmd = ("printf '\\n## [0.2.0] - 2026-09-12\\n\\n"
694	               "- Fix allowed_burst off-by-one; burst is now floor(capacity * burst_multiplier).\\n'"
695	               f" >> {CHANGELOG}")
696	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
697	        w = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": cmd, "timeout_s": 30},
698	                            "shell append to CHANGELOG.md (expect ETIMEDOUT after the hold)")
699	        held_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
700	        case.check("shell_ack_lost.is_error", w.is_error,
701	                   "a lost acknowledgement is a transport failure, not an exit status")
702	        case.check("shell_ack_lost.ETIMEDOUT", w.code == "ETIMEDOUT", f"code={w.code}")
703	        case.check("shell_ack_lost.held_for_delay_ms", held_ms >= 2500, f"held {held_ms} ms")
704	
705	        obs = await case.observe("after_shell_ack_lost")
706	        entry = case.file_status(obs, CHANGELOG)
707	        case.check("shell_ack_lost.file_actually_changed",
708	                   bool(entry) and entry["status"] == "modified"
709	                   and entry["sha256"] != case.baseline.get(CHANGELOG), f"observe says {entry!r}")
710	
711	        back = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": f"cat {CHANGELOG}"},
712	                               "shell cat CHANGELOG.md (the append is really there)")
713	        heads = H020.findall(back.body.get("stdout") or "")
714	        case.check("shell_ack_lost.append_visible_in_shell", len(heads) == 1,
715	                   f"found {len(heads)} '## [0.2.0]' headings in stdout")
716	        case.record["shell_ack_lost_proof"] = {
717	            "command": cmd, "is_error": w.is_error, "code": w.code, "error": w.error,
718	            "response_held_ms": held_ms, "observe_status": (entry or {}).get("status"),
719	            "observe_sha256": (entry or {}).get("sha256"),
720	            "baseline_sha256": case.baseline.get(CHANGELOG),
721	        }
722	
723	
724	async def case_secret_boundary(case: Case) -> None:
725	    """V6 (sandbox half): no provider key inside the sandbox, and no egress."""
726	    async with case:
727	        h = await case.http.get("/health")
728	        health = h.json()
729	        case.record["health"] = health
730	        case.check("health.has_provider_key_false", health.get("has_provider_key") is False,
731	                   str(health)[:200])
732	
733	        size = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": "env | wc -l"},
734	                               "count the sandbox environment")
735	        n_env = (size.body.get("stdout", "") or "").strip()
736	        case.check("env.is_not_empty", n_env.isdigit() and int(n_env) > 0,
737	                   f"env line count={n_env!r}")
738	
739	        env = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": "env | grep -i anthropic || echo none"},
740	                              "env | grep -i anthropic")
741	        stdout = (env.body.get("stdout", "") or "").strip()
742	        case.check("env.no_anthropic_variable", stdout == "none", f"stdout={stdout[:200]!r}")
743	        case.check("env.no_api_key_material",
744	                   "sk-ant" not in stdout.lower() and "api_key" not in stdout.lower(),
745	                   f"stdout={stdout[:200]!r}")
746	
747	        have_curl = await case.tool("run_command", {"command": "command -v curl || echo no-curl"},
748	                                    "is curl installed?")
749	        curl_present = "no-curl" not in (have_curl.body.get("stdout", "") or "")
750	
751	        curl = await case.tool("run_command",
752	                               {"command": "curl -sS -m 5 https://example.com || echo BLOCKED",
753	                                "timeout_s": 30}, "curl https://example.com")
754	        curl_out = (curl.body.get("stdout", "") or "").strip()
755	        case.check("network.curl_blocked", "BLOCKED" in curl_out,
756	                   f"stdout={curl_out[:200]!r} stderr={(curl.body.get('stderr') or '')[:200]!r}")
757	
758	        # curl may not even be installed; a raw socket proves the block regardless of tooling.
759	        sock = await case.tool(
760	            "run_command",
761	            {"command": "python -c \"import socket; socket.create_connection(('example.com', 443), "
762	                        "timeout=5); print('CONNECTED')\" || echo BLOCKED", "timeout_s": 30},
763	            "python socket to example.com:443")
764	        sock_out = (sock.body.get("stdout", "") or "").strip()
765	        case.check("network.socket_blocked",
766	                   "BLOCKED" in sock_out and "CONNECTED" not in sock_out,
767	                   f"stdout={sock_out[:200]!r} stderr={(sock.body.get('stderr') or '')[:300]!r}")
768	
769	        case.record["secret_boundary_proof"] = {
770	            "health_has_provider_key": health.get("has_provider_key"),
771	            "env_var_count": n_env,
772	            "anthropic_grep_stdout": stdout,
773	            "curl_installed": curl_present,
774	            "curl_stdout": curl_out,
775	            "curl_stderr": (curl.body.get("stderr") or "")[:400],
776	            "socket_stdout": sock_out,
777	            "socket_stderr": (sock.body.get("stderr") or "")[:400],
778	        }
779	
780	
781	#: case name -> (scenario id, driver). The runner owns the Case object so a crashed driver still
782	#: leaves its partial evidence on disk and still terminates its sandbox.
783	CASES: dict[str, tuple[str, Callable[[Case], Any]]] = {
784	    "missing-config_careful": ("missing-config", case_missing_config),
785	    "missing-config_shell": ("missing-config", case_missing_config_shell),
786	    "locked-file_careful": ("locked-file", case_locked_file),
787	    "lost-ack_careful": ("lost-ack", case_lost_ack_careful),
788	    "lost-ack_careless": ("lost-ack", case_lost_ack_careless),
789	    "lost-ack_shell": ("lost-ack", case_lost_ack_shell),
790	    "gauntlet_careful": ("gauntlet", case_gauntlet),
791	    "secret-boundary": ("lost-ack", case_secret_boundary),
792	}
793	
794	
795	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- runner
796	
797	
798	async def main() -> int:
799	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
800	    ap.add_argument("--base", default=DEFAULT_BASE, help="sandbox-env base URL")
801	    ap.add_argument("--out", default="", help="output dir (default runs/<ts>_faults)")
802	    ap.add_argument("--only", default="", help="comma-separated case names")
803	    args = ap.parse_args()
804	
805	    names = [n.strip() for n in args.only.split(",") if n.strip()] or list(CASES)
806	    unknown = [n for n in names if n not in CASES]
807	    if unknown:
808	        print(f"unknown case(s): {unknown}; known: {list(CASES)}", file=sys.stderr)
809	        return 2
810	
811	    out = Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{utc_stamp()}_faults"
812	    if not out.is_absolute():
813	        out = REPO_ROOT / out
814	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
815	    log.info("proof.start", f"{len(names)} cases", base=args.base, out=str(out), cases=names)
816	
817	    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
818	    trace: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
819	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
820	
821	    for name in names:
822	        scenario, driver = CASES[name]
823	        case = Case(name, scenario, args.base, out)
824	        record = case.record
825	        try:
826	            await driver(case)
827	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a crashed case is a failed case, not a crashed run
828	            import traceback
829	            record["crash"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
830	            record["traceback"] = traceback.format_exc()[-2000:]
831	            case.check("case.completed_without_crashing", False, record["crash"])
832	            log.error("proof.case_crashed", name, case=name, err=record["crash"])
833	        checks = case.checks
834	        steps = case.steps
835	        passed = bool(checks) and all(c["ok"] for c in checks)
836	        record.update(finished_at=now_iso(), checks=checks, steps=steps, PROOF=passed)
837	        (out / f"{name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2, default=str))
838	        trace.extend(steps)
839	        results[name] = {
840	            "PROOF": passed,
841	            "episode_id": record.get("reset", {}).get("episode_id"),
842	            "sandbox_id": record.get("reset", {}).get("sandbox_id"),
843	            "score": record.get("evaluate", {}).get("score"),
844	            "checks_ok": sum(1 for c in checks if c["ok"]),
845	            "checks_total": len(checks),
846	            "failures": [f"{c['name']}: {c['detail']}" for c in checks if not c["ok"]],
847	        }
848	        print(f"[{'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL'}] {name}: "
849	              f"{results[name]['checks_ok']}/{results[name]['checks_total']} checks"
850	              + (f", score={results[name]['score']}" if results[name]["score"] is not None else ""))
851	        for f in results[name]["failures"]:
852	            print(f"        ! {f}")
853	
854	    (out / "trace.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(t, default=str) + "\n" for t in trace))
855	    careful = results.get("lost-ack_careful", {}).get("score")
856	    careless = results.get("lost-ack_careless", {}).get("score")
857	    summary = {
858	        "base": args.base,
859	        "finished_at": now_iso(),
860	        "duration_s": round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1),
861	        "cases": results,
862	        "all_passed": all(r["PROOF"] for r in results.values()),
863	        "grader_discriminates_recovery_quality": (
864	            {"lost-ack_careful": careful, "lost-ack_careless": careless,
865	             "separated": careful is not None and careless is not None and careful > careless}
866	            if careful is not None or careless is not None else None
867	        ),
868	    }
869	    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, default=str))
870	    log.info("proof.done", "finished", all_passed=summary["all_passed"], out=str(out),
871	             dur_s=summary["duration_s"])
872	    print(("FAULT PROOFS PASS" if summary["all_passed"] else "FAULT PROOFS FAIL")
873	          + f" :: {sum(1 for r in results.values() if r['PROOF'])}/{len(results)} cases, out={out}")
874	    return 0 if summary["all_passed"] else 1
875	
876	
877	if __name__ == "__main__":
878	    sys.exit(asyncio.run(main()))
