1	# `faultline-harness` — what the DEPLOYED service actually emits
2	
3	Audience: the `apps/web` owner. Everything below was **measured against the live deployment**, not
4	read off the source. Nothing here is aspirational; where the deployed reality differs from
5	`PLAN.md` / `ARCHITECTURE.md` / `schemas.py`, the deployed reality is stated and the divergence is
6	called out.
7	
8	- Base URL: `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run`
9	- Measured: 2026-09-12 22:12–22:33 UTC, Modal env `local`, build deployed at 22:29 UTC.
10	- Evidence: `runs/20260912T221236Z_harness_integration/` — `web_contract.json` (23/23 checks),
11	  `event_shapes.json` (per-type data keys over 3 real runs), `sse_window.json`, `health2.json`,
12	  `runs_list.json`, `app_logs2.txt`.
13	- Reproduce: `services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python services/agent-harness/tools/check_contract.py <finished run_id> <out.json>`
14	- Companion doc: `docs/web-contract-findings.md` (same system, written by the other integration
15	  session — read both; they agree, this one is the emitted-shape reference).
16	
17	---
18	
19	## 1. Answers to the contract questions, verified live
20	
21	| Contract point | Verified result |
22	|---|---|
23	| SSE frame `id:` == `Event.id`, 0-based, contiguous | ✅ 151-frame gauntlet run: frame ids `0..150`, exactly equal to `Event.id`, no gaps |
24	| `event: done` when the run reaches `ok` / `error` / `truncated` | ✅ `{"reason":"finished","status":"ok","last_event_id":150,"run_id":"r_…"}` |
25	| `event: done` with `reason:"window"` on the 110 s self-close | ✅ measured at **110.5 s** on a still-running run: `{"reason":"window","status":"running","last_event_id":1,…}`, then a resume delivered only the new event and closed `reason:"finished"` |
26	| `?last_event_id=<n>` honoured | ✅ (**was missing — added in this pass**, see §5) |
27	| `?after=<n>` honoured | ✅ |
28	| `Last-Event-ID` header honoured | ✅ |
29	| `tool.result.data.output` is a JSON **string** for structured tools | ✅ 29/29 structured results parsed as JSON on the gauntlet run |
30	| `fault.fired` data is the `FaultFired` object at top level | ✅ `{"step","kind","path","mode"}` directly in `Event.data` |
31	| `POST /runs` returns `{run_id}` | ✅ (`{run_id, status, scenario_id, model, max_steps, seed}`) |
32	| `conversation_id` on `POST /runs` | ❌ **not yet** — the SQLite Store of ARCHITECTURE §4 is not implemented; see §6 |
33	| `GET /scenarios` returns `{scenarios:[…]}` | ✅ 4 scenarios: `gauntlet`, `locked-file`, `lost-ack`, `missing-config` |
34	
35	Precedence when more than one resume point is supplied:
36	**`Last-Event-ID` header → `?last_event_id=` → `?after=`**; an unparsable value falls through to the
37	next source, and none at all means "replay everything from id 0".
38	
39	---
40	
41	## 2. Event stream — exact `data` keys per type
42	
43	Measured over three real runs (`lost-ack`, `gauntlet`, a truncated `lost-ack`), 151 + 61 + 34
44	events. `Event` itself is always `{id, ts, run_id, type, step, data}` and **every one of the 246
45	events validated against `faultline_common.schemas.Event`**.
46	
47	| `type` | `Event.step` | `data` keys emitted |
48	|---|---|---|
49	| `run.started` | `null` | `scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace, sandbox_env_url` |
50	| `episode.reset` | `null` | `episode_id, files, task_prompt, scenario, workspace_root` |
51	| `llm.call` | int | `attempt, model, stop_reason, request_id, usage{input_tokens,output_tokens,cache_read_input_tokens}, duration_ms` (+ `error` on a failed attempt) |
52	| `turn.text` | int | `text` |
53	| `turn.thinking` | int | `text` — **never seen on the default demo path**: `claude-haiku-4-5` is sent no `thinking` param, so this only appears on `claude-sonnet-5` / `claude-opus-5` |
54	| `tool.call` | int | `tool, input, tool_use_id, mutating` |
55	| `tool.result` | int | `tool_use_id, tool, output, is_error, duration_ms, error_code, summary` (+ `fault` when one fired) |
56	| `fault.fired` | int | `step, kind, path, mode` (the `FaultFired` object itself) |
57	| `workspace.diff` | int | `files, diffs` |
58	| `episode.evaluated` | `null` | `episode_id, score, passed, checks, tests, ledger` (a full `EvaluateResponse`) |
59	| `run.finished` | `null` | `status, usage{input_tokens,output_tokens}, duration_ms, steps, score` (+ `error`) |
60	| `log` | int or `null` | `svc, lvl, ev, msg` + free extras, each value capped at 500 chars |
61	
62	Notes that matter when rendering:
63	
64	- **`Event.step` is `null` on the five lifecycle events.** Anything keyed by step must tolerate it.
65	- **`usage` on `llm.call` has no `cache_creation_input_tokens`** — only `input_tokens`,
66	  `output_tokens`, `cache_read_input_tokens`. `schemas.Usage` defaults the missing one to 0.
67	- `log` events are a large share of the stream (44 of 246 here). They are UI-optional.
68	- `episode.evaluated.data` carries the **whole ledger** — this is the only place the ledger is
69	  released, and it is the ground truth behind the score card.
70	
71	### 2.1 The one real trap: two different `step` counters
72	
73	```json
74	{"id":24,"step":4,"type":"fault.fired",
75	 "data":{"step":6,"kind":"ack_lost","path":"CHANGELOG.md","mode":"transient"}}
76	```
77	
78	`Event.step` (4) is the **harness loop turn**. `data.step` (6) is **sandbox-env's ledger index**,
79	which counts MCP tool calls. They routinely differ — on the gauntlet run the three faults were
80	`event.step 4 / data.step 9`, `8 / 13`, `19 / 25`.
81	
82	Attach a fault to a timeline step by **`Event.step`**; use `data.step` only as part of a dedupe key.
83	This is the same finding as `docs/web-contract-findings.md` §2.1. The harness deliberately does not
84	renumber `FaultFired.step`: that field is the ledger index the grader scores against, and rewriting
85	it would make the UI disagree with `evaluate`'s ledger.
86	
87	Belt and braces: the same `FaultFired` is also echoed on the causing `tool.result` as `data.fault`,
88	keyed by `tool_use_id`, which is exact. Prefer that path; `fault.fired` is the fallback for the case
89	where `observe()` was briefly unavailable and the harness had to infer the fault (such an object
90	carries `"inferred": true`).
91	
92	---
93	
94	## 3. REST surface as deployed
95	
96	| Route | Response |
97	|---|---|
98	| `GET /health` | `{svc:"harness", ok:true, version:"0.1.0", has_provider_key:false, model_default:"claude-haiku-4-5", sandbox_env_url:"…", detail:{sandbox_env:{svc,ok,version}, sandbox_env_reachable:true}}` |
99	| `GET /scenarios` | `{scenarios:[Scenario]}` — proxied from sandbox-env; `502` with `{error, detail, sandbox_env_url}` if the gym is down |
100	| `POST /runs` | **200** (not 202) `{run_id, status:"queued", scenario_id, model, max_steps, seed}`. `400` on an unknown `scenario_id` (body lists the `known` ids) and on a model outside `MODEL_ALLOWLIST` (body lists `allowed`). Does **not** 502 when the gym is unreachable — the run is accepted and fails loudly at reset with `status:"error"` |
101	| `GET /runs?limit=n` | `{runs:[…]}`, newest first. Each row: `id`, `run_id`, `status`, `scenario_id`, `model`, `created_at`, `finished_at`, `events` (count), `score`. **`id` and `run_id` are both present and identical** (added in this pass, §5) so a row validates as `schemas.RunSummary` while existing `run_id` consumers keep working |
102	| `GET /runs/{id}` | the whole `RunRecord`: `run_id, status, scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, episode_id, created_at, finished_at, events, evaluation, usage, error, task_prompt, steps, summary`. Validates against `schemas.RunRecord`. `404` `{error}` for an unknown id |
103	| `GET /runs/{id}/events` | `text/event-stream`; `404` JSON for an unknown run |
104	| `/me`, `/conversations*` | **404 — not implemented**, see §6 |
105	
106	CORS: `access-control-allow-origin: *` on every route including the SSE one; the `OPTIONS /runs`
107	preflight returns 200 with `access-control-allow-headers: content-type,x-faultline-user`. The SSE
108	request carries no identity (an `EventSource` cannot set headers) and no ownership is enforced on it.
109	
110	Response header `X-Request-Id` is echoed (or minted) on every request and threads into the harness
111	log lines; send your own to tie a UI action to its server logs.
112	
113	---
114	
115	## 4. SSE wire format, byte for byte
116	
117	```
118	retry: 1000
119	
120	id: 0
121	event: run.started
122	data: {"id":0,"ts":"2026-09-12T22:20:29.8Z","run_id":"r_…","type":"run.started","step":null,"data":{…}}
123	
124	: keepalive
125	
126	event: done
127	data: {"reason":"finished","status":"ok","last_event_id":150,"run_id":"r_…"}
128	```
129	
130	- `retry: 1000` is the first thing on every stream.
131	- Every data frame is named with `event: <Event.type>` **and** carries the whole `Event` as its
132	  `data` — so a client may subscribe to named events or to `message`; both work.
133	- `: keepalive` comments every 10 s (10 of them observed inside one 110 s window).
134	- The `done` frame has **no `id:`**, so it never advances `Last-Event-ID`.
135	- **`reason:"window"` is not "finished".** Reconnect with the `last_event_id` from that payload.
136	  Measured window: 110.5 s (Modal hard-kills a web request at 150 s).
137	- Terminal statuses are `ok`, `error`, `truncated`.
138	
139	Resume semantics verified live: after a `window` close at `last_event_id: 1`, an event `id: 2` that
140	arrived *while the client was reconnecting* was delivered on the next segment, and only that one.
141	
142	---
143	
144	## 5. What changed in this integration pass
145	
146	1. **`?last_event_id=<n>` is now honoured** (`harness/api.py`). It was not before: only the
147	   `Last-Event-ID` header and `?after=` worked, and `apps/web` sends the query param because
148	   `EventSource` cannot set headers. Precedence documented in §1. Tests:
149	   `tests/test_api.py::test_sse_resumes_from_last_event_id_query` and
150	   `::test_parse_last_event_id_query_precedence`.
151	2. **`GET /runs` rows now carry `id` alongside `run_id`** (`harness/store.py`), so a row validates as
152	   `schemas.RunSummary` (which keys on `id`, as does `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`) without breaking
153	   the `run_id` spelling in ARCHITECTURE §5.1 and today's consumers. Purely additive. Test:
154	   `tests/test_store.py::test_list_runs_rows_carry_both_id_spellings_and_validate_as_run_summary`.
155	3. `scripts/run_episode_cli.py` now reports each 110 s window close on stderr and records them in
156	   `summary.txt` as `sse_windows`, so the reconnect path leaves evidence.
157	4. `llm.call` and `turn.thinking` emission landed in the same window from the other integration
158	   session; both are live and covered above.
159	
160	---
161	
162	## 6. Not implemented — do not build against it yet
163	
164	The SQLite `Store` of `ARCHITECTURE.md` §4 / `PLAN.md` §2.9 **does not exist on the deployed
165	harness**. `harness/store.py` is still `RunStore` over the `modal.Dict` `faultline-runs`: one whole
166	`RunRecord` per run, re-put on every event. Consequences, all confirmed by curl:
167	
168	- `GET /me`, `GET|POST /conversations`, `GET|PATCH|DELETE /conversations/{id}`,
169	  `POST /conversations/{id}/runs` → **404**.
170	- `RunRecord` has **no `conversation_id` and no `user_id`**; `POST /runs` returns no
171	  `conversation_id`.
172	- `X-Faultline-User` is accepted by CORS but **ignored**; `GET /runs` is not scoped to a user.
173	- Run history survives a `modal deploy` (the Dict is durable) but there is no transcript projection,
174	  no volume snapshot and no `GET /conversations/{id}` rehydration.
175	
176	The degradation path in `apps/web` (404 → `conversations.available = false`, rail hidden,
177	`POST /conversations` falling through to `POST /runs` → `?run=<id>`) is the correct behaviour and is
178	fully functional: live runs reach score 100 through it.
179	
180	Also absent: `modal.Dict` `get`/`put` is read-modify-write, so a single episode's tool calls must not
181	be fanned out in parallel (the harness does not).
182	
183	---
184

"""scripts/run_episode_cli.py — SSE parsing, transcript rendering, resume, and evidence files.

The CLI is exercised against the real FastAPI app through TestClient (which is an httpx.Client), so
this covers the actual SSE wire format the browser also consumes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import pathlib
import sys
import time
from typing import Any

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from harness.api import create_app
from harness.events import now_iso
from harness.store import RunStore, memory_store
from tests.fakes import FakeGym

CLI_PATH = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "scripts" / "run_episode_cli.py"


def load_cli():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("run_episode_cli", CLI_PATH)
    assert spec and spec.loader, f"cannot load {CLI_PATH}"
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["run_episode_cli"] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


cli = load_cli()


def event(id_: int, type_: str, data: dict[str, Any], step: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"id": id_, "ts": now_iso(), "run_id": "r_cli", "type": type_, "step": step, "data": data}

454	def test_events_are_appended_incrementally_and_re_sent_in_full_at_the_end() -> None:
455	    """The browser tails Store.events_after, so events must land DURING the run (PLAN.md §2.9).
456	
457	    The loop batches per step instead of re-putting the whole record per event, and re-sends its
458	    complete in-memory list at run.finished so a Store that missed a batch self-heals.
459	    """
460	    inner = memory_store()
461	    batches: list[int] = []
462	
463	    class SpyBackend:
464	        def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Any:
465	            return getattr(inner, name)
466	
467	        def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
468	            batches.append(len(events))
469	            return inner.append_events(run_id, events)
470	
471	    store = RunStore(SpyBackend())
472	    client, mcp, gym = build_lost_ack_run()
473	    record = run_episode_sync(
474	        "r_test",
475	        {"scenario_id": "lost-ack", "model": "claude-haiku-4-5"},
476	        store=store,
477	        gym=gym,
478	        mcp_factory=lambda ep: mcp,
479	        anthropic_client=client,
480	        sleep=lambda _s: None,
481	    )
482	
483	    assert len(batches) >= 4, "events were not appended step by step"
484	    assert batches[-1] == len(record["events"]), "the final full re-send is missing"
485	    assert max(batches[:-1]) < len(record["events"]), "one batch carried the whole run"
486	    stored = store.get("r_test")
487	    assert stored["status"] == "ok"
488	    assert [e["id"] for e in stored["events"]] == list(range(len(record["events"])))
489

# Harness Store is live — notes for the apps/web owner

Written 2026-09-12 23:2x UTC by the agent-harness owner. Everything below is measured against the
deployed harness (`https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run`), not inferred.
Evidence: `runs/20260912T230110Z_store/` (store checks before/after a redeploy, three live runs, the
exported SQLite file).

## TL;DR

`/me`, `/conversations`, `/conversations/{id}`, `PATCH`/`DELETE /conversations/{id}` and
`POST /conversations/{id}/runs` **answer 200 now** — they used to 404, and your code's "history
unavailable" degradation path is no longer the one that fires. Nothing that already worked changed
shape: `scripts/web_check.py` is 22/22 and `services/agent-harness/tools/check_contract.py` is 23/23
against the new build.

Two 500s you would have seen between ~23:01 and ~23:04 UTC (`GET /conversations` for
`u_00000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000beef`, "no such table: conversations") were mine: the first deploy
of the Store shipped without its migration file. Fixed and redeployed; the Store now refuses to start
rather than serving an empty database.

## What persists

SQLite on the Modal Volume `faultline-db`, written by a single `@app.cls(max_containers=1)` Store
container (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4). Survives a redeploy: proven by running the same
30-assertion check before and after `modal deploy` (`store_check_before.json`,
`store_check_after_redeploy.json`), and by pulling the file off the Volume and opening it with a
fresh `sqlite3` connection (`db/inspect.txt`: `integrity_check ok`, `user_version 1`, 600 events).

## Routes (all shapes verified live)

| Route | Response |
|---|---|
| `GET /me` | `{user_id, conversations: n}` — 400 if `X-Faultline-User` is missing or malformed |
| `GET /conversations` | **a bare array** of `ConversationSummary` (ARCHITECTURE §5.1), newest first, archived excluded. Your `normaliseList` already tolerates both shapes. |
| `POST /conversations` `{scenario_id, title?}` | `Conversation`, 200. Title defaults to the scenario's title. 400 for an unknown scenario. |
| `GET /conversations/{id}` | `{conversation, runs: RunSummary[], messages: Message[]}` — validated as `schemas.ConversationDetail` |
| `PATCH /conversations/{id}` `{title?, archived?}` | `Conversation` |
| `DELETE /conversations/{id}` | `{archived: true}` (soft; the conversation is still readable by id) |
| `POST /conversations/{id}/runs` `{model?, seed?, max_steps?, task_prompt?}` | **202** `{run_id, conversation_id, …}` |
| `POST /runs` | unchanged 200, and now also carries `conversation_id` |
| `GET /runs` | `{runs: [...]}`, rows carry `id` **and** `run_id`, plus `conversation_id` and `events` |
| `GET /runs/{id}`, `GET /runs/{id}/events` | unchanged (RunRecord; SSE with `retry: 1000`, 0-based ids, `event: done` with `reason: finished|window`, all three resume spellings) |

## Identity and scoping (PLAN §2.9.1)

* `X-Faultline-User: u_<uuid4>` is validated against `^u_<uuid4>$`, upserted, and used to scope.
* `/me` and every `/conversations*` route **require** it (400 otherwise).
* Ownership mismatch is **404, never 403** — no existence oracle. Verified for GET/PATCH/DELETE of a
  conversation, `POST /conversations/{id}/runs`, `GET /runs/{id}` and the SSE route.
* `GET /runs` is scoped to the caller when the header is present.
* **Runs created without the header** (the CLI, the smoke scripts) belong to one well-known anonymous
  user and stay readable by anybody — so old `?run=<id>` evidence links keep opening in the UI. Only
  runs owned by a real `u_…` are hidden from other browsers.

## The persisted transcript (what `GET /conversations/{id}.messages` contains)

Projection written in the same transaction as the event insert (ARCHITECTURE §4.5), so it is exactly
the `messages=[…]` array the model saw:

* one `user` message with `step: null` — the task prompt, one `text` block;

320	@app.local_entrypoint()
321	def import_legacy_runs(dict_name: str = "faultline-runs", apply: bool = False,
322	                       chunk: int = 50) -> None:
323	    """One-off: copy runs out of the old Modal Dict into the Store, so pre-Store `?run=<id>` links
324	    (the demo runs the evidence and the web app reference) keep resolving.
325	
326	    Idempotent: `create_run` is INSERT OR IGNORE and `append_events` is keyed on (run_id, seq), so
327	    re-running changes nothing. Default is a dry run; pass --apply to write.
328	    """
329	    import json
330	
331	    import modal as _modal
332	
333	    src = _modal.Dict.from_name(dict_name, environment_name=config.modal_environment(),
334	                                create_if_missing=False)
335	    store = _deployed_store()
336	    index = list(src.get("__index__", []) or [])
337	    moved, skipped = 0, 0
338	    for run_id in index:
339	        record = src.get(run_id) or {}
340	        if not isinstance(record, dict) or not record.get("run_id"):
341	            skipped += 1
342	            continue
343	        events = list(record.get("events") or [])
344	        line = {"run_id": run_id, "scenario": record.get("scenario_id"),
345	                "status": record.get("status"), "events": len(events)}
346	        if not apply:
347	            print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.dry_run", **line}))
348	            moved += 1
349	            continue
350	        store.create_run.remote({k: v for k, v in record.items() if k != "events"})
351	        appended = 0
352	        for i in range(0, len(events), chunk):
353	            appended += int((store.append_events.remote(run_id, events[i:i + chunk]) or {}).get("appended") or 0)
354	        store.finish_run.remote(run_id, {k: record[k] for k in
355	                                         ("status", "finished_at", "error", "steps", "summary",
356	                                          "episode_id", "evaluation", "usage", "task_prompt")
357	                                         if record.get(k) is not None})
358	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.moved", **line, "appended": appended}))
359	        moved += 1
360	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.done", "runs": moved, "skipped": skipped, "applied": apply,
361	                      "store": store.health.remote()}, default=str))
362

365	def prune_empty_conversations(user: str = "", apply: bool = False) -> None:
366	    """Archive conversations that have no runs (maintenance; soft, nothing is hard-deleted)."""
367	    import json
368	
369	    from harness.sqlite_store import ANON_USER_ID
370	
371	    store = _deployed_store()
372	    owner = user or ANON_USER_ID
373	    empty = [c for c in (store.list_conversations.remote(owner, 500) or []) if not c.get("last_run")]
374	    for conv in empty:
375	        if apply:
376	            store.delete_conversation.remote(conv["id"], owner)
377	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "prune.archived" if apply else "prune.dry_run",
378	                          "conversation_id": conv["id"], "title": conv.get("title")}))
379	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "prune.done", "user": owner, "archived": len(empty), "applied": apply,
380	                      "store": store.health.remote()}, default=str))

def test_create_run_is_idempotent_and_does_not_mint_a_second_conversation() -> None:
    """Found by re-running the legacy-Dict import: the run row was INSERT OR IGNORE, but the
    conversation was created first, so every replay left an orphan conversation behind."""
    store = memory_store()
    first = store.create_run(record(user_id=USER))
    store.append_events("r_1", synthetic_run())
    again = store.create_run(record(user_id=USER))
    assert again["conversation_id"] == first["conversation_id"]
    assert len(again["events"]) == 10          # the existing record comes back, not a blank one
    assert len(store.list_conversations(USER)) == 1

So every browser's user_id can be listed and then sent as X-Faultline-User. — strip user_id from header-less responses, or scope them to the anonymous user · medium · medium risk (the CLI, web_check.py:232 and check_contract use unscoped /runs)

3. CLAUDE.md boundary / correctness

  • A second SQLite writer on the Volume.
  • Any modal run modal_app.py … starts a second, temporary copy of the app (docstring :11-12, ARCHITECTURE.md:539 checkpoint_now, the import entrypoint). With min_containers=1 it boots a second Store on faultline-db, and @modal.exit runs checkpoint(force=True) (modal_app.py:118).
  • Evidence (import_legacy_runs.jsonl): lines 2-4 show that copy restoring the snapshot (1196032 bytes) and booting api. Line 26 shows store.checkpoint at 23:23:17.933, again 1196032 bytes, landing after the deployed Store's post-import checkpoint at 23:23:15.798. So a stale copy overwrote the fresh snapshot. The deployed Store only repaired it on its next write.
  • Fix: skip the exit checkpoint when nothing changed, and move the ops entrypoints into a separate script that uses Cls.from_name.
  • medium-high · low risk
  • A run whose grading failed is reported as success. loop.py:523-526 records the evaluate error, but :546 keeps the status ok/truncated. — map it to unevaluated; this is the r_ccda8780cbee case · high · low risk
  • Intent is saved after the side effect, not before. tool.call is emitted (loop.py:438) and then mcp.call runs (:452) without a forced flush. With EVENT_FLUSH_S=0.25 the event usually stays pending until the end-of-step flush (:499), so a worker crash leaves no record of the in-flight tool call. — flush(force=True) after emitting a mutating tool.call · high · low risk
  • Writes with unknown outcomes are retried. gym_client.py:27 retries ReadTimeout/RemoteProtocolError/ReadError on POST /episodes (:120) and /evaluate (:126). That can leak a duplicate sandbox (the first episode_id is never learned, so it is never deleted) or start two grader runs at once. — retry only on connect errors for POSTs · high · low risk
  • Simulated and real failures get mixed up. When observe fails, inferred_fault (loop.py:97-108, :462) labels any ETIMEDOUT as an injected ack_lost. mcp_client.py:213 turns any *Timeout* exception into ETIMEDOUT. So a real outage shows up as "simulated". — remove the inference · high · low risk
  • mcp_client.py:211-221 and loop.py:454-457: failures after the request was sent become EINTERNAL, with no "may or may not have completed" wording. · medium
  • No problems found:
  • Provider key: api refuses to start if it has the key (api.py:116), the secret is attached only to run_episode, and the workspace id is masked.
  • Fault plan: the model only gets the task prompt and file list (loop.py:373-380).

4. Tests

  • 153 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. No fakes for removed interfaces.
  • test_loop.py:129-133 locks in the ETIMEDOUT→ack_lost inference. — update alongside the §3 fix
  • test_events_prompts.py:35 "persisted immediately" runs with flush_interval=0; production uses 0.25. It's misleading. — add a test that a mutating tool.call is saved before mcp.call
  • test_events_prompts.py:83-94 "store index" is a leftover from the Dict design and duplicates test_store.py. — move or rename · low
  • test_store.py:177-182 hard-codes the status list. — use get_args(RunStatus) · low
  • Missing tests: evaluate failure → status; CLI handling of unevaluated (run_episode_cli.py:202-208 would poll until the 900 s timeout).
  • tools/check_contract.py:156-159 will fail on any unevaluated/interrupted run.
